6a1(rgyud): //'phrog dang sbyin;;stong pas stong pa rnam dag bcu gsum ri bong gnas su lnga ste mig dang nyi mar phyogs//cho zhes bcu gcig me la bcu dang rgya mtsho'i gnas su nyi shu gcig gis dman 
6a2(rgyud): nyi shu rtsa gnyis lnga ngu ga dus dang nor la rgyal ba rnams te bdun par nyi shu lnga//de la nyi ma'i rab dbyes gsal ba'i chu tshod//rnams ni sbyin dang 'phrog par 'gyur//'byung ba mnga' dang rig byed me lag 
6a3(rgyud): ri bong shar gi cha dang bzhan du'o//rgya mtsho zla bas cha sdus mnyam pa'i mnyam la gnas rgyu ba sbyin bzhin 'brog//skyin pa kun gi lhag   dus dang nyi mas skyur ba cha thob na yang de gzhan no//de bzhin nyi ma'i rab
6a4(rgyud): dges nyi ma rkang pa chu tshod sbyin bya 'phrog par 'gyur//rig byed gis ni gang zhig ches skyur bsal bar nor te gsum pa'i cha sres bya//rgyu skar dus ni rkang pa gsum bcas nyi ma'i skyin pa longs spyod
6a5(rgyud): dbus nas dag par bya//lhag ma'i bya ba ci ltar dag la ro dus ri bong dman pa'i bya'i rkang pa rnams/longs rtsa rnams skyur nas mnga' dang yon tan ri bong kyis ni longs spyod dang bcas cha thob pa//
6a6(rgyud): nyi mar sbyin dang 'phrod par sgrod 'gro'i dbang gis gzhan dag tu ni dag pa med//nyi mar dus ro klu dang bcu dang drug po phyogs klu drug//me dang nam mkha' bskrod thob pa//nyi mar sbyin dang 'phrod bar sgrod 'gro'i dbang
6a7(rgyud): gis gzhan dag tu ni dag med//nyi mar dus ro klud dang bcu dang drug po phyog sa klu drug//pa mthar gyur nor dang skyin pa byang dang lho ru'o//zla ba drug ni phyogs kyi dbye bas zla ba 'pho ba'i dbang Inyan
6a8(rgyud): phyed rgyu//nyi ma la tshes byin nas 'og tu ros rgyur bza'i longs spyod kyis bsres nas//dman gyur ri bong mtshan mar gyur te zla ba'i 'gyur ro mi dbang kye//mig gis ches byung gang zhig ri bong 
6b1(rgyud): byed pa 'das nas 'dun pa'i cha'i lhag ma'o//dag pa'i lo la klu bsres nam mkha' dang ni brgya mtsho rnams 'gyur klu'i sres pas sbyangs//ri zla ba me sa ni rab bcad nam mkha' ros skyur rtsa la sogs pa 
6b2(rgyud): bcad//gnyis dang sum bcu gza' dang rtsa'i tshogs 'dir ldan lo 'pho zla ba'o//lug swogs la ni gza' dang rtsa rnams rim pas spyod pa bkod nas de bcu gnyis zla ri byung ba rigs byed yon mnga'
6b3(rgyud): dang ri bong ri zla gsum pa la rig byed ma dang 'byung zla mig la dus rig byed mig mig ni drug par ro//zla drug mnga' ste zla ri dus me drug byed ri dang 'jig rten no//drug dang rig byed stong dang stong pa mig mtha' 
6b4(rgyud): rkang pa nya'i dus la nyi ma'o//zla drug 'phel bar 'gyur zhing 'bri bar 'gyur ba mnyin mtshan la nyi ma rgyu bas nyi ma la rgyu bas nyi ma la rgya mtsho srog ste me'i chu srang nyin bzhin dus su 'phel 'grib 
6b5(rgyud): bgod pa'i yan lag gis//lhor ni mtshan mo 'phel bar 'gyur te/gangs ri'i byang du nyin mo'o//zla ba nyi mas sres pa mig gis rnam pa grangs zla ba'i chos cig sres//mkha' dus 
6b6(rgyud): chu gter gis bcad thub lhag gis bgrangs mkha' swogs cha la skar ma ste/lhag ma drug bcus gang skyur spar ga chu tshod de rnams/chu srang gis ni de rnams sgyur thob pas 'khor lo dmar po gdong par 
6b7(rgyud): 'gyur zhing mun can rnga ma rgyu skar phyed sres pa'o//zla ba sum bcus sgyur ba nyin bzhin dang bcas ri mkhan ri 'is thob pa ni//gsum gsum dbus su rnam par bsres kyang brgya mtsho ri yis thob dman mi 
6b8(rgyud): gsal nyin mo'o//ri dus cig sres gsal ba'i nyin tshogs ri dang klu ste/dus rnams kyis ni bcad//lhag ma rgyu skar gis skyur slar yang mkhas ros 'phrog par ri swogs rnam grangs phyed bcas/dgu 
7a1(rgyud): @#//yis dgra ni sbyang bya gal te skyi ni lag 'khor lo dang sres rnam dag bya//'khor lo phyed dor phyed las kyang ni//lhag gyur lhag por dag ni rim min 'gyur//drug bcus gsum pa bcad pa 
7a2(rgyud): 'gyur 'byung ba me dang zla ba bcad//gang thob dam pa'i las la nor sbyin rim pa dang rim pas so//de nyid bco brgyad ri dang gsal bar ro skong 'khor lo phyed de 'og tu'o//longs spyod ni skye bo nye bar dang 
7a3(rgyud): tan ri bong gis ni 'khor lo phyed las thob//shar char bzung ba 'dod 'gyur rkang pa'i dbang gis gzhan gis ma la dor ba nyid//zos pa gong cig byas nas bza' rnams 'gro ba'i dbang gis sbyin pa'i 'phrad 
7a4(rgyud): pa rtsol//de nas nyi mas skyes rnam par sbyang ste bla ma dag dang nyi skyes yang ni myur ba'i sa la'o//zla bu dkar pos nyi ma des par nor 'gyur myur du bya ba'i rim pas song 'dir ni//gza'i rgyu dag pa 
7a5(rgyud): yis ni lhag ma rtsa rnams sgyur//drug bcu chas thob rnams rgyu'i dbang gis gzung dang 'phrog pa de bzhin no//zos pa gong cig byasanas bza' 'gro'i dbang gis ma lus sbyin dang 'phrog par bya//sa skyes rgyu
7a6(rgyud): rgyal ba la sogs gsal ba shed bu'i rkang pa ma lus dkod par bya//de ltar gza' kun la ni rim pa yongs bgrangs rig par bya'o mi dbang kye//zla bu dkar pos nyin phyed rnams dag 'gyur te zla bu dkar po langs
7a7(rgyud): spyod las//ma lus nyin mo'i tshogs ni stong pa nam mkha' zla bgrangs me nyi ri yis dman//ri dang phug pa ri dang klu yis 'phrogs pa yang sgyur lhag ma skar rtsogs dbye bas so//brjod pa'i cha yis rgyu skar skar yang
7a8(rgyud): chu tshod zla dbus longs spyod du 'gyur ro//zla bu nyi dbus ba ta nas rgyal po bor zhing rgyu skar byed ni yang dag rgyal bzang kye//lhag ma'i bya ba ci ltar dgar gyur nor dang sbyin pa dal dang myur ba'i ci las/zla bu'i rkang pa
7b1(rgyud): bya bar brtsa re me stong myur ba'i bya bar rgyal swogs so//nyin mo gong bu byas nas dal ba'i bya ba' rkang ba rnams slar yang mkha' dang/nam mkha' ro mnyam mig gis mnan so me rgya mtshos bcad//lhag ma rgyu skar gis
7b2(rgyud): sgyur skar slar yang chu tshod dman pa'i bya bar bla ma'o//rgyu skar nyi mas dag bya drag rgu me ni dal ba'i bya ba'i rkang pa rnams slar yang myur ba'i rkang rnams gsal bar shed bu'i gnas su phyogs rkang laswogs de rnams so
7b3(rgyud): mkha' zlas nyin zhag sgyur nas chu gter nor gis dman bya ri rgyal mig gis bcad//rgyu skar gis sgyur longs spyod slar yang chu tshod myur ba'i bya bar spal pa'i 'o//rgyu skar dus ni nyi mas dag bya nad 'dus ri bong dal
7b4(rgyud): ba'i bya bar rkang rnams so//myur ba de nyid swogs dbyes gsol bar shed bu'i rkang la ci ltar dgra la sbyin 'phrog go//gsol pa'i nyin tshogs kha mig rgyad dang rig byed kyis dman ro drug ri mkha' ci mkha' cig gis
7b5(rgyud): ni//bcad pa rgyu skar gis sgyu skar 'gyur spen par bco brgyad 'byung bar bya//rkang pa nyi shu rtsa gnyis zhag dus gsal bar ro rkang myur ba'i bya bar ro swogs so//myur dang dal ba'i rgyu ba'i rim pa rkang 'gro dag//'khyog+sa
7b6(rgyud): nyid gis ni rim min pas;shar dang nub kyi phyogs la nyi ma 'gro ba'i dbang gis gza' rnams 'byung ba'o//zla ba bza' bzung sum bcu bdun ni lag pas du ba'i myur dang 'khyog pa'o//gang zhig nyi ma'i longs spyod
7b7(rgyud): rab tu bzhugs par gza' de nges par nub par 'gyur//nyi ma bor nas shar 'gyur bgrod par 'gro ba'i dbye bas de'i lam yin no//g.yon pa'i lam la gnas nyi 'gro ba'i dbang gis gza'-i rnams 'byung ba'o//za ba
7b8(rgyud): de ni byang du 'gro'i//g.yas gnas lho yis gsal bar yang ni phan tshun dgra dag dang ni 'di thob po//rang bzhin yang dag gnas la gal te dgrar gyur de ni nges par 'thob mo'o//rgyu skar phan tshun nges par nub par
7b9(rgyud): brtsod na de ru phan tshun du ni rig pa nyams//g.yon tu gal te zla ba bzhugs gyur nyi ma yang ni byang nis 'byung bar 'gyur//g.yon tu rtse mo 'phar gyur rgya lam bgrod pa'i dbang gis lho dang lho ru'o//tshes chad
8a1(rgyud): @#//bas dang sgra gcan mngon du 'gro ste/mngon du 'gro ste mngon du zos pa nyid yin no//g.yon tu rgyu zhing g.yos min nyi ma 'gro ba'i dbang gis lho ru g.yos kyi char//de ltar sgra gcan gis ni 'tshams su'i ngor
8a2(rgyud): 'dzin zhing 'byung bar rgyab tu yang//tha skar swogs dang nas nag phyed par nyin mtshan mnyam pa dbus su//'og la'i ri mo'o//rgyu skar g.yas min nges par srid pa'i gnas su 'bru'i dbye bas yang dag gnas
8a3(rgyud): gzhu'i rnam par dus 'brur mar me'i dkyil 'khor la gnas g.yas su 'og la'i dbus gnas so//de ni g.yon du gsil ba'i ri ste pad 'dab bza' ltar lu sngon srad la sogs//me nas bsil ba'i
8a4(rgyud): na nam mkha' mkha' dang mda' klu'i ni dpag tshad stong//dbus su 'khor lo phyed kyis ri mos nyi ma yang//'gro mtshan mo cig ni mnyam pa'o//phyi nas g.yas dang g.yas min rgyu zhing nyin mtshan rgyu ska
8a5(rgyud): rim pas so//lam rnams mkha' dang klu zla dor zhing nyin mtshan la ni ji srid dgrod pa'i mtha'//nyi zla'i 'grib dang 'phel ba drug bcas 'phel ba nyi nges par nyin mtshan dag ni drug bcu rtsa//gangs
8a6(rgyud): mi rnams kyi ni 'pho ba mnyam 'gyur lug nyid srang gi nyi ma'o//de nas nyid brgyad dum bur khyu mchog la gnas sdig pa la de bzhin no//bu ga zhes bya med grangs 'khrig pa gzhug nas phyogs kyi phyed chad
8a7(rgyud): la//nya mad dkar ka tar gnas yang ni chu srin la gnas lnga pa bcu gcig par song ga bum par bzhugs pas bcu gnyis ro sar nya dang bu mo la gnas pa//'dir ni g.yon gnyis
8a8(rgyud): chu srin la gnas bum pas nyi mar gnyis pa'o//nya dang dum bu gsum par skrod pa mi rnams lug dang nyi ma bzhi par ro//'byung ba zhes bya ba'i nyi ma khyu mchog gnas dang nyi ma 'a
8a9(rgyud): pa drug bdun pa la//nyi ma kar ka tar gnas gyur pa seng ge la gnas pa'i dum bu brgyad pa la//bu mo bug par srang gnyis 'gyur zhing bcu pa sdig pa drag por dum bur ro//bcu gcig
8b1(rgyud): bcu gnyis la 'gyur bgrod pa mi rnams la yang bcu gnyis brtsibs kyi sa ru'o//de ltar g.yas pa yin zhing nyi mgro dbang gis kar ka tar/bsogs 'bru la 'o//steng du drug dang klu'i grangs ni
8b2(rgyud): gsungs te byang gi sgrod pa'i mtha' ru'o//de nas zang de g.yas 'gyur drag po'i grangs kyis stong phrag rnams so mi dbang kye//stong phrag brgya bcu rtsa gcig mda' rgyas dman pa dbus su I
8b3(rgyud): de la gang zhig 'phel dang 'grib 'gyur nam mkha' mkha' dang mad mnga' byang dang lho ru'o//lan tsha'i rgya mtsho bgal nas nyin phyed lho ru 'gro zhing ci srid me'i mthar//ki las sa'i byad dus I
8b4(rgyud): 'gro ste byang du byang la gnas pa'o//mda' ni tshes rnams kyis 'gyur phyogs kyis bgrod bgrod dpag tshad dag ni stong phrag go//phyid bcas mig klu zlas thob nyin dang nyin bzhin dor zhing IIIIIIIIIIIIIII
8b5(rgyud): stong phrag las//nyi ma 'bum gcig la gnas ma lus dus tshogs zla ba dang ni phyogs byed do//dum bu dum bur zla 'gyur sa stengs bcu gnyis rtsi rim pas so//tshad gang dum bu gang 'gyur nyi ma'i IIIIIIII
8b6(rgyud): gis 'dun pa'i mtshan mor de yin no//'bum 'phrag gsum las lam la ni nyin mo zla 'gyur sa stengs bcu gany+sis rtsi rim bdun du nyi ma'i rgyab tu mtshan ma'o//skye bo kun gi byang nas ri'i rgyal po IIII
8b7(rgyud): dbang kye//nyi ma shar nas 'char zhing ri mnyam bgrod pa nub pa nub pa nyid yin no//dus dang thun 'tshams bzhi dang thun tshod dag ni nyin mtshan rtag tu yang dag rgyu//ri rab phyogs dang gsal III
8b8(rgyud): te nyi ma'i sa la dus 'byor rnams kyi dbye bas phye//rtsa dus rkang pa drug bcas dpag tshad dag ni stong phrag rnams su nyi ma 'khor//drug bcu me'i 'bum phrag nyin mo 'khor te lnga dang ri bo'i stong Ipar
8b9(rgyud): 'og la'i phyed nas mkha' me rtsa 'bab nyin mtshan 'phel ba dang ni 'grib pa'i lnga 'gro la ni g.yas dang g.yas min nyin mtshan re re ni dbus bzung+chu srang gsum//lug gi dus 'byor sang yin mkha'
9a1(rgyud): @#//dgu lag ste mkha' dus mig ni khyu mchog la//bzung gi nam mkha' mkha' dang gnyis mad ro'i chu tshod yin te rigs ldan seng ge'o//bu mo'i drug dang phyed bcas su 'gyur nges par srang rtsogs
9a2(rgyud): thams cad rims min pas//de bzhin nub tu dus 'byor de drug 'char ba yang ni rgyu skar dbye bas nyi ma'o//stong phrag nyi shu gcig dang ro rgya dang bcas 'byung zhing so'i cha rnams kyis//lus la 
9a3(rgyud): dbugs lhag gang zhig nyin bzhin dus su 'byung ba ri dang dus kyi bgrangs//stong phrag sum bcu rtsa drug gis bgrags lo brgya brgya'i tshad du rab tu grangs stong dus mes ni rab bcad chu tshod rab
9a4(rgyud): 'gyur drug bcu cha yis nyin mtshan no//zla ba'i phyogs de nyi ma'i bgrod pa'i sa skyes zla ba dgu dang mtshan mo'o//bla ma'i lo drug yin te spal ba'i grong yin bgrod pa zla ba'i bu la'o//che zhes
9a5(rgyud): lo ni nyi mu'i mun pa can gis dgu de bzhin du bas mnan pa rgyal po kye//de ni thams cad gcig sdus nor grangs pa'i dus lo phyed dang bcas pa zla gsum mo//grangs ni phyed dang bcas pa'i
9a6(rgyud): nyin mo los rgyar 'gyur zhing dus kyi 'khor los dman//srog rnams snying stobs 'bab dang rdul dang mun par lus ngag sems kyi dbye bas so//de nas ji ltar 'gyur mtshan mo phyed dang bcas
9a7(rgyud): pa'i nyin mo grangs ni phyed dang bcas nga'i nyin mo grangs 'di shin du nges par rab grags 'gyur ba dus kyis 'khor lo mi dbang kye//dus 'khor ma bcad bgrod pa skyid pa dgu ni ma lus pa ni
9a8(rgyud): bkod par bya//bkra shis laswogs bza' lnga'i myur ba'i las la rgyu ba'i chu tshod ni//sa skyes rig byed dus me rab tu 'gyur srid pa'i gnas su rgyu ba'i dbye bas so//shar dang gzhan du phyed mnyam
9a9(rgyud): skyin dang nor gnas gnas kyi dbye bas sbyar bar bya//dang po rgya la dbang grangs kyis chu tshod rab 'gyur me mig rnam pa gsum du'o//gnas min gcig bco brgyad chos dang drag me'i rtsibs drag po'o
9b1(rgyud): sum bcu rtsa rgyad rgya bcu gnas pa srid pa'i rkang pa la gnas lnga bcu gsum bcas 'dir//de la nyi ma rnam dag skyin dang nor ni rim dang ma yin pas/rig byed bug pa ba(bdag gi )grangs chu tshod 'gyur zhing myur ba'i bya
9b2(rgyud): bar gza' lag gi//gnas gnyis drug bcu bzhan ni chos dang shed bu mos byas drag por dbang (ba)cha'//rtsa ba las ni rig byed drag po nges bco bcu gnyis pa la ni nyi shu'o//nyi shu brgyad dang sum bcu ma
9b3(rgyud): bzhi shed bu'i gnasu yang dag gnas pas rgyu dbye bas/rgya mtsho dang ni nam mkha' zla grangs chu tshod gong bu 'dir 'gyur sla ma'i 'o//gnas gnyis phyog tshad dgu nor dus drug lag zla'i rtsa las/ma drug
9b4(rgyud): bug pa drag po thub pa rgyu kun rim pa yis ni mi dbang kye//myur dang dam pa 'khyog pa yang ni gza' tshogs nges par nyi ma'i dbye bas 'gyur//drug zla rgya mtsho'i grangs ni chu tshod 'gyur zhing myur pa'i bya ba'i'o
9b5(rgyud): gnas gsum nyi shu lnga ste rgyal ba'i gnyis zhes de bzhin lag gnyisu ni yin//skyon ches rgyad kyis rtsa ba drug dang namakha' yon tan gcig gis dman pa rgya//mtha' ru me re gnas srid pa'i//gnas su sang+yas
9b6(rgyud): rgyu ba rim pas so;nyi mu' drug dang 'byung ba'i grangs 'gyur chu tshod gong bu 'di nyid mi dbang kye//drag dang 'byung ba rig byed chu rter mi gzung stong pa'i phyed kyi rtsa las/phyogs dang rig byed 'byung ba ra ding
9b7(rgyud): nor me rim pa yis ni dgod par bya//de ltar dus 'khor ma lus pa ni nges par gzi brjid rgyu bar 'gyur ba'o//sum bcu rtsa drug phyed bcas zla ba nges par nongs drangs dal ba'i bya ba'i rkang pa'o//mig bug mig
9b8(rgyud): grangs rab 'gyur chu tshod kra shis la ( )sogs gza' rnams kyi//shar dang nub tu de nyid bco brgyad bcu dang rig gnas pa bu'o//phyogs dang dus me gza' lag gi ste rgu bcas me ni bla ma 'a shir bcas nub tu 'o//ba sangs
9b9(rgyud): mda' dang rgya mtsho slar 'gyur spen pa'i mig dang bcas pa mig nyin dus//zla bar sa bu'i chu long spyod chu tshod dgu lnga bcu lnga dang 'jig rten zla ba'i grangs/ba ba sangs kyi 'di ltar gza ga gi yang 'khor ri me ste gong
9b10(rgyud): bu cig tu bya 'di nyid zla ba gsum gis lo nyin skyin pa'i chu tshod long spyod 'di ni gsum gi'o//zla bar bla ma'i long spyod yin zhing bsal ba chu tshod rkang pa dang bcas brag po'i grangs/chu srang suM bcu gsal bar zung gi chu tshod nyi mI tu'i long spyod
10a1(rgyud): @#//gnyen zhes gcig 'gyur sum lnga ba de nyid phyed bcas zla ba mig dang bcas//rtso bo'i longaspyod nyin rnams nyin bzhin spyi pa gza' longs spyod do//zla gcig dman pa'i la ni I pa zhes te
10a2(rgyud): lhag 'gyur gza' rnams gang ma zos//de bas nges par byed bcas bdun ni gsal bar chu tshod longaspyod zla ba'i 'di nyid la//sgra gcan zla ba'i longaspyod nyid bcas ri borkang pa rtsa phyed kyis ni rnam pa 
10a3(rgyud): dman//gsum ni nyi ma'i bgrod rtsogs ri bong 'dod pa'i zhag la zla ba'i rtsa lnga'o//gang yang rtsa phyed ma zos dus dang nyin mtshan dus su cha re zla bas dman//de ni nyi ma'i lhag ma yin tes 'bris
10a4(rgyud): grangs nyi zla'i nyin de bzhin no//sgra gcan zla ba 'i longaspyod mig gis rnam grangs phyogs kyis longaspyod kyis bcad las//thob pa du ba'i rtsa rnams nyin bzhin dus su zla ba spyod I yan
10a5(rgyud): sgra gcan gis phyed bcas chu srang bdun phrogsnges par yon tan gis bgrangs nyi spyod las dag bya//du ba nyi ma dang rnams rgyu bdun dang rgyab du myur dang 'khyog pa'o//rgyu bcas 
10a6(rgyud): chu srang nyes 'gyur nyin dang nyin mo nyi spyod las ni rimpas so//zla gsum bcu drug gis ni chu tshod des rgyu 'jig rten chos kyi sa sgyur//phyed bcas zla dang rtsa gnyis nyin bzhin du 'aman 
10a7(rgyud): myur dang 'khyog pa mdun dang rgyab tu'o//ci srid phyed bcas zla ba de nyid ni shar gyur ma'i 'jig rten dug tu nyams//slar yang galate dpal ldan dus kyi 'khor de bzhugs lo'i longaspyod kyis mi mchis/
10a8(rgyud): de shar che shes zhes de shar yang ni mtha' med dus so//lo'i nyin mo lngas bgrangs slar yang 'das sgyur bgrod pa'i nyin mos rab tu dman//bug sregs 'gyur te gza' rnams dkyil 'khor rang 'gro yon
10b1(rgyud): tan dbang gis so//sa bu'i ri klu drug gi nyin tshogs 'gyur te bu ge ri lu ni//zla bu'i so me rig byed 'gyur zhing lha'i bla ma'i sbal bu'i ri dang rgyal ba mig//drug drug ri mkha' gcig ni nyin
10b2(rgyud): tshogs dkyil 'khor zhing nye'u'i yin//ri dus gcig ni sa'i bu'i nges par nor 'gyur zla bu'i rgyas 'gyur ba//nyin tshogs bcus bgrangs me nyi ris dman stsal bu'i//rgya mtsho brgyad kyis dman
10b3(rgyud): stong pa nam mkha' lag klu zung sngags pa nyi shu bu'i skyon yin no//sa bu'i phyed bcas dgur ste//phyed lhag rgyal po bza' lag ni rgyu skar nyi me ris dman stsal bu'i nyi ma yin//ba ba sangs drug
10b4(rgyud): ste skyin zhes ma lus longs dbus nas dag par bya//phyi nas de rnams dag 'gyur skye skar rgyu ba'i dbang gis mi dbang kye//rang gi yan lag gis smad dag 'gyur 'gro ba'i dbang gis bya ba'i
10b5(rgyud): 'tho ba nyi ma'i dbus so//zla ba'i drug gis zla spyod dgrangs 'gyur dus kyi 'khor lo dang zla ba yis//chos thob skyin yin nyi dang ri bong gis ni rtsa phyed rjes su I I
10b6(rgyud): pa dus kyi slar yang ga lhag nyin bzhin dus su zla ba'i nyin mos dgrang bar bya//drug bcu chas rtsa nyin bzhin dus su myur dang 'khyog pa 'byung ba la//zla ba sngags par myur I
10b7(rgyud): stobs ldan 'gyur te sa bu du ba dag ni 'khyog par 'o//nad pa sangs dam par rang bzhin (y+ton)drung nga gis rgyu ba lhag ma stobs med pa//myur bas shar du mngon phyogs slar yang nub I mig bug mig
10b8(rgyud): 'khyog pa'i rgyu bar rab bzhugs pa'o//dal ba'i g.yas phyogs yin te rang 'gro yon tan dbang gis 'byung bar bya phyogs pas//zla ba'i gang zhig nyin mtshan sum bcu chu gter gi I//ba sangs

11a1(rgyud): @#//sa bu mi 'dod shar des gal te slar yang smin drug sbyor nyid 'gyur ba'o//de la g.yul dang nang du gal te gyur na mi rnams dang po'i dbyangs mchi'o//bzang mo zla bus gnyis pa zhes te
11a2(rgyud): mngag sbyor nyid bza' de la//rgyu skar rgyal por gal te bla mar 'gyur na gsum pa dag ni shi bar 'gyur//stong ches pa sangs gal te mon grus 'gyur na bzhi ba ru ni shi bar 'gyur//gang ba dman pa 
11a3(rgyud): bcas pa phra nyer rab 'gyur lnga pa la ni shi bar 'gyur//nyi mas rga shar rnams la shi ba'i sbyor bas rnam bral 'bras bu mang bar 'gyur//nad dang 'thab mo 'gron 'grong bag len dge dang mi dge ba'i bras
11a4(rgyud): bu shes par bya//de ltar rgyu skar gza' dang dbyangs swogs chos rnams de nyid lnga'i rab dbye bas//phyogs dang zla ba bgrod lo drug bcu'i lo 'di ru ni shar bar 'gyur//spyid swogs yan
11a5(rgyud): lag bcu gnyis kyis 'di shar gyur phyogs kyi dbye ba nyid do//nyin mo bdun bcu rtsa gnyis 'dir shar 'gyur te dbyangs rnams zla ba'i dbye bas so//zla ba drug gis ni 
11a6(rgyud): bgrod pa'i cha ster rab 'gyur skor//tshe lo bcu gnyis kyi lo yin no//de ltar dbyangs lnga 'dir shar gyur na phyi nas yang ni nub par 'gyur//dang po dbyangs rnams byis pa yin zhing rang ches yon 
11a7(rgyud): tan dbangs gis dpal dang gzhon nu gnyis pa la//dar ba rgan mo rim gis me dang chu gter nyin mo lnga pa la ni nub par 'gyur//a swogs dbyangs lnga gang yin nges par yon tan gis grangs nyis
11a8(rgyud): lnga'i rab dbye bas rang rnams nag po'i phyogs las de ni nges par 'gyur te/skyed dang sdud pa'i sbyor ba yis//rgyu skar nyi shu bdun gang nyi ma'i dus 'byor gsal bar ram de'i chu tshod cha dgu'o//
11b1(rgyud): 'di rnams rang bzhin gsum po g.yo btan shin du mnyam pa lus kyis dus 'byor swogs 'dir ro//lug dang kar ka srang des chu srin 'dir rgyu de yis dman pa btan pa rnams yin no//lhag ma gzhan pa'i
11b2(rgyud): rang bzhin gnyis te nyin bzhin dus su rtag par de rnams shar//de la bdud tsam sum bcu'i nyin mo'i dus su rtsa yang drug bcu nyid yin no//dang po dbyangs ni sum bcu 'dir shar gyur na nges par yud tsam
11b3(rgyud): sde yig sum bcu la ni chu tshod rab 'gyur thung dang ring po'i dbye bas so//tshes chad dang ni zla ba'i dbus su 'char bar brjod par brjod de de nas nub par 'gyur ba yin//stong pa rlang dang me chu gzhan yang 
11b4(rgyud): sa ni dbyangs la dang po rigs yin no//sgra gcan me zla bu'i sbal pa bla ma mi srun nyi bu'i 'dir ni lhag pa'i lha//sngon brjod dus 'byor re re nyin mo dbang gis rtsa rnams de ru sgrub par bya//
11b5(rgyud): de bus dbyangs gang 'gyur te bza' 'dir stobs ldan de'i dkyil 'khor dag ni sgrub par bya//g.yon 'dir dbyangs rnams thung ru 'gyur te g.yos pa ru ni gzhan rnams so//g.yon du
11b6(rgyud): stong swogs de nyid bza' swogs sgra gcan gtso bo ma lus pa//g.yos su sa swogs de nyid sa swogs par nyi bu laswogs nyid kyis so//phyi 'am dus kyi rtsa ni gza'i srid pa dang mnyam dbyangs dang bcas pa
11b7(rgyud): sgrub par bya//zhu ba g.yul gyi dus su dus kyi stong par shar bas rab tu 'chi bar 'gyur ba yin//rlung la nyi mas bcom dang rma ru 'gyur te me dang sa bus shar bas so//dum pa chu dang bla mas y+ni
11b8(rgyud): zhing sa dang dman par shar ba g.yul du mi 'gyur ro//shi dang nyon mongs rma dang bde ba mnyam zhing zhu ba po dang nad pa la//sgra gcan stong par shar bas g.yul du gra ni nges par nyams par 
12a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur dang zla bas bcom te zla bu me dag shar bas g.yul du rma dag 'byung ba sangs chu yis nor 'thob dgra yang dbang 'gyur ba dang du bar shar bas so//'di ltar 'grong dang bag
12a2(rgyud): ma len par dga' ba'i 'bras bur 'gyur te g.yon lam gza' rnams kyis//pho nyas dbyangs gang nges par bcus bgrangs dus kyi 'khor lo dang sres pa//slar yang 'byung bas de skyur de nas
12a3(rgyud): nye ba'i ming ldan bdun chas lhag pa rnams//zla ba me dang mda' ri mi mnyam gnas par song bas mi rnams la ni 'grub pa med//mig dang rig byed ro ni shin du mnyam pa'i gnas nas nges par
12a4(rgyud): dgongs pa 'grub//lo rnams zla ba phyogs dang nyin dang mtshan du dus 'byor dbus su rab tu zhugs//dus 'byor gcig ni dbye bas lnga yis gzi' tshogs dang ldan dbyangs cas
12a5(rgyud): de nyid kyis tha dang//de nyid gcig ni dbugs ni drug bcus lhag pa'i phrag yon tan dus kyi rtsa la gnas//rtsa'i cha ni sra chu srang yin bzhin de'i cha shes zhes bya dbugs drug 
12a6(rgyud): mi dbang kye//dbus 'byung 'jug pa'i dus su dge dang mi dge'i 'bras bu rnal 'byor ldan pas shes par byos//ltas shes dus rtsar gnas pa'i nyi ma'i dbang gis//zla ba nyi ma'i sgrub bya sa 
12a7(rgyud): la'o//da ltar byung ba shes par dge mi dge'i 'bras bu 'jig rten 'di ru gang gis brjod//long ba des ni long ba bzung nas glang chen seng ges gang ba'i cha nag tshal 'jug par byed//phra ma'i rnal 'byor
12a8(rgyud): ma shes nam bza' stobs dang bcas dus 'byor ba sbyin par bya//dus med nyi mas skyes nyid byed bu skye ba bdun dus gnas la ni//mchi nad rma dang ma lus nor ni nges par ru ni de la nyams par byed//
12b1(rgyud): de phyir de ni spang bya nges par bdag gis tshig gis dus 'byor gzhan ma rab sbyin bya//sgra gcan zla ba zla bu'i bla ma sbal pas skyes pa bdun dus la gnas na//dgra nyams rgyas pa g.yul du rnam rgyal zhes
12b2(rgyud): ni bud med dang bcas nor 'thob po//yang dag dus 'byor kun gis gal te yang ni rang gi lus la g.yon gi rtsar gyur na//nges par kun 'grub bla ma lus sa stengs gnas su dag pa'i gza' dang chos kyis
12b3(rgyud): so//rgyu skar cha gsum bya zhing rgu rgu skye ba'i rgyu skar dang po'o//mi mnyam rkang pa la gnas rdug rnams zla ba dang bcas nges par nad pa rnams ni mchi//tha skar tshogs kyis rtsa gsum la ni sbrul bzhin
12b4(rgyud): kyang rgyan ci sri nam gru'i bar du'o//sdig pa'i zla ba skye ba'i skar gyur ma rnams rtsa gcig de ldan ni nyams par 'gyur ro//drug tshes rgyad ri zla ba bcu dang dgu bcas bcu nyi lo bzang
12b5(rgyud): nyi shu gcig//nyi zla dag dang gza' lag bla ma klu dang dkar po de rnams rim par zos//de nas nad kyi gnas skabs slar yang de la sde brgyad yi ge rnams kyis ni//drag po rnams
12b6(rgyud): kyis ni min byed sde mchog tu byed glen byed gza' gang mdzes gzugs pa//dgun zla 'bring po ma rig chu srin bum pa'i nyi ma dag dang//dgun zla tha chungs la 'du byed spyid zla ra ba'i nya ba nyi
12b7(rgyud): mas 'gyur zhing rnam shes mi dbang kye//skyid zla 'bring dang tha chungs lug dang khyu mchog la gnas ming gzugs mig swogs dang//dbyar zlar 'bring 'khrig pa kar ka ta'i tshes la reg pa tshor ba'o
12b8(rgyud): dbyar chung ston zla ra ba seng ge bu mo ches la srid pa nye bar len//ston zla 'bring po tha chungs srang dang sdig pa'i tshes la sred pa skye ba dang//dgu zla ra ba'i tshes la de bzhin gzhug nas rim pas
13a1(rgyud): 'gro gnyis dbang gis so//dgun 'bring dgun chung mtshams su nyi ma chu srin la gnas gza' der ma rig pa'o//de nas shi ba gnyis par 'gyur zhing 'di ltar skye ba gsum par
13a2(rgyud): mi dbang kye//'di ltar srid sogs thams cad rim pas yongs bgrangs ji srid yan lag bcu gnyis so//de bas phyi'i cho ga 'gro gnyis dbang gis rgya lam yan lag drug pa'o//
13a3(rgyud): phyogs dang tshes zhes bya gza' la nyi ma rgyu ba'i dbang gis rgya lam yan la ga bcu drug la//'du byed rgu 'bring 'tshams su nyi ma bum pa la gnas ni 'du byed nyid//de'i nyin mo gnyis
13a4(rgyud): bar rnam shes 'gyur zhing ming dang gzugs ni gsum pa la//'di ltar zla ba gnyis kyis yan lag 'gyur te nyi ma'i dbang skye dang sdud pa'i sbyor bas so//'di ru ma rig
13a5(rgyud): laswogs yan lag mi mnyam 'gyur te//zla ba thams cad dge ba'o//'du byed laswogs mnyam pa gang zhig 'gron bgod bag len rtag par mi bgyi'o//'di
13a6(rgyud): ltar phyogs kyi dbye ba ri bong can 'gro ba'i dbang gis yan lag bcu gang rnams so//kar dang nag po'i phyogs la dang po'i tshes kyi dbang dang skye dang sdud pa'i sbyor pas so//
13a7(rgyud): nyi ma bkra shis du ba'i nad pa g.yas dang rgyab tu gnas na dgra ni rab tu 'joms par byed//g.yon dang mdun du gza' ba'i gzog ma lus zhi ba'i tshul gis de bzhin 'thab pa'i sad//
13a8(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma dang ko'u rlung byas drag pos g.yas dang rgyab tu mdzes pa ster//gnas stobs gnyis pa'i gzugs ni mnyam dang mi mnyam la gnas stobs gnyis bsod par byed//tshangs mo drag
13b1(rgyud): mo gzhon nu ma dang mkha' 'gro'i bdag 'gro phag mo rdo+e lag tsa mun ta dang dpal gyur nor tshes dang po'i mtshams dang dgu pa la//nor sbyin mtshams lha min gshin rje chu dang rlung dbang tshes phyed la
13b2(rgyud): dpal mo//de rnams shar byed slar yang tshes rnams la ni brgyad gnyis dbye bas rnam thar dang//ke'u kang kar po'i phyogs la shar dang gshin rje rgya mtsho nor sbyin la 'char 'gyur//
13b3(rgyud): slar yang nag phyogs me dang lha min rlung dang dbang mtshams rim pa dag gis so//drag po shar nas nub 'gro'i nyin mtshan dbang gis byang du byad kyi nyi ma la chu nas me mtshams nges
13b4(rgyud): par de 'gro nyi ma 'dir gnas pa ni lho ru'o//shar nas thun mtshams phyed nas rlung du rab zhugs de bzhin du ni lho ru'o//dbang ldan chu me nor sbyin lha min gnas su
13b5(rgyud): 'gyur te rlung gi nyi mar nub bar 'gyur ba dbang ldan rtsa ba'i sgra gcan dor nas phan tshun 'thab par rlung ni rnam rgyal ra ba tu 'gyur//g.yul du rtsa ba'i sgra gcan sgra stobs
13b6(rgyud): zos te de dang mnyam pa gzhan ma med//dbyangs dman 'thab mo ba ni mnyam dang mi mnyam yul du lhag pa yi ni bcom pa'o//dbugs kyis gnyis kyi ming gang snying kar rab zhugs de ni rtsod pa
13b7(rgyud): rgyal bar 'gyur//pho nyas dang pos mi brjod de ni rnam par rgyal ste phyi'i ming ni 'joms par byed//a ya yi ge dbyangs swogs rnams ni mnyam dang mi mnyam gnas kyis 'bras bu med par stan
13b8(rgyud): dus ni 'thab mo sa ru rgyal ba rnams ni g.yas dang g.yon du gal te mun can gyur na/g.yul du seng ge drag po dang bcas lha mgon po rnams kyis ni bcom//rgyu skar sa la gsum du byas
14a1(rgyud): @#//nas thub bzung rnams bcom dbang po nor sbyin rgya mtsho gshin rje la//'khor lo de ni 'khor zhing nyin mo 'gro ba'i dbang gis ma lus rtag par rig par bya//rgyu skar gang la dus ni mun can 
14a2(rgyud): rab 'bab gnyis pa'i lta lhung ba'i//g.yul du dgra'i ste ni myur du lhung 'gyur lto ba'i nad du rgyal bar 'gyur//sgra gcan dus la gnas rnams stobs rnams shi bcas ltung ba mnyam par 'gyur lha cha 
14a3(rgyud): las 'byung ltos 'gro 'gro ba'i dbang gis de ru sde rnams bcom pa'o//lta ba'i cha spangs rnam pa gnyi ka mngal bzhug rnam par rgyal bar rab tu 'gyur//'di ltar dpal ldan rtsa ba'i sgra gcan 
14a4(rgyud): bla ma'i gsung du gnas pa'i dus kyi 'khor lor shes par bya//de'i phyir dpal ldan rtsa ba'i sgra gachan khams gsum las rgyal 'khor lo drag po lha min las//'khrug dus du sa'i dkyil 
14a5(rgyud): la gnas shis bya gzhan na dgra rnams med//rnal 'byor ma rnams 'jug phong drag po gza' tshogs dang/bcas dbyangs bcas shar de rnams dor nas srid gsum rgyal ba rgyal po rnams 
14a6(rgyud): kyis gcig bu bzung bar bya//gang gis nyi zla zos nas lha rnams 'jigs byed drag po'i sman dang 'dra ba//sa'i shar dang nub tu 'khor zhing nyin mtshan dus su lho dang byang rnams dag go//
14a7(rgyud): g.yul las dgra bcom blo bur dag tu rdzo dbus rgya la ma ni na rab zhugs na//phyi rol 'phrul 'khor byas nas rgyun mi 'chad pa'i rdo dang me 'dag rab tu 'phang ba dang//ral gri laswogs gcod pa'i 
14a8(rgyud): 'khrul 'khor rnams kyis sa stengs gnas su 'dre ba rtsed dbye yis//rdzong de phyi mar byas nas nyin mtshan chu tshod kyis ni srang ba de ni sgrub par bya//bzhi ni brgyad gnyis khru ste mnyam dang mi mnyam rkar bas 
14b1(rgyud): gnas brgyad dag tu dbug par bya//ka ba lags thag gis bcings zung dang bzung ste rgyab tu gcig gis so//'khrul 'khor rtsa ba'i tshad ni bcu dang lag 'gyur ste cha ni de'i phyed//srog shing khru gnyis
14b2(rgyud): dman par 'gyur te ka na ga yas ste chas lhag pa'o//zlum po phyed bcas khru gnyis thub pa shed bu nges par sor mo dbugs gcig/khru gang dor nas ka na yar ni srog gnyis rgyab kyi tshal phur gdab
14b3(rgyud): spyi bo 'og tu thag pa lnga bcus bcings ste tshad phyed kyis zlum po'o//srog shing mtha' ru dkyil 'khor rab zhugs mtho//gang dag gi ni zlum por ro//srog shing sor mos khru gsum sgul//ldog
14b4(rgyud): nges par 'gyur zhing thag pa dang bcas nges par 'gyur//de dbus rdo rab bzhug 'dren byed stobs ldan rnams kyis thag pas 'then pa na//bkrol nas myur du namakha'
14b5(rgyud): 'gro ste stong thog sgo gang blo bur lhung bar ro//ma lus phye mar byas nas sa stengs 'gro ste rdo rje dang ni ltung ba ci zhi na no//de'i sor phye 'khor los steng gnas su tshangs pa'i
14b6(rgyud): ri mo gzhog gnyis la//brgyad nyid 'ba' zhig gnas te lag gnyis mthil gi khu tshur bcings las rnam par grol//grol nas sa reg nges par mnyam dang mi mnyam rkang pa 'khrul 'khor dbus
14b7(rgyud): gnas kyis//rang 'dod bde ba lhung 'gyur 'gro gsum dbang gis ma rdzong ni nyams bya'i don phyed do//gang gis rgyab na glang chen myur du lhung 'gyur na phran tshegs smos ci dgos//rdzong gi steng khang
14b8(rgyud): gang zhig gnas pa'i dgra stobs ma lus phyi rol gnas lhung byed//de dang lhan cig mtshungs pa gang ste sa stengs gnas su bzhug 'gengs rnams la rdzong 'thab pa//rdzong der dgra gang gshin
15a1(rgyud): @#//rje kha ru bzhugs gyur gang du 'khrul 'khor gis snun pa'o//ga pa drug drug gis ni las 'gyur gnyis 'byor mnyam pa khru bzung mtha' ru'o//snying po re re nyi sbyor gnyis dang 
15a2(rgyud): mnyam lhag ma'i dman pa nyid ma yin//le tshe thams cad so so re re shing la ko ba sbra tshil gos kyi bkri//dus rgyad skyon gi snying po 'am ni nyin phyed gsum dag gis ni 'khrul 'khor gcig na rab 
15a3(rgyud): tu bya//gnyis gnyis snying po'i mtha' ru lag pas g.yo ba 'am//kun nas rgya yis gdab//rgyab tu skya ba drug ste lag gnyis mnyam pa mngon du 'khrul 'khor mnyan pa rnams//khron pa ka 
15a4(rgyud): bas bcings la rlung yab kyis ni skyod pa 'am yang na rlung nyid kyis/de la rang ste gnas te chu gter la 'khro chu rdzong nyams par bya ba'i don ched do//gnas gcig mnyam rgya mtsho'i 
15a5(rgyud): mtshams su rlung yab g.yob kun du ka bas bcing//rgyab nas mi rnams sa g.yo ba thag pa'i sa ni steng du 'gro//rlung gis nges par nam mkha' song ste rdo rje steng du yi/de nas 
15a6(rgyud): me sgol til mang 'phangs nas dgra'i stobs ni sreg par byed cing rdzing rnams thams cad do//'khor lo'i rtsa bar zo chun rab 'gyur ka na ya'i mdun du 'khor lo gzhan ma'o//sor mo gnyis 
15a7(rgyud): gnyis mtha' ru brtsibs kyis kyang mdzes/kun nas dkyil 'khor rtse mor ro//'khor lo phyed bcas khru gnyis 'gyur te ka na ya'i phyed kyis srog shing gzhan ma de//srog gi rtse mor ral gri 
15a8(rgyud): rnon po 'khor zhing nges par de yis 'khrul 'khor rtsa ba 'khor bas so//thag pas rlung gi 'khrul 'khor nam mkha' 'gro ste rang gi gnas nas rim pas so//rdzong ste dgra'i khyim du nges par saur 
15b1(rgyud): 'gro bar ( )thag pa'i tshe ( )byas nas ( ) sa'i tshad ni nges par de yis brjod 'gyur dgra rnams rtse mor ngang gis 'khrul 'khor byin nas dgra'i gnas khyim gi 'og tu me ni rab sbyin bya//'khor 
15b2(rgyud): lo'i steng char lcags kyi gser bu dang shing rnams kyis khyim chen mdzes par byas//kun nas zlum po gzugs te shing leb dang ni ma he'i ko ba rnams kyis gdab par bya//mi rnams 
15b3(rgyud): kyis skyod mnyam pa'i sa la 'gro ste ci srid 'obs kyi bar du'o//'obs de gad bar byas nas rdzong gi rtsig pa dang ni sgo myur du rab 'jug byed//gnas la gzer bu gnyis kyis nges par
15b4(rgyud): gzhu rgyud mda' mnyam mnga' 'am med 'u rnon po rnams//g.yul gi dus su 'phangs na bu ram dang bcas glang chen phug nas rab tu 'gro//ka gnyis sa'i snying por bstan 
15b5(rgyud): par btsugs te thag par yis ni rnam parbcings//thag pa'i dbus su thag pa mang po bskor nas zlum po dangbcas ral gri changs par zhugs//de dbus gri'i changs
15b6(rgyud): par rab 'gyur nges par g.yo min phur bus gdab//phyag rgya skor ba dang ral gri 'dzin 'jug cha ru rang sa par byed de//steng bug pa'i ri mor 'gyur zhing shing leb 'byung ba'i
15b7(rgyud): g.yo ba la ral gri'i rtse mo'i dbus kyi cha ru lcags kyi byas pa yang ba'i zla ba gcig sbyin bya//zla ba'i steng du gang gis rkang pa'i blo bur ram myur de ni ral gris gcod par byed//
15b8(rgyud): rdzong sgo'i mthar dang mi bdag gnas su 'khrul 'khor 'di nyid kyis bdeg//snying po phyed ni rtsa ba'i gnas gyur nyi ma'i 'khru yis 'khor lo'i phyed kyis dgu cha'o//mtshams kyi ka ba 
16a1(rgyud): @#//lcags thag gis ni mnyam dang mi mnyam shing rta gnas nas phyed bcas tshad kyis so//cha phyed kyis ni 'thab mo'i sa stengs mnyam dang mi mnyam rta glang chen dbang gis
16a2(rgyud): phyed bcas khru ni gnyis su'o//lha rnams gnas ni rta dang blang chen rnams kyi'o//de nas 'khor lo'i phyed sa'i steng du me long do shal rgyal mtshan la sogs rnam par mdzes//phyed 
16a3(rgyud): bcas gcig gnyis cha gsum ri bong lag pa me ni rtsa ba'i gnas kyi lcags thag rnams phyed bcas cha gcig gis zlum po pad ma'i shing rta'i sa ni dga' ba'i dbye bas mnyam pa yin//
16a4(rgyud): phyed bcas rtsa bas rtsibs kyi 'khor lo tshad steng rta dang glang chen dbang gis phyed bcas khru gnyis su'o//lha rnams pad ma'i rigs te lta bu rnams bcas dag pa'i rigs kyis
16a5(rgyud): mi rnams la//brgyad dang chu rter mig gis 'khor los rnam gsum 'di ru shing rta chen phyod dang mnyam mo//nyi ma'i mdzes pa 'khor lo gcig ste mi mnyam rta'i shing rta'i lha
16a6(rgyud): min ni rnams la nyams par byed/'thab mor phyed mi ldog 'gyur mnyam dang mi mnyam dang mi mnyam shing rta che ni rgyal po'o//phyed bcom sngon du yin te mnyam dang mi mnyam g.yul du rgyab tu 
16a7(rgyud): bcom pa mnyam pa yin//drug lnga rgya mtsho me chas ka ba'i mtha' mnyam bug par rab tu 'gyur//rtsa ba'i steng du cha gnyis 'gyur zhing khyim gis mchi med g.yas khang /'di ltar sar ni rgyal
16a8(rgyud): po rnams la pu ra rnam gsum dbang gis 'gron bgrod dus ston la//khru nas khru mthar phyogs kun la ni skor khang dag du 'gyur//'khor phyed rtsa ba'i steng du rnam gsum pu ra'i dbang gis 
16b1(rgyud): phyogs kun la ni 'chang ma'o//brgyad gnyis rgyad ste dbyed phyogs dus dus ni lag pa'i rten 'di sa gsum la/steng dang 'og tu 'khor zhing lag pas bzung ba'i ka na yar ni bud med gdan//
16b2(rgyud): phyi dang nang du gnas yin steng 'og tu gnas pa phyogs brgyad g.yo bar byed//steng 'og tu 'khor lo'i sbubs su 'gyur zhing sgyur kyi rnam pa 'khrul 'khor lo kun nas so//sbubs kyi rtse mor
16b3(rgyud): bum pa'i gzugs ni khong stong 'gyur te sbubs kyi bu ga pa'i tshad yin no//chu ni 'gugs par byed de g.yo ba'i chu ni//hU~M bum pa'i bug pa nas dgrol bya//skyed tshal ldum ra rwa 'gyur zhing ma nyi
16b4(rgyud): pa'i sa ni gang dang gang du khrod pa'o//sngon brjod dang po'i sangs rgyas las ni gsum bla mas zla ba bzang la ni thams cad gang//de dbus cung zad yul 'dir gsal bar 
16b5(rgyud): bdag gis da ltar bstan pa'o//rang gnas srung phyir 'di kun gis shig zhes sdang chags pas ma yin nyi ma kye//chang dang lan tsha'i mtsho dbus thub pa'i sa'i gnas su las kyi
16b6(rgyud): sa ni yang dag pa'o//dpag tshad 'bum phrag gsum la mi'i bdag po nyi ma'i dum bu rim pas 'khor//dum bu gcig ni nor 'dab dang bcas dpag tshad stong phrag nyi shu lnga dag go//de dbus 
16b7(rgyud): mchog gis gangs ris skor ba'i kye la sa ri'o//sa la gangs ri dang bcas kye la sa'i dum bu de'i sum ca kun nas so//phyi la 'dab ma re re nyin phyed yul dang gling dang yul rnams kyis ni rgyan 
16b8(rgyud): pa'o//g.yas phyed la thub mchog gnas yin bya ba'i grong gnas sam 'bal zhes bya//bye ba'i grong gis skor ba'i yul du 'gyur zhing grong 'bul gis ni (kyi la)'khor ro//gcig gnyis gsuM
17a1(rgyud): @#//brgya mtsho mda' re skye bo'i dbye ba lnga dang 'jig rten laswogs dbye ba yin//mi dad 'khor lo can ni tha dad sa gnas 'khor lo te de'i mkhas pas rtsal bya ;'khor lo can dang
17a2(rgyud): gnyis min ;dus bu yul gi mi bdag lag bca de nas gzhan//phyi rol dus kyi dbye bas ci ltar nges par 'gyur te/'gyur te 'gyur te 'jig rten mgon gi de bzhin no//'od zer rdun
17a3(rgyud): dang gza' ni ri steng de bzhin thub pa snying ;;stobs lha'i skye gnas so//nyi shu rtsa gnyis sngan gi mtharu lha min dgra ni phyag na 'khor lo rgya par 'gyur//phyed dang chu bo ma yin phag pa ma lha min klu rigs ni gzhan 
17a4(rgyud): la 'jigs ster rdul ni 'byung po'i skye gnas so//nyi shu rtsa gnyis sngan gi tha ru lha min dgra ni phyag na  'khor lo rgya par 'gyur //phyed dang chu bo ma yan phag pa ma lha min klu  rigs ni gzhan 
17a5(rgyud): rnams las mun pa'i lnga//'di lnga dkar po g+so/can sbrang bdag srub pa gang zhig rgya pa de ni long ba yin//bsal bar mkha'i yul gi ngag sbyin laswogs grong khyer du ni 
17a6(rgyud): bdun pa yang dag 'gyur gang du lha min rigs dang ;stobs ldan snying rje dang bral bla klo'i rnam pa yin//rnga mo rta dang bsad nas khrag dang bcas pa dag ni bcos pa cung zad do//
17a7(rgyud): ba lang sha dang rdul chu mar dang tsha dang mnyam lo ma 'bris dang sres//gcig du mes btsos nags kyi 'bras bu dang bcas gang du mi rnams kyi ni zas//btung ba mkha' 'gro'i sgo ngar 'gyur te lha ma 
17a8(rgyud): yin gi ste gnas mi dbang kye//thig le nus ma min sman nam lding chu bo mes sbyin 'dod 'jo dang //rdzong dang klog pang rig pa bzang po mi 'gyur che mchog lus med lha'i skad//kun chod 'di rnams 
17b1(rgyud): gzugs kyi rgyal ba'i ye shes sku ni sa mngon 'jug go//'das dang ma 'ongs da lngar byung ba'i rig byed rtog gi laswo bstan bcos bcos ma lus ston//dpal ldan ka pa la par ni nyi ma cung zad kyis ni sham
17b2(rgyud): pa zhes grags mi dbang kye//phyogs bzhi ris skor ro yis rgya'i yon tan grags pa dpag tshad tshad//gnas su mi mchog bdag po lha dbang bkod nas khyod ni bgrod par gyis//dpal ldan sha kya'i rig+sa
17b3(rgyud): la bsal bar mi dbang bdun ste dpaldan grags pa brgyad//'di dpal ldan 'jam pa'i rdo rje lha mchog gis bdud rdo rje'i dbang ni skur nas ma lus thub pa'i rigs gcig dag tu byed yang dag ye shes
17b4(rgyud): mngon lha min rig bsal //'jig rten ster dpal ldan grags pa lag na mdun//sems can thar pa'i rgyu ru dus kyi 'khor los gsal por byed par 'gyur//de dbus nyi shu rtsal
17b5(rgyud): rims yongs su grangs pa sngan rnams dus kyi mtha' ru'o//rigs ldan rigs la lha dbang lha'i mchog gis phyag byas rig+sa ldan drag por 'gyur mkhas rnams la zhi ba'i gzug+sa
17b6(rgyud): te de bzhin kla klo'i rigs ni mtha' ru byed//ri'i rta zhon lag na mdung ste nyi ma'i khod la 'khor lo can gis stobs dgra bcom//rigs ldan rigs kyi dbus su lag pas dus grangs
17b7(rgyud): bu dang tsha'o 'das pa dang//yul du kla klo'i chos ni rab tu 'jug par 'gyur//ji srid lha min dbang po rtug pa lha mchog phyag byas drag po rigs ldan ji srid do//de dus nges par sa stengs
17b8(rgyud): gnas pa drag po gnyis la 'thab mor 'gyur ba yin//g.yul du lha min rnams ni ma lus sa gnas yan lag bzhi'i rang gi sdes bcom mo//dus kyi mtha' ru ke las ring lha mdzes gnas 'khor
18a1(rgyud): //los sgyur bar 'gyur bar 'byung//drag po phug po tshangs dbang khyab 'jug dang ni rigs ldan dang der 'grogs so//shing rta glang chen dbang dang gser gi shing rta rgyal po rnams dang
18a2(rgyud): lag na mtshon cha 'thab mo pa//rlung shugs ri rta yon tan gis bgrangs yon tan bye ba kha dog sna tshogs dang//rigs byed zhes bya 'bum phrag myos pa'i glang chen 'byung ba'i shing rta 'buM
18a3(rgyud): phrag go//'khrug med drug med dgu bcu rigs ni gcod pan bcings pa mi dbang po ste 'di nyid kyis seng ge drag po dang bcas rigs ldan kla klo nyams par byed//mchog tu nyam nga ba'i
18a4(rgyud): 'thab mo pas ni kla klo tshogs bcom glang chen dbang pos glang chen mo//ri rtas ri'i rta dang mnyam dang mi mnyam g.yul dus bdag rnams kyis sa dag go//rta rtan sras chen po 'gram pa
18a5(rgyud): dang ldan mtshon cha rnon po rnams kyi bcom//drag po'i kla klo'i dbang po mgon ma lus lha min bdag po blo byed drag po rigs ldan te//g.yul du bla blo bcom nas seng ge dang
18a6(rgyud): ni khyab 'jug dang bcas thams cad sde dang rigs ldan pa//ke las mi ring gshegs te lha mdzes gnas ni gang du 'khor lo can ni yang dag gnas//de dus sa la ma lus
18a7(rgyud): skye bo'i rigs ni bcos dang 'dod pa nor gis gang//'brog tu 'bru rnams skyes nas btan pa'i 'bras bu'i drangs pa'i shing de rnams su 'gyur//kla klo'i chos ni 'khor bcas bcad nas
18a8(rgyud): shed bu lo rgya'i phyed la ni//lha mdzes gnas ni mtho ba ke la rgyab tu rigs ni 'grub par 'gyur//lha'i mi'i bla ma'i sras ni tshangs pa lha dbang bzung ni chos su 'gyur//rgyab kyi dus su
18b1(rgyud): gcig la mi bdag tshangs par 'gyur te g.yas pa'i ru lha dbang ngo//srid gsum bla mas kla klo'i ma lus chos ni bcad par byas nas ni//brgyad rgyar lor gnas nas ni go 'phangs bde bar bash+ye
18b2(rgyud): te tshangs pa gzhag nas so//slar yang de dbus sras po kha dog dbye ba rnams kyis mi'i bdag por 'gyur//de rnams dbus su lha dbang mi bdag thub pa rnams dang gzhan rnams 'phel bar 'gyur
18b3(rgyud): kla klo'i nyams bya'i ched du dbang po bcu gnyis mngon du lhan cig lha rnams bdag po gshegs//dum bu dum bur kla klo'i chos bcom 'khor lo can ni bde ba'i go 'phang gshegs//lha mi bla 
18b4(rgyud): ma'i rgyab dang mngon du sras po dag ni tshangs pa dag dang lha'i dbang po'o//rgyab du tshangs pa laswogs sna tshogs steng du ni du ma'i dbye bas 'gyur//tshangs pa laswogs shed
18b5(rgyud): bu'i lo ni brgya ni bco brgyad dang ldan mi rnams tshe ni 'di nyid do//de phyed 'ong srung gis te mchog gis steng gi ni mi'i seng ge'i de phyed do//de bzhin drug bcus dman par thun tshod 
18b6(rgyud): re rer mi thub laswogs seng ge'o//ci srid skal pa'i mtha' ru mi'i tshe ni lo gcig drangs brgya ru rab tu 'gyur//'di ltar dum bu thams cad dus kyi tshod du kla klo'i chos ni 'jug pa'o
18b7(rgyud): rgya dang lo rgya gcig gnas de nas kla klo'i chos ni nyams pa yin de nas stong phrag nyi shu lag pa rgya yis dman ni pa ni sangs rgyas chos 'jug go//rdzogs dang gsum ldan gnyis ldan ni 
18b8(rgyud): brtsod pa'i dus te nus ma'i tshad kyis ru 'gyur//'jig rten khams 'byung gza'i rgyu ba dang mnyam 'khor lo can ni 'byung ba dang//kla klo rnams kyi chos mchom mchog tu bde ba'i go 'phang
19a1(rgyud): @#//rigs ldan la ni lam sbyin pa//'di nyid thams cad ji bzhin de zla bzang khyod la dus gsum du ni bdag gis stan slar yang skye bo ma lus phan pa'i don dang thar pa'i rgyu ru khyod kyis bdag
19a2(rgyud): la ci zhig zhu//dpal dang po'i sangs rgyas las drangs pa dpal dus kyi 'khor lo zhes bya ba la//'jig rten gi khams kyi rnam par bkod pa ste////le'u dang po'o////
19a3(rgyud): //rgyal ba bskyed mdzad khyod kyis sna tshogs tshad ni gang bstan bdag gis ma mtshal ba//slar yang nyan par 'tshal lo//srid gsum ma lus lus kyi dbus su ci ltar lags//zla bzangs tshig
19a4(rgyud): 'di gsan nas bde gshegs lags zhes gsungas nas rab gnas mdzad//sems can thar pa'i don du mchog gi thugs rje lus la sna tshogs chad rnams dang //sa dang chu me rlung
19a5(rgyud): dang rdo rje lha gzhu rlung rnams dus kyi 'khor lo dang /nyi bza' sgra gcan lhun po gdengs ka mdzad shed bu lhag gnas 'khyog pa skar ma dang bcas pa 'pho ba zla phyogs nyin
19a6(rgyud): mo ches rnams ma lus kyi dbus su nirnal 'byor pa yis stod pa'i dbye bas rtso bo'i rnam gsum gnas ni rang rang yi ges shes bya lus 'dir khams tshogs ma lus ro drug zos
19a7(rgyud): shing bdung ba las 'gyur zhing 'byung ba las ni ro drug nges par gsal ba nam mkha'i khams las 'byung ba'i tshogs//stong la ye shes rnam par sres na 'gyur zhing ro mnyam dang ni nam mkha' 
19a8(rgyud): rtag pa'o//'di ltar zhib 'byung bar gnas pa rnam gsum srid gnas rang gi lus la rig par byed//sa bon sa yis 'dzin zhing ro mnyam dang ni namamakha' pad ma la gnas phyi nas chu yis skye ba
19b1(rgyud): byed pa yin//me yis sangs par byed cing ro dang ro za rlung gis 'di yis 'phel bar byed 'phel na nam mkha' go skabs byin te dus kyi dbang gis de ltar 'grub po mi dbang kye//skye ba'i dus 
19b2(rgyud): dang byis pa la sogs dus su lha'i gnas rnams byed do//sa bon las ni sa yis bsal bar ldi bar skye byed chus ni 'di ltar gshegs byed nyid//me yis smin par byed cing mchog gi rnam
19b3(rgyud): bcu rlung gis 'di ni 'phel bar byed//'phel te slar yang nam mkhas go skabs byin te dus kyi ngabang gis 'di ltar skye ba'o//dngos po sna tshogs du ma lus bcas dgra dang yid ni nus
19b4(rgyud): ma rnams kyis spro bar byed//bsang ba'i chu skyes khrag gi dbus su srog dang bcas pa gal te byang chub sems ltung na8nya dang rus sbal phag par 'gyur zhing mi'i seng 
19b5(rgyud): ge mi thub pa dang//nag mo sangs rgyas mi dbang rnam bcur 'gyur te//'di rnams kun ni gnas skabs bcu mngal du gsum yin//btsa ru dus de bzhin gcig ste byis 
19b6(rgyud): pa'i dus yang ni nyis gzhon nu lang mtsho gzhan du 'gyur te rgyas pa gcig ni yang dag de bzhin gcig//rgas pa nyid du ni gcig srid pa'i mnyam nyid mnga' nas skyes pa'i tha ma'o//nya ba'i 
19b7(rgyud): dngos por nya rnams nyi ma cung zad 'gyur te ru sbal la ni ru sbal dngos po 'o//phag par de nyid nyi ma skye mched 'gyur te mi'i seng ge la skye ba'o//me'u thung la byis pa nyid der ma la ni rgyal po 
19b8(rgyud): gnyis kyi so skye 'gyur//slar yang ma la 'gyur te de nyid nag po'i dus la rgyu ni bsal bar byed//sangs rgyas la ni zhing dang rtul zhugs nges par byed de rigs ldan 'chi 'gyur//rkang rus 
20a1(rgyud): @#//rtsa ni khu ba las 'gyur khrag las nges par pags pa khrag dang sha//mngal du bus zla ba gcig ni bdud rtsi ror 'gyur sa bon khrag gis rab tu bskyed//phyi nas sa bon myug la
20a2(rgyud): rang sems rnam bcu shin du phra ba'i rtse//lte ba la yang brgya rnams rgyad gzhan rka lag kha'i steng nas grol tshad ni//zla ba gnyis pa rdzogs pa rka lag kha ni 'di ru cung zad stan par 'gyur
20a3(rgyud): zla ba gsum rdzogs nas ni rka lag 'grin pa dang ni mgo ni mnyam par 'gyur//zla ba bzhi ni rka lag gdong par phra ba'i rtsa rnams mgrin pa'i gnas su'o//sha dang khrag dang rus pa mkhA
20a4(rgyud): ro me 'od dam pa ni lnga pa rnams dang ngo//zla drug la ni sha dang khrag dang tshor ba dag dang sdug bsngal 'gyur//thub pa mchog smin ma skra dang khu ba ni lhag ma'i rtsa rnams te//zla ba
20a5(rgyud): nyin bzhin gong bu skye ba stong pa dang ni mi stong pa mig gi grangs kyis so//tshigs dang rus dang ro lhan dri chu yang ni rgyad pa dag pa'o//bug pa zhes byar skye ba'i dus su mi zad
20a6(rgyud): sdug bsngal skye ba banas dag//gis sngams mngal du dmangal gi sdug bsngal gi skye ba'i dus na byis pa dngos kyi sdug bsngal lo//gzhon nu lang btso la ni bud med nor bral nyon mong+sa
20a7(rgyud): sdug bsngal chen pos gang rga bar mchi ba'i sdug bsngal dag ste slar ni 'gro drug 'jigs te dug 'bod rtsogs//gti mug sgyu ma'i 'di dag sdug bsngal nas ni sdug bsngal len par byed
20a8(rgyud): 'khor bar mi nyid brgya lam na ste slar yang chos ni blo rgya lam na//de phyir sangs rgyas chags pa dang po'i theg par 'jug pa'i dga' ba'i gnas kyis so//de bas dpal ldan rdo rje theg par
20b1(rgyud): ma lus sgom pa'i blo 'jug rgya lam na//de nas sangs rgyas nyid 'dod mchog gis 'phangs la 'jug par rga e ma'o//mngal nas yang dag sku ste skye ba'i dus nas so skyes pa yang chos
20b2(rgyud): kyi sku;so rnams skyes nas rgyal ba longs spyod sku ru nges par 'gyur ro rgyal kye//de nas sprul pa'i sku ru brjod de nges par mi rnams 'chi ba'i mtha'//slar yang 'di ni shes dang rnam
20b3(rgyud): shes dbye ba yis ni mngal dang phyi ru rnam bzhir 'gyur//rnam shes zla ba nyi ma yang ni pad ma la gnas de ni mngal du dag ba'i sku//khams dang phung po rnam bcu 'byung ba rgyal ba'i chos 
20b4(rgyud): skur 'gyur//de nas rna ba sgra swogs skyes pa yon tan dbang gis longs spyod rdzogs pa'i sku ru 'gyur//de sgra bswogs skye ba'i dus la 'dir ni sprul pa'i sku//skyes nas gang zhig 
20b5(rgyud): dbugs 'byung nges par de ni yang dag sku ru 'gyur nyid//de nas so skyes gang zhig tshig ni mi bsal ba yang de bzhin chos kyi sku//de nas gnyis skyes ltung ba'i rgyal ba'i long

20b8(rgyud): rje laswogs mngal nas skye ba'i rim pas stobs dang ldan par 'gyur//sa'i dbus su me ru 'gyur te sa dang rlung ni stong pa'i dbus su//rlung dang sa dag ngu ni rang bzhin med pa'i dngos po chu ru 'gyur;
21a1(rgyud): @#//sa'i rten ni chu ste chu'i 'bar yin 'bar gis yang ni rlung yin no//rlung gi stong yin ji ltar rten dang rten pa'i tshul du rab 'gyur mi dbang kye//lus mchog phung po'i rten ni 'byung ba

22a1(rgyud): //spyod dag pa'o lte ba dang ni 'grin bsang spyi gtsug snying khar de bzhin gtsug tor dbus su ni//ma dang rtso bo rim pas so sku gsung thugs kyi rdo rje rnam par gsum du 'gyur
