22b1(rgyud): tan dang bcas yid blo ngar 'dzin bzang mo'o//lus la de nyid 'di dag srid pa gsum gi gnas su 'di ni rtso bos zos phung po lnga dag srid pa rnam gsum gnas dag nyid laswogs pa'o//
22b2(rgyud): srabs dang gsher ba nges par chu ste dre ba la yang rlung nyid do//thug pa stong pa sra ba'i rus de lta'i rim mchog char 'bab pa 'dir ni bdud rtsi'o//rdo rje sgra dang gzhu'i dri chen yang ste chu
22b3(rgyud): tshod dbugs 'byung 'jug gis bzung//zung 'di dgu rgya nyis ltab kyis ni 'pho ba gcig yin skar rtsis bug pa'i dbye bas so//rgyu skar so'i 'phreng nyid de nyid bzhin chu tshod tshogs kyis
22b4(rgyud): dbye bar 'gyur//rtsa ni chu bo rgyu ma sprin te gzhan ma ri bong cha dang khams rnams ngo bo gnyis//ba spu shig dang khu ba srin bur bcas pa 'byung po'i skye gnas rnam pa bzhi//
22b5(rgyud): nan tan laswogs the bong tshigs te pad ma spyi bo lte ba'i pad ma'i nus ma'o//tshangs sgong g.yul sa mtho' ris 'jig rten mchog lag rkang ba 'jig rten stod dang 'og 
22b6(rgyud): dag go//rgya mtsho gling ri gsher dang 'jam pa sra ba rnams ni khams rnams gsum ngar ngo bo nyid//'dir ni dug mtsho gcin te mi g.yo dkyil 'khor khrag dang pags pa'o//rgyal po kye//
22b7(rgyud): ma lus skra ni grub pa yon tan srid gsum gnas nas du ma'i dbye bas grub pa'o//snying mgrin pad ma'i lte bar rdo rje nyi ma mes ni rim bzhin rab 'pho ba gzhu'i rnam pa bzlum dang nyi ma dang
22b8(rgyud): nyin dus me ni rgya mtsho zur gi thab khung la'o//de rnams steng na mchog gis me ni pad ma ltar 'bar ye shes sku ni mun can mtha'//gang du me dang zla bza' rgyu skar laswogs 'od ni mi 'byung bar 
23a1(rgyud): @#//gza' ni snying gis pad ma'i 'dab ste brjod pa'i chu tshod lte ba'i pad mar drug bcu'o//smin dbus nyin mtshan rkang pa shed pu yang ste longaspyod 'khor lo rgyu skar rgyal po kye//rgyu skar
23a2(rgyud): chu tshod dag ni lte ba'i pad ma rnal 'byor pas rig bya//rtsa dang chu sa srang gsal la ma lus dang rkang pa'i tshigs rnams te//lte dbus chu skyes nyi ma 'dab mar mchog gi 'khar rim pas
23a3(rgyud): rgyu 'phro ba srog la zung dgu rgya nyis 'gyur gyis te rigs ldan dus 'byor rgyal po kye//gang dus dus 'byor dag la nyin mtshan rgyu ba der ni de rig bya//dus 'byor tshad dang nor dang
23a4(rgyud): sbyin ba mnyam pa g.yas g.yon rgyu ba shes par bya//pad ma'i 'bar me re rer nub du sa ni rgyu la byang chub tu ni chu yin no//me ni g.yas ste shar du 'bab pa'i rlung dang pad ma'i dbus su stong pA+o
23a5(rgyud): 'pho ba lnga'i dbye bas dbugs 'byung dman pa drug bcur nyi shur rab tu 'gyur//de ltar 'phyo ba'i dbye ba nyid dang nyin bzhin dus su dus 'byor dbye bas 'gyur//stong 
23a6(rgyud): pa laswogs de nyid lnga ni nges par byed du rgya zhing sa laswogs pa g.yas//sgra gcan ma zla nyi ma bza' lags skyes lha min bla ma sngags pa dang//du ba dman pa mkha' swogs
23a7(rgyud): dkyil 'khor la ni go 'phang bcu po zhing can no//zla drug zla ba dbang phyug 'gyur ba byang gi phyogs te ltar ni nyi ma mi dbang kye//de steng snying ka'i gnas te pad ma'i nor gi 'dab mar rtsa'i
23a8(rgyud): 'khor lor gnas//srog swogs pa'i rlung gi tshogs dang nyi zla me ni g.yas dang g.yon pa dbus su'o//g.yon pa rtsa ni ri bong can dkar 'dab ma yang ni g.yas su nyi ma khrag dag go//
23b1(rgyud): dbus su dus me gzugs kyi bi shur dbugs byung dman pa drug bcu rab tu 'bab//nor 'dab pad mar srog ni 'jug mnyam gnas ched du brjod pa'i rlung dang ni//'jug pa klu dang ru sbal rtsangs
23b2(rgyud): ba rlung ste lhas sbyin dang ni nor las rgyal 'di dag rnam bcu'i rlung ni//rtsi 'khor lo las kyi dbye bas yang dag gnas//lug swogs nas ni dung can mtha'i rtsa'i 'khor lo ma lus rang snying
23b3(rgyud): pad ma la//nyi ma ri bong lam zhugs srog gis srog byed gza' dang btung ba ma lus pa ni 'jug par 'og tu khyer zhing ma lus mnyam khyer zhing//phyed kyi lus la skyed dang kha dang lag pa 
23b4(rgyud): rkang pas klu dang gar byed do//khyab pas rims byed rang bzhin yon tan dbang gis de bzhin lus ni 'jig par byed//klu yis sgregs pa nyid de rjod dang rka lag skum ba ru sbal
23b5(rgyud): rlung gis so//rtsangs bcas rlung gis ma lus 'khrugs dang 'khrug ste//lha min gis ni bla klo shi 'gyur lus kyang nor rgyal rlung cig gis ni 'dor bar mi byed rgyal po kye//
23b6(rgyud): de ltar rlung swogs thams cad rang bzhin yon tan la gnas rlung rnams 'dor mi 'gyur//ser skye'i phra ba lug swogs lam gsum la gnas snar ma gang po che'i lce//rgyal ni drug ste rnam
23b7(rgyud): rgyal 'gro ba yang ni nor skye dgu pa sku pa ku ta ha dang//phyogs kyi grangs kyi dung can ma gang byang chub sa bon bde ba'i mthar ni rab tu 'bab//dpal ldan rtsa ba'i 'khor lo 'di ni tshogs gis
23b8(rgyud): dbye ba du mas rnam grangs mang du 'gyur//dbang po me dang gshin rje bden bral chur bcas rlung dang gnod sbyin drag dbus su//'di rnams gnas su nyin mtshan du ste nyid gzugs kyis sten 
24a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur ba'o de ste 'grin par gnas su gza' tshogs bcas pa rgyu skar 'khor lo gnas//tshangs yul dpal ldan 'phel ba dag dang dbyangs bcas pa las thig le dkar po 
24a2(rgyud): 'dzag//rtsa bar sa dang g.yon du chu ste g.yas su mi ni rab 'bab steng du rlung dag go//gnyis kyi dbus su nam mkha' dang ni sna phug rim pas bi shur rab tu 'bab//'di rnams nus sam
24a3(rgyud): phug ste bcu gnyis mtha' dang cha'i mtha' ru mchog gi go 'phang 'gro//zhing rka lag dag dang lag pa'i pad ma'i 'the bong ma lus mtha'//'khor lo can dang rdor can rang gis lus
24a4(rgyud): la lha mchog bdag po yan lag bcu gnyis 'gog//yang dag ye shes rigs ldan glang chen rta dang shing rta 'khor ni 'phags pa'i tshad//drag po so so'i ming du 'gyur te ha nu 
24a5(rgyud): man ta nyan thos srog chags so//kla klo dbang po sdang ba sdig sde gang zhig mi dge lam ni blo phyed sdug bsngal ster//a shad tha ma rta rtan ma rig pa ste ma lus lha min stobs
24a6(rgyud): ni bdud de rnam pa bzhi//de ru rtsod pa nyams pa srid pa'i 'jigs pa zad de dpal gi rgyal ba thob pa'i lam//ke le'i sar ni chos kyi sbyin pa byin par srid pa 'jigs 'jom+sa
24a7(rgyud): nor rnams rdzogs pa'i sa//gang zhig mi dang mi'i bla ma'i 'dun rgyab sras po tshangs pa lha dbang po//srid gsum rnam rgyal 'jam dpal 'jig rten 'gon ni dbang ldan rdul dang byang chub
24a8(rgyud): sa bon no//sna tshogs sa stengs rgyab tu tshangs pa laswogs rigs ni du ma dag pa'i sangs rgyas swogs//de ltar kla klo'i dbang po nges par thob 'gyur lus can lus kyi dbus su'o//phyir 
24b1(rgyud): ni rgyu tshul mkha'i yul du nges par kla klo 'khrug pa'i 'khrug ma yin//dus kyi lo ni me grangs nyin mtshan tshogs kyis skyur te//bgrod pa'i yan lag las ris drug sum bcu'i cha thob 
24b2(rgyud): ro yis bgrangs pa'i lo ni rnal 'byor ma rnams rigs//gza' yis bcad nas thob pa'i dus kyi 'khor lo'i lha ru nges par brjod pa'o//phyogs kyis bcad pa lha dang lha mo rnams kyis
24b3(rgyud): mngon brjod 'gyur ro mi dbang kye//rig byed bug pa'i dbye bas nyi ma'i yan lag la sgyu 'phrul dra ba rnam gsum 'gyur//slar yang 'byung ba rgya mtsho yin zhing me dang rgya mtshos dpal 
24b4(rgyud): ldan dus kyi dus dbye ba//de nyid zhes bya drug gi dman pa gzhan yang 'gyur te byed kyi dbye bas tha dad pa slar sres gnyis dbye gsum bcu dgu dang phyogs kyi bug
24b5(rgyud): pa de nyid dang bcas pa//nges par nyin rtsibs zla ba'i cha la nyin drug gang zhig gza'i cha'i snyed//de na rdo rje can gi phyag rgya drug ste nyin mtshan tshogs gzhan sku gsung thugs kyi phyag
24b6(rgyud): rgya'o//shes rab sku la dus 'byor gsum brjod nges par rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud//nus me 'khro bos rim bcu drug rab 'gyur lnga ni sangs rgyas lha mo rnams dang mig swogs drug ste gzhan yang lha'i tshog+sa
24b7(rgyud): ni drug gnyis mi dbang kye//rnal 'byor ma klu gtum mo zhes bya rgyad srid pa 'di rnams kun ni yon tan gnyis//'di nyid dpal ldan dus 'khor bzhis lhag rgyad dang de'i phyed ni khams gsum pa la'o//
24b8(rgyud): gang zhig nyi ma'i cha la nyin bzhin chu tshong byung ba zla ba dbus las de rnams ma lus srid gsum gnas na rnal 'byor ma ste dus 'byor 'bres pas gnyis lta ba pa'o//nyin mos the dang nyin ma 'char ni yon tan
25a1(rgyud): @#//gnyis kyang gang zhig rnal 'byor ma tshogs 'dir//ma lus gcig sdoms nyi ma'i nyin mtshan gsum du 'gyur ba mngon par brjod bcas 'gyur//zla bar phyogs kyi rab 'gyur nyin byed la
25a2(rgyud): ni lo'i dbye bas ma lus pas//'di dag dang po'i sangs rgyas la ni mkha' dang lag pa rgyal pos lha dang lha mo rnams//shes rab thabs nyin dang mtshan mor 'gyur te nyin phyed mtshan phyed mnyam pa'i cha//
25a3(rgyud): gang gis nyin mtshan dus dang zla ba'i dbye bas rang gi lus la shes//de ni dpal ldan 'jam rtor srid pa'i 'jigs 'joms skye ba 'di nyid sangs rgyas so//de la sum bcu'i cha yis gsum gis nges par
25a4(rgyud): bgrangs pa'i dus kyi 'khor lo'i lha//phyir ni phyag rgya drug ste gzhan yang de nas 'khor lo'i rtsi ni de bzhin no//gtsug tor rgya mtsho'i snying kar brgyad dang spyi bor rgyal po'i grangs ni 'grin par ro//lte 
25a5(rgyud): bar yon tan brgyad po brgyad kyis bgrangs te rgyal po gnyis su 'gyur bar bsung dbus bgrangs//rtsa drug 'khor lo 'gog cing bcu yis yul 'joms 'dus pa'i ngo bos so
25a6(rgyud): yang nas yang du nyis ldab pa ste slar yang bgrangs pa'i 'khris pa'i ban kan rlung gi cha//'di rnams nges par 'chi ba ster zhing bla ma las nges par dbang gis tshe dang nad med ster
25a7(rgyud): 'khor lo drug la dus rtso rnams ni 'chi ba'i 'jigs 'joms rnal 'byor la ngo mtshar med//brgya mtsho gtsug tor la ste 'phral bar brgya mtshos dus bcom bad kan khams ni bskyed 
25a8(rgyud): byed pas//'grin par song ste snying ka'i chu skyes mig gis dug bcom 'khris pa'i khams ni bskyed byed ma//rgya mtsho drug bcu lte ba la ste gsang bar rgyal po de bzhin rlung gi khams 'khrug 
25b1(rgyud): pa|gsang bar gzhan rnams phyogs drug gsungs pa rnams ni nges par 'dus pa 'gog pa'o//rtsa drug 'khor lo 'gog pa lam rnams gsung ngam gnas nyi zla me yi dbye bas so//srog
25b2(rgyud): chags rnams la gang zhig mchi ster bla ma la nges dbang gis lus la de rnams bsrung//drug la gal te srog zhugs gyur na srog chags rnams kyi 'chi nges 'joms so//phra bar ma zhugs rnal 
25b3(rgyud): 'byor rnams kyis 'chi ba'i yul ni 'bigs pa gang zhig yin//gal te nyin mtshan rtsa gcig las 'bab dus kyi lo ni ru 'tsho bar 'gyur//gal te zla phyed rgyun mi mchad par 'bab na mi rnams
25b4(rgyud): lo ni phyed bcas 'tsho//zla ba gcig tu rgyun mi 'chad 'bab pa zla gsum du mtsho mi dbang kye//de ltar rnal 'byor rang gis lus la zla bar song ba'i 'chi ba dus kyi 'khor lor
25b5(rgyud): shes nas nyi shu lngar ni nyi 'gro gnas pa'i skye ba lnga ru 'phel bar 'gyur//de nas gcig nas lhag ste gsum gis bgrangs pa'i bcu ni gsum gis lhag pa nyid//rdzog+sa
25b6(rgyud): dus nas de na sum mig zla ba drug gsum zung dang zla ba gang//zla ba de rnams nyams pa'i lhag ma dag ni tshes dang phyogs mda' yon tan nyi zla srog gi'o//rang gi gnas nas chog ni cha yis 'byung bI 
25b7(rgyud): 'khor lo spong ste/nyams dang lhag ma mnyam//lo zla nyin mo gsum mig zla ba dang ni drug gsum zung dang zla bgang//rdzogs pa nas mi zad pa nyam zhing tshes phyogs mda' dang yon tan lte ba la
25b8(rgyud): ni zhugs//nyin mtshan sum bcu drug gi mtha' ru nyin phyed nyen la gnas pa'i bza'i rtsa ste rnams dbyangs la ni 'ji nyin phyed rgyu zhing 'grod par 'bru'i 'debs pas so//phyi nas gcig nas lhag pas
26a1(rgyud): @#//rgyu zhing nyi dang nyin bzhin nyid kyi lo'i bar du'o//'chi ltas sbyor bas 'chi bar 'gyur//dad pa mtha' me 'jig rten nyin mtshan tshogs ni nyi mas lo gsum dag nas 'dor//
26a2(rgyud): de nas gnas bcas mda' dang dus me nges par 'dzin te mkha' dang ri zla gsum pa la pro//mig sbrul ru mtha' dang ni nor dang lag pa gzhan te bdun pa dag la gnas//de nas tshes
26a3(rgyud): bswogs thams cad nyin tshogs gang ni de ltar lte ba ci srid nyid//ci srid dus kyi lo nas migacig la nyin mtshan 'gro ba gnas shing yang dag 'pho//zla ba zhes dang nyi ma'i lam la mnyam
26a4(rgyud): dang mi mnyam nyin mtshan gcig gi rim pas 'phel//slar yang 'phro ba'i dbye ba mnyam dang mi mnyam nyin mtshan gis ni bcu gnyis rtsibs su byas//ji srid zla ba phyed dang gnyis
26a5(rgyud): par gzhan ni gsum lnga ba bcu ste 'tsho ba'i nyin mtshan gsum//dbugs ni chu'i srang dang rtsa dang nyin mtshan dus las de ni rim par gnas//lo bdun bgrod pa'i dbyangs
26a6(rgyud): kyis drug bcu'o//sum bcu lo zla gsum las nyin mtshan 'gro ba'i dbang gis lo dang phyogs gsum gis dus kyi 'khor lo ni sum bcu rtsa gnyis phyed bcas bcom zhing nyin
26a7(rgyud): mtshan tshogs bcom zhing nyin mtshan tshogs bcom zhing gnas par 'gyur//rgus ni sgul sgyur lte ba'i 'khor lor phyogs dang phyogs kyi rtsa ni 'chang//tshigs re re'i rtsi ni me'i nyin mtshan
26a8(rgyud): gis dor las kyi 'khor lo dang bya zhes bya ba las//'grin par rgyu skar rtsa ni nyin dang nyin du zla ba'i nang du rim par 'dor//thig le la gnas phyogs nad zla ba'i bar 'dor//lo bcad nas
26b1(rgyud): ni phyogs kyi dbus su dbyangs dang nyin mtshan dbang gis skye ba zad do//nyin dang mtshan gi dus su dbugs 'byung 'jug pa chad pas 'tsho ba'i nyin ma gcig lte 'grin bral ba dag ni rang gi
26b2(rgyud): snying pad mar rtsa'i rnams de'i lhag min//nyin mar re dang me'i gnas rang snying pad mor phyed bcas rtse ste de bzhin no//rka lag tshigs ni tshes kyis dus bgrangs de bzhin nyid ma drug 
26b3(rgyud): gi bar du nyid do//mthar ni rnam pa thams cad 'phra rab rdul 'bab pa nyin mo snying gi pad ma spangs//stong phrag sum bcu rtsa drug lo rgyu'i nyin mtshan tshogs kyi rtsa ni gnas pa'o//gang 
26b4(rgyud): rdzogs pa nyin mtshan dus su nyi zla rang rang tshul gis nus ma yin//nyi ma nyin mo nyi ma rgyu bar skyin par yang 'gyur zla bar de'i phyed rgyu skar nyin mtshan rab dbye nyi zla'i//tshul de ste
26b5(rgyud): stong pa'i zla bar 'chi ba'o//lus srog lhag ma gang ni gzhu'i rab tu brjod pas rkang pa rig byed kyi dman pa drug bcu'o//'pho ba'i dbye bas nyin bzhin dus su rtsa'i 
26b6(rgyud): zung ni rab tu 'joms pa'o//slar zhing lo'i dbus su nyi zla spyod phyir stong pa mig dang ri grangs so//dus kyi lo ni rgyu ste lha dang mi rnams kyi tshad ni rang rang tshad kyis so//gal te mi rnams
26b7(rgyud): dus kyi rtsa la zos par nad dang snun par gyur na ni //de la rten te myur du blo myur du ni zhugs te rtsa ba'i 'khor lor dus 'khor lor rtsa'i ni 'gogs nas nyi ma'i skyod ba'i bcad nas ma lus
26b8(rgyud): pa//dbus su phra mo'i rten pa gang zhig srog chags rnams kyi srog ni 'phrog go//mi dbang kye!snying bad dpal ldan pral bar ri bong rkang 'gyu 'byung ba phyogs dang nyin zhes bya//longs
27a1(rgyud): @#//spyod lta ba'i 'khor lor rgu la bcur bcas drag po rlung dang rgyal zhes bya//de nyid zhes bya mtshan bdun 'dor te zla ba rim pas zlog pas slar yang ngo//thig le rdzogs par 
27a2(rgyud): byas te nag po'i phyogs la snying kar zhugs cing skyi ba'i dus//slar yang nag por de bzhin 'jug ste rkang pa rkang pa nas ni lte ba'i pad ma'i bar//rgyad gnyis bcu drug rtsibs te snying ka spyi rtsug
27a3(rgyud): de bzhin dkar nag pad ma'i ming//'grin par sum bcu gnyis rtsibs gzhan ni gnyis lngab dmar ser lte ba la//brgyad gnyis lte 'grin la ste spyi bor snying ka spyi gtsug de bzhin dkar nag
27a4(rgyud): pad ma'i ming//'grin par sum bcu gnyisartsibs gzhan ni gnyis ldab dmar ser lte ba la//brgyad gnyis lte 'grin pa ste spyi bor snying kar dang//nag po rdzogs pa'o
27a5(rgyud): a swogs sum bcu'i dbyangs gang ha ya ra ba lar ldan thig le dpal ldan tsheg drag gis//phye na drug bcur 'gyur te dus rgyal por nor dang so ste de rnams ge sar rtser 
27a6(rgyud): thig le'i rnam par tsheg gis ni pad 'dab mchog la ka swogs sde yis tha dad do//brgyad dang nyi shu la ni thun 'tshams 'gro ba'i dbang gis nyi ma gnas dang zad//phyogs dang zla bar rang 
27a7(rgyud): rgyus nyin mtshan zhes bya'i rkang pa skyin pa dag dang byed//slar yang zla ba gnyis kyis zhan pa'i nyi lo'i sbyor bas 'phel bar 'gyur ro//rdzogs pa'i lo nyin drug yin te gzhan yang 'chi ba'i 
27a8(rgyud): 'tshams su de bzhin 'phel//de bzhin nyi mar gnyis ldab kyis 'phel 'di ni lo'i sbyor bas rgyal po kye//dbugs drug gis ni srang gcig rab 'gyur chu srang drug bcus kyang ni chu tshod do
27b1(rgyud): drug bcu rtsa yis nyin mtshan yin te nyin mtshan gsum gis bgrangs pa bcu yis zla ba gcig//zla ba drug gis ni bgrod yin bgrod pa gnyis pa nas ni lo gcig dag tu grags te//lo rgya
27b2(rgyud): mi rnams tshe ni skyin par 'gro bar 'gyur ba dus kyi rtsa'i dbang gis so//'jig rten gsum la nyi zla rang gi lus la rdzogs par byed nus pa'i lha lha min mi'i rnal 'byor pa med bzla ba 
27b3(rgyud): rang rgyas mi sbyong dang//nyi ma rgyu skar thams cad srid pa'i gnas su lo'i nang nus rim gis rgyu ba min//lo dang lo skyin gis ni dus kyi 'khor lo dpa'o gcig po de yis srid pa zos//gral te
27b4(rgyud): rtse gcig chad bar 'gyur na gcan skyur nyid du 'gyur ro mi dbang kye//de tsam mtshan mo lnga yis 'dor te mchog gi chas ni gong bu bcad pa'i dus su'o//rna ba sgra dang kha dang yis nges
27b5(rgyud): par ro 'dor mig dbugs su ni mig gi 'bras bu ste//sna yis dri ste mi rnams kyis ni rkang pa lag pa lus kyi drod ni rim gis so//sna rtse 'phrang spyi bor lag pa zhag
27b6(rgyud): pa de'i rang bzhin dag tu mthong lag ba'i 'thil gnyis rgyu skyegs khu bas byugs na//'di ltar dmar por mi byed do//nyi ma kha dog gnag dang rdzogs pa'i ri bong 'dzin
27b7(rgyud): pa kha dog ser por mthong//gcin dang lus ni grang bar gyur na de nyid nyi ma de nyid mchi'o mi dbang kye//lce rtsa skyud nag rab 'gyur mig la tshangs pa'i ri mo shin du phra ba'o//dbus ni nyi ma'i
27b8(rgyud): lam la de bzhin khur chos gnyis dag rab tu g.yo ba dang//mchan khung la zhugs nu ma'i steng du gza' tshogs thams cad 'phro ba dang bcas mthong//'grin pa lus bcas rengs dang lus
28a1(rgyud): @#//ni 'khor ba yang ni 'chi ltas 'chi mtshan no//skye ba tsal ba'i dus su byis pa gzhon nu gzugs te gang zhig byed pa ni  zod pa 'jig rten la ni slar yang de nyid dus kyi 'khor lo can
28a2(rgyud): gis byed ste nyi mas min//nyi zla la sogs lha rnams 'byung bar ldan pa mtshan mo'i cha la bcom ldan 'das de zos//slar yang dpag med dor te nyin mo'i dus su skyed pa'i don du rim gis dor
28a3(rgyud): sgra la sogs pa las drug 'gyur zhing byed pa dbang po rnams ni drug du 'gyur//lo zla phyogs dang nyin mtshan mo tshes dang nyi zla rnams ni byed pa po//de rnams sdud pa'i byed pa po bzhan 'di ru 'gyur te skyed
28a4(rgyud): byed byed po byed ma yin//mkha' khyab nam mkha'i rdo rje can ni yul bral yon tan bral zhing dngos po med pa'o//dri dang gzugs dang ro dang reg pas dang chu ste me dang rlung rnams ni gzugs
28a5(rgyud): can nges par brgyad yin yang dag yul la 'byung ba'i rtso bo gcig po ma lus pa//ma lus pa 'jig rten gsum po 'di ni dus kyis dbang gis 'phel zhing nyams par 'gyur//stong pa
28a6(rgyud): chos kyi dbyings dang yid ni gzugs med yin zhing mi dbang kye//bdag nyid rang gis bcings te rang bzhin la gnas yon tan rnams kyis dar gi srin bzhin no//bdag gis bdag ste gzhan yang rnam par mi
28a7(rgyud): rtog yid kyis grol te/phyir rang bzhin yon tan gnas pa'i rang gi las kyis bde sdug byed do rgyal po kye//byed pa gang gis ci zhig byed de rang bzhin rnam bral mi shes 'jig rten rmongs//sa chu me rlung nam 
28a8(rgyud): mkha' yon tan dag ste blo ru nga ru 'dzin dang sems rags dang phra ba gzhan ste yon tan rnam pa gsum gnas yi ge thig le la swogs 'byos//rags la rags pa'i dbang po phra ba'i sems la phra ba'i
28b1(rgyud): rang dag dang gnas//ye shes sku ste rang bzhin mi 'gyur ba'i srog ni 'gyur ba ma yin//a swogs stong pa lnga po rang bzhin mi 'gyur dpal ldan cha dang thig le sgra//dus dang sems dang blo ste de bzhin
28b2(rgyud): rang bzhin yang ni//dbyangs gzhan rlung la swogs gsum gi yon tan bcu pos rags pa'i yige ste ni 'gyur ba bcu drug gzhan;de rnams nang nas khyab byed ngo bo nyid med rang bzhin med dang ma bcos
28b3(rgyud): rdo rje can//'khor ba bde ba sdug bsngal rang bzhin yon tan la gnas las las skyes pa de spyod do//rags dang phra ba gzhan ste rnam gsum 'di dag mi rnams las su 'gyur bdag gi byed po khyab
28b4(rgyud): par las te gzhan yang phyogs la yod pa'i byed po zhes ni las//bdag ni byed min bzhan pa 'am na//rang bzhin rnam bral las ma ni zhes de phyir gang zhig byed pa pos ni srog chags rnams
28b5(rgyud): la bde sdug ster dang 'phrog par med//gang zhig sngon las rnam gsum 'khor ba ru ni 'bras bu rab ster 'gyur//rmongs (rnams) blo ni bskyed pang sdud byed po//bas;'di ru 'phrog dang 
28b6(rgyud): ster ba'o//dpag med dge ba rang lus la//phrog phug pa rang mig gis mthong//bye ba 'dod pa'i nus dang rang yid grol ye shes nus pa gsum pa'o//dngos por snang ba rab 'jug ro mnyam byed
28b7(rgyud): pa don ni nye bar thob par byed//slar yang 'dor zhing srid gsum phyag byas bzhi bas gnyis su med pa'i rim las so//de rnams dbus su rdo rje can gis bza' dang;;'dir ba bcu zhig
28b8(rgyud): med do//mi dbang kye!bdag gis byed po bde dang sdug bsngal bster bar de la ni mi 'gyur rgyal po kye//sems nus rnam par spangs nas gang zhig bde dang sdug bsngal ster
29a1(rgyud): @#//ba yod ma yin//nam mkha'i bum pa'i nang du rgyu ste chu ma yin zhing de dang de ltar ni//mkha'i khyab pa nam mkha'i rdo rje can ni yul dang bral ba lus dbus de ltar ro//
29a2(rgyud): de ltar las yod smra bar 'gyur ba'i bcom ldan 'das de ston cing byed po min//kun mkhyen dang po sangs rgyas srid gsum phyag byas dus kyi 'khor lo yin gis 'khor lo can ni min
29a3(rgyud): gang gi zhabs kyi chu skyes rtsang ni tshangs par khyab 'jug drag po skyabs su song//rgyal ba mchog gi skyed mdzad rtog med yon tan bral ba'i dus 'khor de la 'dud//zhi ba'i dngos po lha nyid
29a4(rgyud): 'gyur te mun pa la ni mi zad mi dbang kye//rdul ni dud 'gro nyid 'gyur sa mchog steng du 'dres pa las kyang mi nyid do/'byung po rnam gsum rdul dang mun pa des par phan tshun 'dres
29a5(rgyud): ba snying po stobs nyid//'chi ba'i dus su dngos gang yid kyis dran pa de ru skye 'gyur skye bo rnams//lha mchog gnas su lha nyid dbye brgyad ste de ltar gcig pa nyid//dud gro rgya
29a6(rgyud): mtsho rnams grangs nges par dmyal ba'i 'gro//de ltar dmyal ba gcig 'di rnams skye bo ro'i gnas su 'kham zhing las kyi zhabs pas bcings pa'o//las kyi zhags pas ma grol bar du mchog
29a7(rgyud): bde'i gnas su skye ba 'bum phrag du mas 'gro ba yin//srid pa'i 'jigs pa rnam gsum phung po 'byung ba dbang po dang ni las kyi dbang po lnga de tsam las kyi skyon dang yon tan yid bcas blo dang nga 
29a8(rgyud): yir 'dzin laswogs//de rnams gis bcings srog ni ro'i gnas su 'kham ste slar yang phra ba'i dngos po yis//dngos po spangs te mi bzang mchog gi (ga)'phang rab 'gro ba'i gang du slar mi skye//
29b1(rgyud): yan lag dang bcas rig byed dran dang blo gros bcas pa rtog//ge grub mthar ldan pa rig pa ma yin//gzhan yang 'jig rten la byas snyan dgas mkhan//ni sna tshogs rgyas par laswogs so//nang gi
29b2(rgyud): rig pa rig ste mi 'gyur//thub pa rnams kyis 'jig rten 'dir gsungs nyid//gang du ma lus 'jig rten gsum po slar yang de ru thim par 'gyur ro mi dbang kye//rnal 'byor dbang pos rnal 'byor ma
29b3(rgyud): thob pa ni g.yo'i yid kyis gal te shi 'gyur na//dpal ldan mi'i 'jig rten thub mchog rigs su rnal 'byor dang ldan 'gro bar 'gyur sngon dang gom pa de yis dang du len pa ye shes rnal 'byor
29b4(rgyud): rgyas pa'o//ye shes thob nas mi bzad mchog gi go 'phang rab 'gro gang du slar yang skyes pa min//ye shes skye ba rgyal ba rnams kyi nyin dang dus mtshan mo'i mtha' dang phyed dang ri bong mtshan dus nyi ma
29b5(rgyud): nub dus lha ma yin rnams so//yang dag ye shes dag dang rnam 'byed tshig ni legs par sbyangs dang bas par 'gyur//las ni zhi dang drag po skyes bu phal par srid pa'i gsum gnas
29b6(rgyud): su khyad par 'phags so//nyin phyed nas ni mtshan phyed rab tu nyin mtshan sa dus la dus te gnyis longs spyod//zan dang ba glang sha ni zas te btung ba sna tshogs bcu ste sgo nga'i bcud
29b7(rgyud): gos ni dkar dang dmar te nyi ma'i ri bong 'gro gzhan mtho' ris sa 'og gnas//chos dmar sems me dag song bla ma la de dbang gis rdo rje gnas dang lha min gdan//'byung po rnams kyi las 'dres
29b8(rgyud): tshig ni sha za dag dang 'dres par gyur pa yin//chos ni dmar sems med dang bsod ste bla ma la//nges par dbang gis srog gcod laswogs skyon//mchog sbyin laswogs don du 'jug par 'gyur ri
30a1(rgyud): /brgya lam ldog par mi dbang kye//bza' dang btung 'dres te nyi ma ri bong bgrod pas lam ni 'dres phyir ram//'od dang 'od zer nyi ma'i lam ni nyin mtshan dkar po 
30a2(rgyud): byang bgrod lo phyed do//skar rtsis zla ba mtshan mo dkar min bgrod pa 'di ni g.yas pa'i dus ma'i lam//'od ni mtho ris lha rnams dkar po ma yin bgrod pa klu'i 'jig rten lha
30a3(rgyud): mni rnams 'dres mi'i 'jig rten la ni dud 'gro yid btags mi rnams 'gyur ro mi dbang kye//gang zhig gang gis dbus zhugs rtul zhugs la ngas zhing pa las kyi zhags 
30a4(rgyud): pas bcings//de'i nang rang rang dngos pos 'gyur la de rigs 'dzin pa de yis mi dbang kye//ji srid sems kyi rang bzhin srid pa rnam gsum dbang gis tshor ba bde dang
30a5(rgyud): sdug bsngal te//de srid 'khor ba mi bzad 'khyam pa mtho' ris mi dang sa 'og mi dbang kye//ka sogs sde ni dbyangs bcas gzhan yang yon tan gis bgrangs 
30a6(rgyud): gang rnams phyogs gsum dbyangs bsal byed sum bcu nges par rnam gsum bgrod pa'i dbang gis 'chi bas de rnams mnan//rgyu ba bza' lnga'i nyi ri bong rgyu ba sgra gcan du ba'i rkang 
30a7(rgyud): pa'o//lo rnams sum bcu gnyis kyi phyogs kyang yon tan gis bgrangs nyi zla mes phye ba//lo gsum rnams kyi phyogs dang yang ni ri bong yon tan dus kyi me'i phyogs gnyis ni//lo brgya las
30a8(rgyud): ni dman 'gyur mnyam mi mnyim rtogs par lhag ma nyi zla'i phyogs//'jig rten mig dang me'i mig bgrangs nyin mtshan dus su ma lus gtso bos nyung //nyi zla'i dbyangs dang sde'i 'gro ba'i 
30b1(rgyud): dbang gis bsal byed laswogs 'chi bas zos//phyed bcas 'khor lo'i rtse ni ri bong rkang pa spangs pa gza'i rtsa rnams dag gis nyung//shes rab thabs kyi dbye bas nyin dang mtshan mo'i
30b2(rgyud): dus su de rnams 'chi ba'i zos//de ltar dbyangs bcas sde ni dbyangs kyi tshogs lhan bsal byed rnams dang rgyu nyid do//nyung ba 'am ni dbus dang 'dod pa nyi zla 'chi bar gsal byed lhag
30b3(rgyud): pa'i lha//sum bcu rtsa gnyis nyin gcig nyi zla la gnas dang bcas 'di ni rtsa chad pa//slar yang sum bcu mtshan gsum gis rdo rje dang bcas phung po khams dang dbang po spogs//
30b4(rgyud): dor nas nyi ma zla ba'i tshad ste mi ru 'jug ni nyin cig srog//rlung dang sa dang chu me rlung dang nam mkha'i khams ni ji srid bar du rnam chad pa'o//dang por yang dag srung bya ma lus
30b5(rgyud): rgyal ba'i sku ste sngags pas dngos grub don du'o lus kyi dngos po med pas dngos grub me dang bde mchog skye ba 'dir mi thob//de phyir lus kyi don du nyin mtshan dus su 
30b6(rgyud): rtsa ba'i sbyor ba sgom par bya'o//lus grub pas ni dngos grub gzhan ma srid pa nas su ji bgyir rab tu 'gyur//stong rab tu swogs lam bla ma la nges dbang gis gzugs mnyan 
30b7(rgyud): mtha' ru sgom par bya//de nas rtsa'i 'khor lo la ni srog dang thur sems rlung yang rtan par bya//de nas zla ba 'gog ni bza' bzhin byed//ste byang chub sems//de ni cung zad gang 'dod nyin
30b8(rgyud): mtshan cung zad kyis ni skye ba 'di ru 'thob//nyin zla 'gog pa mnyam dang mi mnyam la gnas dbus ma dang ni me la zhugs//srog dang thur sel gnyis po dag ni dang po'i nyi ma la gnas
31a1(rgyud): dus min 'chi ba'i slu//rlung las gang byas gang ka lag dang bcas the bong tshigs dang sen mo mthar thug pa//'khor lo drug por sangs rgyas lha mo mi 'jigs phyag gi pad mo rnal 'byor bas 
31a2(rgyud): sgom bya//thur sel 'gag par lus mchog rlung nad ma lus dag la'o//srog 'jug ban kan la yin//slar yang spangs pa de gnyis grol bas 'khrigs pa'i nad la'o//steng dang 
31a3(rgyud): 'og ni yang dag 'grog pa lus dang cha'i nad las bde byed 'gyur//lte steng srog gi rlung yin nad rnams kun 'joms lte ba'i rtsa bar bzhan ma'o//skum par thur sel rlung dang lam gsum la 
31a4(rgyud): gnas zla ba'i sbyor bas 'joms//steng du dbugs mi bde ba dang blo nad dug rnams gsum ste mig nad laswogs so////
31a5(rgyud): mchan khung g.yas g.yon du ma yang ni 'chi stobs kyis srog rnams kag nas mnye bar bya//g.yas dang g.yon du blo bur du ni bdud rtsa mchog rgyu ste mchan 'og g.yon nas g.yas su
31a6(rgyud): 'o//rnal 'byor sbyor bar ldan pas 'chi ste 'chi rtags kyang ni zla ba phyed kyis 'joms//rkang pa rdo rje dkyil dkrung gis gnas lag pad ma'i bzung bas rgyab kyi gzer bas 'joms//
31a7(rgyud): rkang pa steng dang spyi bo 'og tu lus la ban kan nad ni ma lus 'phrog par byed//srog ni lag par rab 'dzag nad ni myur ba'i nyi mas chu 'gag nad ni 'joms par byed//sngo dro chu ru gdong bcug ming 
31a8(rgyud): med dag gis so'i 'phreng ba rab gnyer bya//mig la mig sman yin te nad ni 'joms byed chu yis rtor ba 'am//gan rkyal nyal nas lte ba lag pa'i pad mos gzung nas ma zhu nad rnams//'bras 
31b1(rgyud): rnams 'brum la dam bcas cha'is bdugs na bu la nges par 'phel bar 'joms par byed//nyi ma'i 'o mas byugs pas yang na so srin dang bcas gzer 'gyur na//stag gis sa bon du ba sbu gu rtsog+sa
31b2(rgyud): kyis bzung na//srin spogs 'joms par byed;lo phyed kyis ni dkar po'i 'dze ni lus mchog dag la sngags pas 'joms na gzhan ci smos//shes rab sbyar la rang gi sems ni bshor la bde srog
31b3(rgyud): rlung 'gags pa'i phyir//bdun bcu lon gnyer mas kar dang bcas lo gnyis rdzogs na 'joms par byed de steng phyag rgya'i sgrub 'gyur nyin mtshan cung zad kyis ni phyag rgya sgom phyir rjod kyi 'dod chags
31b4(rgyud): ma zad pa'i phyir ma yin//gtsug tor phug nas phud po dang ni khams rnams spangs te 'chi ba ru ni 'gro//phyag rgya sbyar ba nyams shing bde ba'i 'bras mi ster ba mi rnams skye ba 'di nyid 
31b5(rgyud): la'o//nyin dang mtshan nyin bzhin dus su gsung gi rdo rje myang ba'i don te gzhan du sngags pa rnams la mos par gsungs pa min//nyin bzhin dus su bzang kun ni bde ba'i don du gsungs
31b6(rgyud): kyi mi 'jug phyir ma yin//dpal ldan spyod pa dngos grub rgyu'i phyir ni dud khrod skor gi gtse ba'i phyir ma yin//mi skyod cung zad dro bas kha'i nad rnams so'i gzer ba de nyid dang//tho rangs mi skyod snas 
31b7(rgyud): 'thungs mgo'i nad 'joms de bzhin chu ni snas 'thungs pa//rna ba mig du blugs pas dri chu tsha brang gnyis ka'i nad ni 'joms par byed//'byung pos khyor la mi skyod snar blugs chab gsum chas bde ba
31b8(rgyud): ster te mches btab la'o//dri chen chu dang sa bon khrag ste mi shar bcas zos pas tshe yang ste//bsam gtan dang bcas me tog snas pa dung nyer ma dang bcas yan lag min skye rgas pa 'joms//
32a1(rgyud): @#//sha sbyangs skyugs pa dang sres mar me lnga ni zos na nad rnams 'joms par byed//bud med me tog khu ba dang sres lo gcig zos nas rgas pa 'joms par byed//lan tsha skyur pos rlung 'joMs
32a2(rgyud): rab 'gyur mngar dang ska ba ba dag ni 'khrigs pa'i dag//tig ta ma lus tsha ba gsum bcas sman nam bcud kyis bad kan 'joms par byed//ra'i 'o ma tsha ba gsum ldan bad kan 'joms te ma
32a3(rgyud): he'i yad ni 'khris pa'i dgra//rnga mo'i 'o mas rlung 'joms pa'i tsha ba gsum ldan 'o mar nyid de bzhin nad rnams gsum 'joms so//mdza' ti'i chu skol dro ba kha nad zad par byed cing so'i nad
32a4(rgyud): kyang de bzhin no//til mar dngos po'i chu dang tsha ba gsum ldan lan tsha rna ba'i nad ni 'joms//mar dang 'o ma sbrul dang dmar po skol bas rtag par sna'i nad rnams 'joms
32a5(rgyud): dkar ko ti dang thong shol dmar po til gi ta bong bu dang bcas skus na 'bras kyi phreng ba 'joms//lag pa rkang pa bya ba zhing ba'i pad ma mnyam pa srog gi rlung bkab te//ji srid
32a6(rgyud): sa la lus ni ltung bar mi 'gyur ba 'am rims ma grol ba'i bar//yang nas yang du ting nge 'dzin gis 'jigs byed tsher ma nges par 'joms//snying kha'i pad ma'i zla ba'i steng du dri med zla ba'i lta bu'i
32a7(rgyud): sna tshogs yum sgom bya//sran chung btags nas btung bar mi bya rnams rims dang ldan par tsher ma 'joms byed des//thod pa mi skyod sres la bdar bas 'jigs byed tsher ma 'joms te rims kyis
32a8(rgyud): mtha' ru'o//sngags ni sngon du yige oM ste chu dang me rlung rdo rje sngon du min//de nyid kyis ni srung bya spyi gtsug 'grin pa lte ba gsang la sogs mnyam par skyu ru so ba gzhan yang de bzhin ti ti ri'i lo ma'i phye
32b1(rgyud): phye dag ni chu ni nyi zlas mnyes te yang ni dri bral rkang pa gcig lhag skol bar bya//skol ba de ni kha ra dang sres nyin nas ni zhag gsum bar du btung//sos ka'i lam ni nyi ma'i 'od kyis gdungs pas 'chi 
32b2(rgyud): ster khams bdun la gnas dang 'joms byed//gser dang nyi ma lcags bzangs snod du lhang tshor lcags te sbrang rtsi dang ni sres par bya//sa bon phyed kyis sgongs la dri ma bral ba'i ro yis srang ni drug 
32b3(rgyud): tu byas nas ba'i dar ba gdug pa'i me yis skol bar byas te chur khu blang par bya//dpal ldan gong bu lcags bzangs snod du slar yang skol ba ci srid phyed kyi tshad//chung nas khan d+ha dag pa blangs te srang
32b4(rgyud): brgyad dam rgu ni der blugs bya//drod pa'i bar du dus rnams kun du zan bzhan bor te de ni bza' bar bya//zla ba ru drug gis lha'i lus te ska dkar mnyer ma bral zhing 'di yis 'chi
32b5(rgyud): min nyid du 'gyur de nas de'i nyin bzhin dus kun bzang ste 'og tu gong bus dman//sngar brjod sa bon rgyal po ro yis mnyam la slar yang phyed kyi sre//lan tsha skyur pos mnyam ste
32b6(rgyud): sna tshogs du ma'i sman gis ci srid gcig tu 'gyur//'khris pa skyar po mu bzi laswogs lhang cher rdo dang rngul chus mnyam par rdar//des dul rngul rdo dang gser gnyid gyur te snyim pa phyed kyis tshad 
32b7(rgyud): kyis so//rnon po nam mkha' las skyes a lam tshad dang ba k+sha ra tsa rim pa yis//'phel ba ni nyi ma'i nyis lngab ro mnyam byas nas nyi ma'i lhag mas sreg par bya//slar yang lan tshas dag 'gyur ba'i 
32b8(rgyud): bdag nyid laswogs bsregs pas de bzhin 'jam pa'o//sum tsha rdo la sbyin bdungs te bkang bas dag pa'i gser nyid do//sug smel pur phreng ba g.yo ba srin dang 'bras bcas pa rol ri skyes dang//
33a1(rgyud): @#//ka ko la dang seng ge'i chu skye ste ud pal 'bras bu ri dgas las skyes dmar po dgra rul pa//klu dang bsel yul lo ma srang dang chu dang lcug ma nam mkha' 'khyogs 'di dag rdzas 
33a2(rgyud): lnga drir gyir ri dgas ri bong rnams kyis spos dang me tog kun snyen laswogs pas//mig zla med de rgya mtsho mda' dang yon tan chu rter mda' lag zla mig mda' mig zla mig me rgya mtsho zla dus mda'
33a3(rgyud): mig dang ba zla 'jig rten//sug smel laswogs cha bgrangs le tshe lnga lnga yongs su rim pas brgyan//le tshe'i sbyor rtsegs la gnas rdzas rnams kyis ni drir 'gyur dag pa'i cha ni mtshan 
33a4(rgyud): nyid zhes bya yis//mdza' ti'i dang por sug smel lcug ma 'dab ma bum pa gnas su rim gis bkod bya ba//rgya skyegs 'o ma swogs pa'i slar yang dkar po spos kyi bya ba la ni 
33a5(rgyud): sbos//ga pur khan d+ha me tog ror ldan mi'i cha ni sen mo yis//dri chus de rtags 'on kyang sbrang rtsi spangs pas yongs su spos kyi ring du bya//lte ba laswogs seng ge 
33a6(rgyud): chu dang ri bong nam mkha'i gnas su khrus kyi sbyor bar 'god//nam mkha'i gnas su khrus kyisbyor bar bgod//'gran zla ti stag dang ha ra ni ste 'joms byed de bzhin du gzhan gi dri shing
33a7(rgyud): cha'i tshad//sa pa ur pa'i yan lag brgya dang bcu pa mngar te de dang de bzhin gnye//sngar brjod mi zlog chu skyes lus dang 'dud pa'i 'dab ma 'ang til mar la phye ma lan bdun lta bur 'gyur
33a8(rgyud): zhing lus gdung dang ni kha la yang/shing tsha la mdun pa dung rnams 'bras 'dab sbyor smin laswogs pa//de ltar sum bcu'i dbye bas shin du brgyan pa sna tshogs dri spos laswogs sbyor//byas te rdzas
33b1(rgyud): ni rnam par dag pa'i 'bras bu sbyor ba smin dang sku dang sgos pa yis//cha rgyad dang pos skabs 'gyur 'dab ma dbang gis de'i gnyis ldab drag po'i spos//phyin nas rdzas kyi tshad ni bu ram yang ni 'phel
33b2(rgyud): 'gyur te//sos ka'i sbyor ba yis rkang pa'i chal dung dang spos te sbrang rtsi yang ni dkar po rdzas mnyam sreg//gong bu dung gi ma la yang dang yang g.yo zla ba dang ldan de bzhin no//me tog bzang po smin
33b3(rgyud): pa'i dri ni nyi ma cung zad tsam la ji srid 'dod pa ste phyin nas rgya'i cha'i sgo ba 'bras bu gsum dang lcug ma ri bong 'dod dang bsnyen pa dang bcas pas shing gru chung dang ra chu me tog ro mnyam me tog las skyes mtha'
33b4(rgyud): ru skol bar bya zla gcig song bas smin par 'gyur te rigs dang mnyam snyen pa lte bar 'bigs//bsang ba'i dri ni rul ba ro dang sen mo g.yo ba sgos la spyir la ni bza' ba'o//de ltar 
33b5(rgyud): bsil dang du ru ska dang 'gu ru yang ni ri bong sgo ba'i dgongs pa me tog go//phug par ga bur lte ba 'bras bu gsum ka rtsi khrus kyi sbyor bas yang dag go//bsang bas shing rtsa 'grin par
33b6(rgyud): ni 'bab na tsa ra dang 'gu ru yang ni ri bod sgo ba'i dgongs pa me tog//ldan dril phyir la ni 'grin tha'o//dag pa'i chu skyes la ni rjes bral sbrang rtsi rnam par dmar zhing dri chu'i chu yis btags//
33b7(rgyud): smin pa'i spos kyis sgra dang drag po mar bcas rdul ni 'phreng ba'i cha'i rim po yis//kham gnyis kham des sres zhing ma la g.yo balcags dang ga pur dag kyang ngo//cha'i dang po mthar ni sbrang rtsi dkar po dang 
33b8(rgyud): por spos las sreg par bya//me tog bzang pos sgo byas nyi ma bcung zad lnga ni dri'i chu yis sre//dpal gi thod par mi 'dzag par ni kha mi srang bcings sra tshil gis sbyar bya//yangs pa'i snod byas yang 
34a1(rgyud): @#//na sa'i 'og gam phye mas bkang byasate//smin mas chu gnas zla drug sbyor bas 'gyur zhing de ni lte mchog dri//til ni dag par byas te skol ba'i chu dang 'dab ma spros dang byug
34a2(rgyud): pa laswogs pas phyi nas mdza ti laswogs me tog gis ni nyi ma cung zad ji srid 'dod pas bsre//'khrul 'khor til mar blangs te mkhas shing shun snod du gzhag par bya'o rgyal po kye//khrus 
34a3(rgyud): sam sku byug la ni me tog til mar ma bcos pas ni dga' byed 'gyur//sas 'dzin nyi zla shed bu mig dang 'dod pa nor dang drag po rgyal po dang ni me//phyogs dang 'byung bug drug tshes dang chu rter mtha'i
34a4(rgyud): tshe la gzhag par bya//le tshe bzhi'i drangs ni chu gter me ste ma lus bri bar byas nas su dpal ldan 'khor lo tsad kyis rgyab du bris te sgrum ma rnams la btsa ba'i dus su bstan par

53b1(rgyud): sha za mo ni dri'i rdo rjer 'gyur zhing mi ni gzugs kyi rdo rjer ma ste ma dibang kye//gdug pa'i srin mo gang de ro'i rdo rje ma ste klu mo rdo rje ma//mdzes ma sems can phan byed rtul bzhugs nges rga tshulakhriMs

54a1(rgyud): @#//'byung skyes kho bo las skyes pa//rta sbrul byi ba rko dkar spre'u dang ni ri bong sha ba e shu rtsangs pa brgyad pa ste//kug rta ne tso dang ni khu byug ri skyegs rnams ni gsal bar rgya

54b1(rgyud): drug rnal 'byor rgyud des stengs la gnas rnal 'byor ma yang de ltar ro//chu dang mchod yon dri spos me tog dang ni 'bras bu ma nyams mar me dang//lha bshos dang ni 'di ru nam bza' gyur par yang ni

54b7(rgyud): rma bya'i 'jug ma 'dir ni brgyad pa'o//sum bcu drug gis mtshan dbye bas mchog gnas rnal 'byor pas mchod par bya//slar yang rmad rigs la swogs mtshan mar 'gyur te yon tan dbang g+si

55a1(rgyud): /thod 'gyur yul gyi mtshan ma rnams bzhi 'ang ma rung chen gcod pan 'phreng ba rgyal po kye//dri chen dri chu dang khrag gyur sha bcas lha mo rnams kyis bzhi pa ste//rna ba sna mig lce dang

87a6(rgyud): ste//mgrin par mgo bo'i phreng ste spyi bor gcod pa na rdo rje dar sbyangs thub pa dang/'gul do dang ni rkang pa rkang gsum la gdub lag pa las snye skye rags rna cha dang//thod 'phreng rangs pa dag

87a8(rgyud): bsnams//bdung dang dbang phyug zhabs kyis rtsa ste nyi zla me rlung dkyil 'khor lus la thal ba'o//phyag rgya klu dbang dang bcas dus mtha' me 'dra mkha' 'gro ma la mdzad pa'o//phyogs 'dab 
87b1(rgyud): 'kha' 'gro ma rnams lhag pa'i rde'u chung rdo rje khwa twa~M kha gnyis nyis//rin chen chu khrag mi sha dag dang mtshams gdab la gnas phyag na sa bon snod//de bzhin lha mo brgyad po phan tshun bde 
87b2(rgyud): bde bas snyoms zhugs 'khor lo kun la'o//ser po dkar dang dkar dmar reng lta bu nag dang ljang khu ste sngo mdog rang gi phyogs//kha dog mtshan ma gtso bo bzhin du rgyal ba bskyed mdzad
87b3(rgyud): rigs ni spyan mchog sngar bzhin no//phan tshun 'khyud pa bdag dang bzhan rigs dbang gis ma lus rig par bya//sbyin sogs phung po 'byung ba yul dang yul can bzhan yang dbang po lnga
87b4(rgyud): sogs dang/sa phyogs phyogs kyi 'dab mar rgyal ba'i sras po rnams kyis lha mo rnams par sbyangs//lhun po phyogs kyi dbye ba rgyad kyi phyogs 'tshams la gnas 
87b5(rgyud): gnas dang nye ba'i gnas rnams dang//zhing 'thung spyod 'du sa pa'i dur khrod sa dang khyim dang rlung gis sangs rgyas nyi ma'i sa 'dir srin bu mi rnams lus kyi 'byung po lha 
87b6(rgyud): dang lha min rnams//nus pa gtso ba'i nyi ma gcig ste nyi ma zla dbang gis rigs can bcu//rgyab du lha mo'i lhag byed thim pa'i mdun ste ngo bos so//rlung dang dbang ldan lha min
87b7(rgyud): me dang mkha' lding 'og lta dang//gshin rje gnod sbyin gzhan/gtsug tor snying dkar migran gnas spyi bo la gnas lte ba bsang ba 'khor lo la//'khor lo rtsib brgyad steng 'og nyin
87b8(rgyud): mtshan chu srin las ni dus 'byor gnyis gnyis 'tshams//gtso bo ba'i pad mo rdo rje nur bu gnas pas mi 'gyur thig le rnams//dga' ba kun gi kun nas bde mnyam gnas te rdo rje'i
88a1(rgyud): @#//dus kun du=zla ba zhu ba de la gang zhig bla ma'i zhal la zhugs pa de nyid dus kyi 'khor lo ste//dus brjod bdud rnams 'chi ba'i 'jigs byed mkha' 'gro ma'i 'khor lo 'di nyid do//
88a2(rgyud): thabs dang shes rab yi ge dag ni rigs dbang gis mkha' 'gro ma'i ming//bzlas pa rdo rje'i gsum dang tshig ldan thog mar bar dang tha mi phyi//srog gi byin rlabs gang yang gnyis ka la bgrod rang rang las
88a3(rgyud): tshul gis//myur du 'grub par 'gyur zhing ro'i 'gror gnas mkha' 'gro ma dang rdo rje mkha' 'gro'o//gang zhig dbugs ni dbus mar skyes pa'i dus su rkang pa dang bcas ro dang mda' ru 'gyur//de yis
88a4(rgyud): 'khrol brgyad yin steng 'og dbang gis sgo'i dbus na gnas pa'i lha ma'o//rkang gcig du ma las skyes srid gsum skyed mdzad phyag na thod pa'i dri gug bsnams//nyi zla'i steng na g.yon rkyang bde

88a7(rgyud): rkang la nyi zla'i bdan//rtsibs ni nyi zla'i rgyu yin te pad ma'i 'dab dang sgo bzhi mtshams kyi bdan dag la//slar yang rgyu ba'i yon tan gnyis ni nyin phyed zla ba de dag gdan//rdzogs pa'i nyi mas rgyu 
88a8(rgyud): ba mnyam dang mi mnyam rkang pas rgya mtsho rgyu bas gdan//nyi ma'i ma rdzogs pa'i zla bar 'gyur te rigs kyis dbang gis gtso bo'i kha sbyor la'o//rlung la reg pa'i gdong cig gzhan yang yon tan
88b1(rgyud): dbang gis phyag rgya zhal ni bzhi pa dang//dri bsogs reg skyes bar la yon tan zhal zhes sa steng phyag rgya yi ni lnga pa'o//stong pa rnam lnga zhal rnams yani zhing mkha' la phyag rgya' zhal ni drug pa'o//
88b2(rgyud): de ltar nges par ye shes khams la yon tan khams gsum dbang gis stong pa drug gi zhal du 'gyur//bzhi la gcig sres bde mnyam 'bras ster lnga pa la ni gnyis dang gsum gis bsres
88b3(rgyud): shes rab thabs zhal bcu ste yon tan rnam gsum dbang gi zhal bcu gnyis//thun mtshams dbye bas rnam bzhi rnam gsum zhal ni dus sbyor dbye bas kha dog gsum//dus 'byor phyed ni dpung pa'i
88b4(rgyud): dbye bas mi mnyam pa la mnyam 'khyud sku thugs la//thun 'tshams cha la bdun dang brgyad ste zhal gsum ro dang klud 'khyud pus mo'i char//shes rab sbyar zhing snyoms zhugs pa 
88b5(rgyud): dang rgod la swogs pa gdengs ka'i dbang po gnyis dang khro gnyer phag rgod bya dgod bcas brjod pa//de dag dpal ldan g.yas kyi zhal nas mchog tu dga' bas sna tshogs gtso bos 
88b6(rgyud): spros//lte bar yang dag sa la gser mchog dang 'dra phyag na 'khor lo thub pa dang 'dra phyag na 'khor la thub pa dang//sgrib pa sel dang spyan ma rengs byed ma dang rdo rje dri'i mi dbang rgyal po kye//
88b7(rgyud): 'od zer can dang dbang po brgya byin zhal gnyis dang bcas rje'u rigs dang klu lasogs pa kun rtso bos ltos pa med pas skye bo kun la rtse ba nub kyi zhal nas spros pa'o//gtsug tor rnam sdang ba las ni 
88b8(rgyud): phyag na rdo rje mchog dang mi 'khrugs dbyings kyi dbang phyug gnyis//gtsug tor chos kyi dbyings nam mkha'i yon tan dbang gis lag na rdo rje laswogs gzhan//bsang ba dag las ma lus bde mnyam 
89a1(rgyud): @#//gtor te rdo rje sgra laswogs pa dang//steng dang 'og na yon tan kun nas spro ba srog chags rnams kyi thar pa'i (rgyur) phyogs 'dab la ni//spyan swogs dang mtshams kyi 'dab ma
89a2(rgyud): la gnas thod pa khrag gis bkang//g.yon rkyang nyi ma'i steng dus mtha'i sprin 'dra zla ba'i dbang po gnan//thod pa'i 'phreng ba bcings shing chu rter he ru ka'i zhal la nyi ma yi//phyag rgya sbrul dang 
89a3(rgyud): stags pags mchog gis glang chen bags pa dang ni sgyu ma 'dzin//gnag dmar dri med ri bong lus kyi lha mos phyi rol ga'akhor gsum dag dang//'khor lo drug dang ni khro bo las skyes phyag gnyis
89a4(rgyud): zla zhal de ni de bzhin 'byung po las skyes pas//sgor ni tsan dan sha pa la gnas rtag par 'tshams la dri gug thod pa phya ga na bsnams//dpa' bo rig byed phyag na rdo rje re'u chung dril bu
89a5(rgyud): rdo rje khwa traM khA//dpal ldan lhun po'i phyogs brgyad sa chu 'i dkyil 'khor la ni gnas dang nye ba'i gnas//zhing 'dun pa nye bdun dur khrod rnams ni me dang rlang gi dbus na'o//
89a6(rgyud): de bzhin grong khyer yul gyi phyogs 'tshams la gnas lha rnams dag ni rig par bya//shes rab rgyud kyi mngon brjod srid gsum bla mas mdun gyi zhal nas spro bar byed//snying po 'khor lo'i
89a7(rgyud): rgyu cha dag ste nyi zla'i pad mas dkyil 'khor phyed kyi ni//ka ba rdo rje 'phreng ba la ni phung po'i khams chen lhag pa'i lha yis mdzes//phyi ra'i rtsig pa la ni yul dang yul can nyi ma dang ni zla ba'i
89a8(rgyud): bdan la bzhugs//sgo skyong rigs gzhan nyi ma la gnas ma lus mu khyud dag la'o//drug po de lasogs te de bzhin gzhan yang rnal 'byor rgyud dang gsang 'dus dag nas gsungs//bzung 
89b1(rgyud): dang logs su khro bo yul dang yul can 'dzam pa lha ba la ni phyag na snyi ma bsnams//ngag dang rkang lag bshang lam b+ha ga nyi zla'i dus kyi rtsa'i tshad bzhin no//thabs dang shes rab ma lus zhal 
89b2(rgyud): gsum ro'i phyag ni g.yas kyi zhal nas spro//snying po'i 'khor lor brgya mtsho'i zur te dkyil 'khor las ni nges par sum bcu mi dbang kye//khams dang phung po lha ste gzhan yang 'phar ma la na gzugs
89b3(rgyud): bsogs rdo rjes brgyad//dkyil 'khor phyir gnas phyogs bcu la bkod khro bo'i tshogs kyis nyi mar gnas pa nyid de//de nas ra ba'i rtsig pas chu gter zur gis bcom pa ma lus byang chub 
89b4(rgyud): dpa'o//sgyu 'phrul dra bas dbye ba gsum ste zhal gsum ro'i phyag rgya ni lha rnams sku'i dbye bas so//bskal pa'i dang por gang yang ma lus bems po'i thugs kyi dbang gis g.yon gi
89b5(rgyud): zhal nas spro//rnal 'byor rjes rigs rgyud la 'di ni mkha' 'gro sdom pa chen po thun tshong mngon par 'tshams la'o//shes rab yan lag dmar dang ser dus kyis 'khor lo dngos po
89b6(rgyud): nges par 'jug//shes rab dngos po dkar po yan lag sbyin nag 'phra bar rdo rje can gi nas g.yon du shar//shes rab dngos kyis tha dad lus mchog la 'gyur g.yon dang shar du
89b7(rgyud): zhi ba'o//g.yas dang yan lag gzhan ni drug la ba mchog gi dngos po tha dad de bzhin no//shes rab tha dad rgyal ba mchog gi tshogs rigs ngo bo dag gi gnyis nyid do//shes rab 
89b8(rgyud): ma'i tshogs kyi rigs ni gtso bo'i ngo bos tha dad nges par 'tshams su'o//g.yas g.yon gzung ni nyi zla'i sku ste yang ni 'di shar dang nub dag ni//gnas dang ser po sngo dang ljang gu 
90a1(rgyud): @#//yang ni de bzhin mkha' dang sa 'og yang dag gnas//dkar po rnams kyi shes rab dmar po zla ba ltar dkar de bzhin sngon por gsung//de ltar nges par lha ma'i rang rig
90a2(rgyud): la gnas pa'i lha rnams rig par bya//shes rab rgyud ni shar la gzhan zhal nas ni rnal 'byor rjes su 'jug pa nyid do//lho'i zhal nas rgyal ba'i bdag pos rnal 'byor rgyud gsung g.yon gi zhal
90a3(rgyud): nas bya ba swogs//shar nas yang ni rnal 'byor yong ste gzhan yang nub kyi zhal nas ma lus dbu ma'o//lho'i zhal nas mdo sde gsungs te dkar po'i zhal nas bye brag smra ba nyid//nub kyi zhal
90a4(rgyud): nas ni nges brjed dag ste g.yon gi zhal nas rgyal dbang gis ni mchod sbyin brjod//lho'i zhal nas ni snyan tshig dag ste mi rig mchog gis srid srung shar zhal nas//kyi la'i
90a5(rgyud): rgyud ni shar zhal nas de yang ni nub kyi zhal nas mkha' lding 'byung ba'i rgyud//grub mtha' g.yon zhal nas te ri'i rtser mchur ba lta bu'o//lho nas khyab 'jug chos khyab nas da ltar

90a8(rgyud): lho zhal nas ni gzab pa dag ste bdag rgyab kyi zhal nas mchog tu 'jigs pas 'gro//shar zhal 'khrig pa nges par shing dkar po mchog gi zhal na rmi lam nyid do rgyal po kye//rgyab nas rnge'u chung rigs

90b6(rgyud): bsod nams ye shes (rjes)chags rtag tu bde ster ba yang byang chub sems dpa' byin gis brlabs pa gang phyir mya ngan 'das 'gro dam tshig bde ba'i dbang ste nyon mongs bdud ni bcom pas so//de phyir
90b7(rgyud): nyen bzhin dus su de dag rnams la bdud kyis bgyegs ni du ma byed//dang por las kyi phyag rgya rgyal ba'i lan cig skyes te bde 'phel ba'i don du bsten//de nas nyi ma'i gzugs ni gdong rkang

91a1(rgyud): @#//dbyings yin no//mi 'gyu bde sras ster ba'i phyag rgya 'di bzhin rnal 'byor pas ni sgom bya ba//dus rnams kun du bde dga' la gnas 'jig rten lam ni rnam par grol ba dang//
91a2(rgyud): grong khyer dang ni 'brog dgon dud khrod btsang dang mi gtsang gnas dang lhag pa'i khyim dag dang//rigs dang mi rigs mi rtogs lus la bde ba'i stobs ster bza' btung longs spyod dag gis so//

91b3(rgyud): par rab tu 'gyur na nges par sangs rgyas yum du 'gyur ro//de nas gzhan ni me tog gsum gi pad ma dkar po 'dzin pa 'jam pa la dang rdo rje'i phyag gzhan ma me tog brgyad ni phyogs mtshams la

91b6(rgyud): de las 'phongs pa rnams la de yang pad ma 'dzin pa chags can chags pa'i rgyu ru'o//bgyegs rnams gzhom pa'i don du bdag gis pad ma ral gri don yod grub par 'gyur//zhes sdang las ni gang

92a1(rgyud): //can smin pa'i don du nges par dang bral bsod nams ma yin bsags rnams la'o//gtsang ma'i gnas bsogs dag tu gang zhig bcom ldan 'das bzhugs pa dag ni nyan

92a6(rgyud): pa yang sems can du ma'i ched du /rgyud du e bas yang spu Ta zhes tshig gang dag gis shes pa nyid//rdo rje can ni gang zhig zla ba zhu ba las spyi 'grim snying pad lte ba gsang ba ru//rdo rje bud med
92a7(rgyud): skye gnas de ni mchog gis char gnas thar pa'i thig le gsum gis so//sangs rgyas zhing la rab zhugs bcom ldan 'di de rnal 'byor rnams kyis rig par bya//gcig lan du mar mthong ste smon

92b1(rgyud): kyi sems la rab tu 'byung//'khor lo la bzhugs bsod snyoms dag la gshegs te gnas lnga rnams kyi bsod nams don//dud 'gro yid btags lha min lha dang klu dang bong sogs 'phags
92b2(rgyud): pa mi rnams la//'byung dang 'byung 'gyur da ltar rnam pa gsum gis rtag par bden pa'i chos ni gsung mdzad cing//srid gsum ma lus skad gis mtha' yas lam la yang dag dgod par 
92b3(rgyud): 'dzad//bde mnyam 'bras ster kun gter mkhan bsung 'di ni lha rnams kyi ni skad ma yin//dpag med yon tan sangs rgyas rnams kyi sprul sku sangs rgyas rnams la rtogs pa dka'//srid
92b4(rgyud): gsum gnas su bdag nyid ston par 'dzad de dbang po yi ni gzhu dang mtshungs//dngos po du mas tha dad rgyal ba mi rnams dang bcas rang rang dag gis sems can de la 'jug//skye med chos
92b5(rgyud): 'di nam mkha' bzhin du 'khrul te 'dir ni bskyed pa'o//rnam pa thams cad yul dang yul can gis ni rtogs dka' ba//rgyal ba'i sku//rdo rje gsung ni sems can kun gis rang gi 
92b6(rgyud): sems la brjod cing chos rnams rdzogs par mdzad//sems can kun gi sems kun la gnas rdo rje can gis rdo rje'i thugs//dngos rnams 'dzin pa po ste dri med nor 'dra ye shes rdo rje de nyid do

123a1(rgyud): @#//de ltar zla ba gsum gis ni//lo nyi shu lon chu tshod te//gsum po 'di rnams 'di spyod do//zla ba la sbyong phur bu yang//chu tshod rnams su gsal ba nyid//drag po'i grangs dang bzhi char bcas
123a2(rgyud): chu srang sum bcu tham pa rnams//dus kyi chu tshod du ni gsal//nyi ma'i bu ni spyod pa yin//gcig tu 'gyur ba nyi mar gshad//de ni bsam du yang sgres pa//zla ba phyed dang gnyis mtshan bcas//nyi ma

127b2(rgyud): dag pa'i res dang tshes kyis kyang//rgyu skar cha ni gsum byas te//skye ba'i rgyu skar dang po yi//dgu dang dgu ni dgu pa yis//bdun pa zla ba dang thabs gcig//cha ni ma mnyam spang bar
127b3(rgyud): gyur;nad pa rnams ni 'chi bar nyid//tha skar laswogs pas rtsa gsum//sbrul gis rnam par sbras pa ni//nam gru yi ni bar nyid do//sdig pa'i zla ba skye ba skar ma ni//galade mi rnams 
127b4(rgyud): kyis 'gyur na//rtse gcig la ni rnam par nyams;drug tshes brgyad dang nyi zla ba//bcu dang dgu ru bcas pa'i bcu//nyi ma zla ba dgra gza' lag//nyi bu bla ma la bgrod dkar//
127b5(rgyud): de'i rims kyis longs spyod pa//de'i bar gidus la'o//slar la yang ni de'i ni//sde tshan rgyad kyi yi ge yis ;bdug pa zhi bya min pa byed//zhi ba'i gzugs kyi gza' gang dag;
127b6(rgyud): mchog tu bde ba byed par 'gyur//dgun zla 'bring poma rig pa//chu srin song ba'i nyi ma'o//bum pa'i nyin dang dgun tha chungs;'du byed nya'i nya 'gyur ba//spying zla ra ba rnam par shes//lug gi 
127b7(rgyud): nyi ma ming dang gzugs//glang dang 'khrig par song ba ni//spyid zla 'bring spyad tha dbyar ba//skye mched drug dang kar ka'i nyin//'gyur ba seng ge'i nyi ma la//reg pa dang ni tshor ba ni;dbyar zla 'bring ;
127b8(rgyud): dbyar zla tha chungs la//btsun mo dang ni srang srong ba//ston zla ra ba ston 'bring bo//sred pa dang ni nyer len nyid//sred pa ru rab 'gyur ba//ston zla tha chungs sdigs pa'i nyi//gzhu nas nyi mar skye ba ste;
128a1(rgyud): @#//dag shi yang ni de bzhin du//dgun zla ra ba'i rim pas so//de ltar nyi ma zla phyed la//gnyi gar 'gro ba'i dbang las ni//rgyal po yan lag bcu gnyis so//dgun zla 'bring
128a2(rgyud): dang dgun tha'i mtshams/chu/srin du ni song nyi ma//de'i res 'ga' ma rig pa//de rjes gnyis pa la rga shi//'gyur ro mi'i bdag po kye//gsum pa la ni skye ba nyid//de bzhin srid pa laswogs
128a3(rgyud): pa;;yan lag bcu gnyis rab tu ni//rims bzhin yongs su rtsi bar bya//de phyir rgyad gnyis che re la//rga shir song ba'i dbang gis kyang//rgya lam rgyad gnyis kyi ni cha//zla phyed tshes su bstan 
128a4(rgyud): pa'i res//nyi ma rgyu ba'i dbang las so//rgya lam yan lag drug bcu na//dgun zla tha chungs mtshams 'du byed//bum song ba'i nyi ma ni//de ru 'du byed nyid shes bya//de'i gnyis 
128a5(rgyud): pa la rnams shes//su ni 'gyur ba'i nyi ma'o//mi dang gzugs ni gsum pa la//de ltar zla ba gnyis pa lag//nyi ma yi ni dbang gis 'gyur//spro dang sdud pa'i sbyor bas so//
128a6(rgyud): de la ma rig spogs yan lag//mi mthun par yang 'gyur ba la//bya ba thams cad bzang bar bya//'du byed laswogs mnyam pa gang//dbang skur mchog sbyin bag ma len//rtag tu
128a7(rgyud): mi dge bar ni 'gyur//de ltar phyogs kyis rab dbye dang//zla ba 'gro ba'i dbang gis ni//yan lag bcu gnyis ma lus pa//dkar po nag po nag po'i phyogs la ni//dang po'i tshes kyi 
128a8(rgyud): dbang las ni//spro dang sdud pa'i sbyor pas so//nyi ma mig dmar tog spen pa//g.yas dang rgyab tu yang dag gnas//dgra rnams don ni med par byed//g.yon du mdun du zhi ba'i gzugs//
128b1(rgyud): gza'i tshogs ni ma lus pa//rtsod pa'i sar yang de bzhin no//rnal 'byor ma yang mi shigs drag/rang gi rlung dang lhan cig pas//g.yas dang rgyab tu bde ba ster//g.yon du mdun du mchi ba'i gzugs//
128b2(rgyud): 'thun dang mi mthun par song nas//gnyis kyi nus pa 'joms par byed//tshangs pa drag po gzhon nu ma//mkha' lding bdag mor 'gro ba dang //phag mo lag len rdo rje ma//rgan byed ma dang dpal mo gnyis
128b3(rgyud): nor gi tshes rnams su 'gyur te//dang po'i tshes dang dgu pa la//rgya byin gnod sbyin me'i mtshams//bden bral dang ni gshin rje chu//rlung dang dbang ldan gi ni sar//'dir ni 'char bar byed pa yin//
128b4(rgyud): slar la yang ni tshes rnams la//brgyad gnyis dbye bas rnam par phye//dkar po'i phyogs ni mi shes pa//'di ru 'char bar 'gyur ba ni//brgya byin gshin rje chu gnod sbyin//nag po yi
128b5(rgyud): ni phyogs la yang //me dang srin po dang ni rlung //dbang ldan mtshams su rim pa bzhin//sgra gcan shar nas nub phyed dang //'gro ba'i nyi ma'i dbang gis te//nyi ma byang 'gro byang du'o
128b6(rgyud): chu nas me'i mtha' ru ni//de'i 'gro bar nges pa'o//nyi ma lhor 'gro lhor ru'o//shar nas thun gi phyed na ni//rlung gi mtshams su 'gro bar byed//lhor ru 'gro ba yang de bzhin//dbang ldan chu
128b7(rgyud): me gnod sbyin dang //srin po grong dang rlung du 'gro//nyi ma shar nas de nub par//rtsa ba'i sgra gcan de dor nas//rnam par rgyal bar rab tu 'gyur//rlung du smos pa'i gcig cig la//g.yul du zla ba sgra can
128b8(rgyud): gi//sgra'i stobs ni zab pa la//de dang 'thun pas gzhan med do//mthun dang mi mthun g.yul du ni//gug skyed dman pa rtsod pa dang //lhag pa yis ni snun par 'gyur//gzhan du mi brjod dbugs
129a1(rgyud): @#//gnyis ni snying du 'jug par 'gyur ba na//de ni rtsod las rgyal bar 'gyur//pho nya smras pa'i ming dang los//de ni rnam par rgyal 'gyur te//ming phyi ma yi ni snun par 'gyur
129a2(rgyud): sna tshogs kha dog dbyangs la swogs//'thun dang mi mthun par song bas//'bras bu med par ston pa nyid//dus ni g.yas dang g.yon pa ru//gal te mun pa can yin no//rtsod pa'i
129a3(rgyud): sa ru mi rnams ni//g.yul du rlog pa nyid 'gyur te//khyab 'jug dbang phyug lhan cig kyang//mi rnams la ni snun par 'gyur//de phyir rtsa ba'i sgra gcan dpal//yon po lha min 
129a4(rgyud): drag po rnams//sa'i 'khor du song ba yang//rtsod pa'i dus su shes par bya//gzhan du dgra ni rlog pa med//sa la rgyu skar rnams byas te//thub pa dus su bya ba ni/
129a5(rgyud): dbang po gnod sbyin chu gshin rje//de'i 'khor lo 'khor bzhi na pata;nyi ma 'gro ba'i dbang gis ni//ma lus par ni rig par bya//rgyu skar rgyu ba'i dus gang du//
129a6(rgyud): mun pa can du 'gyur ba'o//lta ba'i gnyis ka gtad par ru//g.yon du dgra'i dpung rnams ni//'dor na dgra'i ltung 'gyur te//lho'i dbus su rgyal bar 'gyur//sgra 
129a7(rgyud): gcan dus la gnas pa rnams//de ni 'chi bar nges par 'gyur//stobs ldan rnams ni mtshungs par ltung//lta ba'i cha ru nges song rnams//gnyi ka'i rigs kyi dbang las ni//de ni 
129a8(rgyud): dpung rnams 'jig pa yin//lta ba'i cha ni spangs pa rnams//rnam par rgyal bar 'gyur ba yin//lho'i cha ru gnyi ka yang//de ltar rtsa'i sgra gcan rnams//bla ma'i tshig ni rtogs pa'i 
129b1(rgyud): dus kyi 'khor lo shes par bya//rnal 'byor ma rnams mi shigs dag//gza'i tshogs dang lhan cig pa//rang gi dbyangs ni 'byung bar dang//de rnams thams cad dor bar bya//srid 
129b2(rgyud): pa gsum du rnam rgyal ba//rgyal rigs kyis ni gcig bzung ngo//gang gis nyi ma (ba)za' zhing//sum bcu pa yang 'jigs byed pa//drag po'i las can mig sman 'od//sa'i shar du
129b3(rgyud): nub dag dang//lo dang de bzhin byang du yang//nyin dang mtshan mor 'khor bar 'gyur//g.yul du dgra bos bcom pa na//rgyal 'khar gi ni nang du ni//sgyu yis 'jug par 'gyur
129b4(rgyud): na ni//phyi ru 'khrul 'khor rnams byas te//rgyun mi 'chad bar rdo rje dang ni//me'i mtha' ni 'phen par ni//'khor 'khor gis gcod ral gis swogs//sa'i 'og gi 
129b5(rgyud): gnas su ni//dra ba yi ni rtse mo 'byed//de ni 'khor ni phyir byed cing//nyi ma cung zad rnams kyis ni//gdug pa de dag sgrub par bya//ka ba bzhi la khru rgyad 
129b6(rgyud): gnyis//mthun dang mi mthun pa'i gnas//gnas brgyad du ni bu ga phug//chu rten dus dang dus rnams kyi//gdang bu rnams kyis bka' bas sbrel//rgyab du rdang bu gcig gis 
129b7(rgyud): sbrel//'khrul 'khor gi ni rtse'i tshad//khru ni bcu ru 'gyur ba ste//rtse mo'i cha ni de'i phyed//sgyogs mtha' 'khru do ma chad pa//bum pa yi ni thung 'gyur ba//lhag pa rtse mo'i cha yin no ࿒
129b8(rgyud): zlum su khru ni phyed dang gsum//thub pa yid du 'ong ba des pa yis//sor mo'i tshad ni bu ga'o//sgyogs mtha' ko log chu ra chud//gru gang tshad dor man chad snun//steng gi cha ru gzer gis tab 
130a1(rgyud): @#//khris ni lnga bcu tham pas bcing//sgyogs mda'i rtse mo yang ming gi//rtse mo'i tshad ni gi phyed zlum po//sgyogs mda'i rtse mo'i sor tshad du//thag pa sgril ma gdags pa ni//
130a2(rgyud): mtho gang zlum pa'i tshad tsam mo//sgyogs mda'i rtsa tshad chur khru gsum//thub pa nges par 'gyur ba ni//'ur 'thil thag pa lhan cig pa//de'i dbus rdo bcug la//stobs dang ldan pa'i nad 
130a3(rgyud): bcug ste//'dren par byed pas drangs pa'i tshe//btang na mkha' ru nyur bur 'gro//thogs pa med par lhung 'gyur te//khyams dang sgo la swogs par ni//ma lus par ni phye mar byed//ji ltar rdo 
130a4(rgyud): rje thog bzhin du//sa'i steng du 'gro bar 'gyur//de'i sor tshad zla ba phyed//sa'i steng gi gnas su ni//tshangs pa'i ri bo'i ngos gnyis su//brgyad gnyis kyis ni phyogs 
130a5(rgyud): su btang//lag pa gnyis kang lag 'thil dag//khru tshad bcas pa rnam par rtor//gtor tshe sa la reg pa nyid//'thun dang mi mthun brkang pa ru//'khrul 'khor nang du gnas 
130a6(rgyud): rnams kyis//sangs rgyas seng ge'i ni rang//rang 'dod gzhin du rdo ni ltung//rnam pa gsum du 'gro dbang gis//mkhar ni gzhom pa'i don du'o//gang gis stobs kyis mtshon cha rnams//
130a7(rgyud): thogs pa med par ltung de la//skye bo phal pas byar ci yod//mkhar gi dag rang gnas pa gang//dgra'i stobs kyi ma lus pa//de ltar gnas su ltung byed na//su zhig de dang lhan cig 'bran
130a8(rgyud): sa'i steng gi 'grin nas su//dgrod dka' rtsod pa 'ching bas na//mkhar de ru ni dgra su zhig//gang du 'khrul 'khor mtshon cha ni//gshin rje'i kha ru 'jug par 'gyur//bka' ba drug 
130b1(rgyud): swogs rtsibs shing dag//gnyi ka mnyam po kho nar 'gyur//rab tu zung gi khru dor ro//sbubs di la ni khru bcu gnyis//kha sbyar gnyis ka yang mnyam pa//lha dang chad pa nyid med pa//
130b2(rgyud): re mig so so'i thams cad pa//spang leb rnams kyis gshig par bya//lpags pa sbra tshil gos kyis btsag//nang du dus dang brgyad nyes pa//nyin mo byed pa'i sum 'gyur ro//'khrul 'khor
130b3(rgyud): du ma rab tu bya//gnyis gnyis stabs gi bar rnams su//rtsibs shing spang leb rnams kyang ngo//steng phyogs na ni rgya yis btab//steng du skye ba drug rgya 'dzin//rtsibs shing gnyi ga rang
130b4(rgyud): mnyam pa//'dun du 'khrul 'khor gtor rnams so//khung bur shing la bcings pa yi//rlung gi ba dan rnams kyis skyod//yang na rlung gis skyod par byed//de ru rang gi dpung
130b5(rgyud): rnams zhon//chu'i mkhar ni gshig don du//chu'i gter du 'gro bar 'gyur//stan cig rgya mtsho yi ni zur//kun 'gro ka ba la bcings pa'i//rlung gi ba dan gis ni
130b6(rgyud): skyod//gshog pa'i steng du mi rnams kyis//sa la gyen du skyod pa yang//thag pas steng du 'gro bar 'gyur//rlung gis bus bzhin pa yis ni//nam mkha' yi ni phyogasu
130b7(rgyud): 'gro//rdo'i mkhar gi spyi bo ru//de phyir me'i mar khu 'bod//dgra'i stobs ni thams cad dang//mkhar ni kun du sreg par byed//'khor lo'i rtsa ba 'khyud mo ru//'khrul 'khor shing
130b8(rgyud): la rtod par 'gyur//rtse mo ru ni 'khor lo bzhin//sor mo gnyis kyi bar tsam du//ral gri mchog gis spras pa ste//dkyil 'khor rtse mo thug par ni//'khor lo khru ni phyed dang gsum//
131a1(rgyud): @#//'khrul shing gi ni rab tu 'gyur//steng du gzhal du 'khrul dang bcas//'khrul rtser ral gri rnon po ni//myur bar ru ni 'khor 'gyur te//rtsa ba'i 'khrul 'khor du 'khor lo//
131a2(rgyud): thag pas bsrings nas 'khrul 'khor ni//nam mkha' ru ni 'gro 'gyur te//rang gi gnas nas rim pa bzhin//mkhar gi steng nas dgra gnas su//nges par rab tu song nas su//
131a3(rgyud): thag pa yi ni grangs te blangs//sa'i tshad ni de nyid kyis//gsungs pa bzhin du 'gyur ba ste//so sor phur pa dgra rnams kyi//kun nas sa gsor sbyar bar bya//dgra'i gnas su
131a4(rgyud): 'og nas ni//khyim la me ni rab tu btang//'khor rnams kyi ni steng gi cha//'dir ni lcags kyi gzer bu yis//khyad par de bzhin spras pa ni//kun nas zlum 
131a5(rgyud): po'i rnam pa la//phu ba rnams kyis kyang dag par bya//ma he'i ko ba rnams kyang rung//mi rnams kyis ni skyod bzhin pa//rang gnas nas ni mnyam por 'gro//
131a6(rgyud): 'obs su rkos pa'i bar du'o//rkos pa de ni bkang byas te//'khar gi rtsig pa'i sgo ru ni//thogs pa med par 'jug par 'gyur//bzhi pa gzer bu gnyis kyi gzer//sbril ba yon tan 
131a7(rgyud): dang bcas pa//mi 'gyur bar ni sdam par bya//rgyab tu lcags kyi gzer bu gnyis//srog shing la yang rab tu spras//sor mo phyed dang bcas pas bzlum//sor mo'i rtse mo sgres pa ni//
131a8(rgyud): de yang thu ru ma brgyangs pa//ye shes de gnyis byed las kyang//g.yul du rtsod pa'i dus su gtong//bgo dgos glang po de yis ni//phug pa yis ni 'gros par 'gyur//sa'i khong phu bka'
131b1(rgyud): ba gnyis//rtan pa ru ni nges springs pa//thag pa rnams kyis bkris bzhin pa//ral gri yu ba thag dbus su//bcug po dang ni bcu pa yis//sgril zhing lan mang du sgril ba//de dbus ral gri changs
131b2(rgyud): gzungs ni//gzhug pa ru ni 'gyur ba ste//mi 'gyur bar ni gzer gis gdab//sgril dang lhan cig rgyas btab pa//gral ral gri mchog dang ldan pa yang//rtse mo'i cha ni gnan pa nyid//de'i steng
131b3(rgyud): du spang leb kyi;bu ga'i ri mo ru ni 'gyur//'byung ba dang ni skyod pa yi//ral gri mchog nyid sbyin pa'o//'di ni lan cig myur bar ru//zla ba'i dbus su longs spyod do//gang gi rkang pa 
131b4(rgyud): 'khrul 'khor gi//steng du bag med par gzhag na//de'i ral gris gcod par 'gyur//mkhar gi sgo'i nang rol dang//mi'i bdag po'i gnas su ni//'khrul 'khor nyid kyi mtshan mo'o//
131b5(rgyud): dra mig steng du rtsa'i gnas//'khor lo'i tshad la dgu'i cha//nyi ma'i lag pa rnams su 'gyur//zur du ka ba gdo bussgyel//mthun dang mi mthun shing rta la//gnas kyi phyed dang gnyis
131b6(rgyud): tshad la//phyed kyi cha la rtsod pa'i gnas//mthun dangmi mthun pa 'phel ba//rta dang glang po rnams kyis so//de nas 'og nas gnas mtshan ma//steng du rnam par spras pa ni//me long
131b7(rgyud): do shal rgyal mtshan bsogs//de yang gcig gnyis gsum gi cha//zla ba lag pa rtse mo can//rtsa ba'i gnas dang gdang bu rnams//cha gcig phyed dang bcas bzlum po//pad ma 'i ni shing rta so//
131b8(rgyud): dga' ba'i khyad par mnyam pa yi//'khor rtsibs phyed dang gnyis kyi tshad//rta dang glang po'i dbang gis ni//khu ni phyed bcas rnam pa gnyis//lha rnams pad ma yi ni rigs//mchog tu dga' ba
132a1(rgyud): @#//bskyed pa dang dga' ba'i rigs ni mi'i rtso//'khor lo brgyad (la)rgya mtsho mig//'dir ni rnam pa gsum che ba//shing rta steng ni mnyam pa dang//'khor lo gcig pa lha'i nyi ma yi//mi mthun
132a2(rgyud): rta'i shing rta ni//lha ma yin rnams rnam par 'joms//phyir mi ldog pa'i rtsod par 'gyur//mi mthun pa dang mthun pa che//shing rta rgyal rigs rnams kyi ste//mthun dang mi mthun brtsod pa la//
132a3(rgyud): kha phyogs pa dag nyid steng 'joms te//mthun pa'i rgyab ni 'joms par 'gyur//khru drug lnga bzhi gsum gi char//ka ba'i mtha' ru phug pa ni//mnyam par rab tu 'gyur ba yin//rtsa ba'i steng 
132a4(rgyud): du phyed kyi cha//lha'i gzhal yas kha rim smad;pho brang gi ni dbang gis 'gyur//de bzhin rgyal po rnams kyang gnas;pho brang dbang gis rnam pa gsum//yul gi ltad mo dga' ston
132a5(rgyud): la//khru do khru gsum khyad par 'gyur//phyogs dang dus kun du bskor ba'i//'khor lo steng du ka rtse ru//pho brang dbang gi rnam pa gsum//phyogs kun du ni khyogs 'khor
132a6(rgyud): ba//brgyad gnyis brgyad dang de nas bzhi//phyogs dang dus dang dus lhag cing//'di dag bzhi ste sa ru ni//steng dang 'og tu 'khor bzhin pa//lag shing 'khrul shing zlum po la//bu mo
132a7(rgyud): rnams kyi bdan yinate//nang dang phyi ru chud pa dang//'og dang steng du song ba dang//phyogs brgyad rnams su 'khor ba'o//steng dang 'og du 'khor sbu gu//sbu gu can du 'khor 'gyur ba ni//
132a8(rgyud): kun nas de dag bya ba bya//sbu ge rtse mor bum pa'i gzugs//shin du yun rings bar du 'gyur//sbug gu'i (bu ga'i)tshad tsam gis//chu ni 'gugs par byed pa yin//chu ni skyod pa tsam gis kyang//
132b1(rgyud): sku ba'i bu ga las ni gtong skyed mos tshal dang ldum ra ru//sa ni mnyam po las 'gro ste//gang du grags pa der 'gro'o//gdug pa 'pham don dung//chos dang ldan rnams rgyal 
132b2(rgyud): don du//rab mchog sa'i steng du ste//srid pa gsum gi bla ma yis//dang po sangs rgyas su sngon gsungs//zla ba bzang la gang thams cad//de'i dbus su 'di cung bzad//yul
132b3(rgyud): 'di ru ni gsal ba nyid//bdag gis gsungs 'dir de bstan pa//rang gi gnas ni srung don du//'di dag thams cad gis shig pa//chags dang zhe sdang la min nyid//chags dang ba tsha'i rgya
132b4(rgyud): mtsho'i dbus//thub pa'i lhan cig 'khor yug la/las kyi sa ni yang dag gnas//stong phrag dpag tshad 'bum phrag gsum du yang//mi'i bdag po 'khor bzhi ni//nyi ma'i dum bur
132b5(rgyud): rim pa yis//dum bu gcig la dpag tshad rnams;nor gi 'dab mar bcas pa la//stong phrag nyi shu rtsa lngar 'gyur;de'i dbus su ti se ni//kha ri'i khyad par gis dang ni//phyogs
132b6(rgyud): kun du ni skor ba yi//ti se'i dum bu sar//kha ba'i ri dang lhan cig pa//kun nas de'i cha gsum la//phyi ru 'dab ma re re'o/nyin mo byed pa'i yul la ni//kling dang yul gis rgyan pa yin
132b7(rgyud): g.yas kyi phyed nas gshams pa yi//thub pa mchog gi gnas yin te//grong ni bya ba lhag par gnas//bye ba'i grong gi khor yug ni//rgyal khams su na 'gyur ba ste//grong 'bum la ni
132b8(rgyud): ljongs su gsungs//gcig gnyis gsum bzhi'i dbye ba dang//lnga dang drug la swogs pa'i gnas//'jig rten lnga la swogs dbye bas//mi dbang 'khor lo can tha dad//se'i steng du 'khor
133a1(rgyud): @#//ba ni;de ni mkhas pas rjes su rtags (྾);dum bu phyed la mkhor lo can//rgyal mkhams la mi bdag po ste//phyag rgya'i phyag can de las gzhan//phyi'i dus kyi dbye bas//ji ltar
133a2(rgyud): 'jig rten 'gon po bzhin//de bzhin du ni nges par 'gyur//nyi shu rtsa brgyad la swogs res//thub pa rnams ni 'dir lhags bzhin//snying stobs can de skye ba bzang//nya dang rus (྾)la 
133a3(rgyud): phag pa dang/mi dang seng ge lhan cig pa//me'u thung dang gshin rje sreg//dga' ba nag po de bzhin brgyad//rjes su 'thun pa'i 'jigs pa ster//rdul gi skye gnas las rab 'byung//
133a4(rgyud): rgyad gnyis sum bcu gnyis kyi mthar//de'i rjes su dgar 'gyur ro//'khor lo'i lag pa rgya'i tshe//ba la phag bsobs rkang bzhi pa//de'i rjes su lag 'gro'i rigs
133a5(rgyud): 'di lta mun pa can yin no//sa mthar srin mo lta bu yi//bud med sbrang rtsi'i blo sbubs ma//brgyad pa gang de man can 'gyur//de ni mun pa las rab 'byung//namakha'i yul
133a6(rgyud): 'khor na rin na//ngag ster laswogs grong khyer du//gang gi 'jig rten lha min rigs//stobs dang ldan pa nges par gnas//bla klo'i che phyed rtse med pa//rnga mo rta dang ba glad gsad//
133a7(rgyud): tshil dang zhag dang khrag dang sha//bcung zad bcos pa 'ba' zhig dang//ba la sha dang gcin dang chu//mar dang tsha ba gsum sras zhing//'bras dang rngad du sres pa ni//gcig tu me la btsos pa

133b1(rgyud): 'dab bzangs lha dang khyab 'jug dang//mi ster dang 'dod 'jo dang//dgrod dka' rig pa zad rig pa//mi 'gyur dam pa yi ni cha//bzang por snang ba yi ni lus//'di rnams rgyu'i gzugs kyis ni//sa'i

133b8(rgyud): snyigs ma can 'byung 'gyur//dul ba rnams la zhi ba'i gzugs//bde ba ster ni de bzhin du//bla klo'i rigs rnams mthar byed de//rdor rtar bcing ba'i 'khor lo can//nyi ma'i gzi la phyag na 
134a1(rgyud): @#//mdungdrang srong nus pa snun par byed//snyigs ma can rigs kyi dbus su ni//lag pas sgres pa yis ni dus//bu dang tsha'o 'das pa yi//de'i dbus su nges 'gyur ba//
134a2(rgyud): mkha'i yul du nges 'gyur ba//bla klo'i chos ni 'byung bar 'gyur//ji srid rdol pa'i dbang du gnas//lha'i mchog gis phyag byas pa//de srid drag po'i snyigs ma can//de'i dbus su de nyid 
134a3(rgyud): gnyis ka//sa'i steng gi gnas su ni//drag po'i rtsod pa 'gyed par 'gyur//sa'i steng ni mtha' dag tu//rtsod pa'i kla klo snun nas ni//'khor dang bcas pa rang gi dpung//dus mthar ti 
134a4(rgyud): se laswogs pa//lhas spras pa'i pho brang du//'khor los sgyur ba 'ong bar 'gyur//dbang phyug tshangs pa tshogs kyi bdag//'khyab 'jug kyang ni grags su 'gyur//de rnaMs

134b1(rgyud): mtshan cha rnon po snun par gyur//drag po rdol pa'i rgyal po 'joms//de mtha' mang dag rgyal po ni//drag po'i snyigs can blo yis byis//rtsod pas kla klo'i sman snun nas//khyab 'jug dbang

134b5(rgyud): tshangs pa'i lha'i bdag//sum bcu pa'i ma'i bla ma//bzung gi chos las 'byung bar 'gyur//tshangs pa dum bu gcig gi stengs//mi'i bdag por der 'gyur ro//g.yas 
134b6(rgyud): kyi sa ru lha'i bdag//rdzogs pa g.yas su kla kli'i chos//srid pa gsum gi bla ma yis//ma lus pa ni bcad nas su//lo ni brgyad rgya rnams su gnas//bde ba'i go 'phang du ni 'gyur/
134b7(rgyud): bram ze ru ni 'jog par 'gyur//gnas pa 'di'i dbus su yang//mi'i bdag por ru 'gyur te//mi rnams rigs kyi dbye ba phye//de rnams dbus su lha'i lha//mi'i bdag po thub pa rnams//
134b8(rgyud): tha mal pa ni gzhan du 'gyur//kla klo rnams ni gzhom don du//lha'i bdag pos 'gro 'gyur te//bcu gnyis dbang po mngon par brtson//dum bu dum bur 'khor lo can//bde ba'i go 'phang 
135a1(rgyud): @#//du ni 'gyur;kla klo'i chos ni nges bcom nas//sum bcu ma'i bla ma ni//tshangs pa mi'i bdag po sras//nub de bzhin du shar du'o//rgyab tu tshangs pa dang po'i lhor//rnam pa sna

145a5(rgyud): 'phel zhing lo//lo rdzogs pa ni nyid drug 'gyur!de bzhin du ni gzhan yang 'di//'chi la thug par 'phel ba'o//de bzhin rgyal po nyi ma yi//nyis 'gyur kyi ni bu lon 'di/

145b1(rgyud): zla ba rang gi rgyu dang ni//nyi ma dang ni rgyu skar rnams//thams cad gnas ba rgyu ba min//lo'i dbus su rim pa bzhin//lo ni dang po'i bu lon gyis//gnas te za ba ma yin te//dus kyi 'khor lo

145b4(rgyud): lag pa bzung ba yi//de'i ring bzhin snang ba dang;lag pa gnyi ga'i 'thil la yang//rgya skyegs byugs 'dra dmar ba nyid!dmar po snang ba nyid med dang//nyi ma kha dog nag por snang
145b5(rgyud): zla ba 'gyur te rdzogs pa ni//kha dog ser por snang ba dang//rang lus ci ba drang ba ru//'gyur ba mi'i bdag po kye//de'i nyi ma la ni 'chi ba nyid//lce 'og ri mo nag po dang
145b6(rgyud): mig tu tshangs pa'i ri mo ni//shin tu phra ba snang nyid dang//zla ba nyi ma'i lam du dbus//mi'i bdag po 'phar ba dang//'khur ba gnyis su de bzhin ni//mchan khung nu ma'i steng gzir dang//bza'i
145b7(rgyud): tshogs ni ma lus pa//me stag bcas par mthong ba dang//skye dang yan lag bcas pa rengs//sus ni 'gyur bar mi shes pa//brgyad ni 'chi ba'i mtshan nyid do//bskyed pa ru ni gang byed pa//skyes

146b2(rgyud): 'gyur ba med pa'o//dpal gi cha dang thig le sgra//lus dang sems dang blo dang ni//rang bzhin yang ni de bzhin te//gzhan ni dbyangs dang rlung laswogs//rags pa'i sde tshan yi ge rnams//bcu ni

146b5(rgyud): pa po dang las//gzhan yang phyug pa 'i ni'o//byed pa po dang las ni yod//rang gi byed pa yang min gzhan la yang//rang bzhin dang ni rnam par bral//shes bya de'i las ni byed;
146b6(rgyud): de phyir byed pa 'ga' yang med//ster ba dang ni 'phrog pa'ang med//srog chags rnams kyi bde dang sdug//'khor ba kun du sngon gi las//'bras bu ster bar rab 'gyur ba//rnam pa gsum ni byang byas

146b8(rgyud): par 'gyur//'dod pa'i nus pa byas pa yis//'gro ba ru ni rang bzhin pa//ye shes nus pa gsum pa ste//dngos por snang ba 'jug pa dang//ro ni mnyam par byed pa yi//don ni dmigs par

147a3(rgyud): bdag po//bum pa nang gi nam mkha' ni//'gro yang 'gro bar mi 'gyur te//stong pa gcig du chu ni khyer bzhin du yang min pa//nam mkha' khyab pa ji bzhin du//rdo rje can yul rnam bral ba
