182a6(rgyud): gyur pa yis//bu bum losags brgyad pa'o//lag pa g.yon par rab rdzogs pa//ri bo 'dzin pa dri med pa'og.yas la rdo rje nyi ma'o//nyi lnga kha sbyor la gnas ba//'khro bo las skyes
182a7(rgyud): ra lnga pa rigs kyi gtso bo 'jigs pa yi//sgo nas lus la rig par bya//gcod pan dang ni spyi bo nas//brtsams te rkang pa la thug gi bar du//dpal gi phyag rgya bzang po 'di//srid pa dri ma sel byed pa//
182a8(rgyud): rgyal po dus kyi 'khor lo yi//gang ci yang rung ba'i bzung dngos po//sa la skyes dang chu la skyes//dpal las skyes dang gsher la swogs//bza' dang btung dang sa bon bcas//bla ma yi ni zhabs la yang//
182b1(rgyud): phyag rgya yis ni rig par bya//bya ba gang dang gang dgos pa//yon tan gis ni dbang gis 'gyur//de la de ni sbyar bar bya//dkyil 'khor du ni sa la gsogs//rkang pa'i 'gro ba dag la yang//rnal 'byor pa yis
182b2(rgyud): snun par bya//lag pa dang ni rdo rje bcing//'khro bo chen po dgon pa yi//phyag rgya nges par 'gyur ba yin//'dzub mo gnyis dang mtha' ma bcing//srid pa gsum las rgyal ba//gtso ba'i khu tshur bcings pa'o//mtshan
182b3(rgyud): ma'i rnam par lhag ma'i rnams//nges par kho nar gsal ba'o//lha dang lha mo rnams kyi 'o//lag pa dag ni rdo rjer bcing//gdeng can rnam par 'khril ba yi//phyag rgya gdengs can rnams kyi'o
182b4(rgyud): mthe bo 'the'u dag chu dag sbyarate//pad ma'i 'dab ma lta bur bya//gong mo dag ni rkang ba dang//'dzub mo gnyis ni zla ba phyed par 'dug//rang gi lag pa'i 'thil son par//mig med
182b5(rgyud): de dag ni nang du dgug//phyag rgya 'di ni rtse lnga pa//rigs kyi rtse bor 'di 'gyurate//rdo rje can gis stan par bya!'dzumo gnyis ni ra ltar bya//gnyis ka'i lag pa'i 'thil du ni//
182b6(rgyud): 'the bo laswogs mtha' dag dgug///lag pa gnyis ni rtse 'dzir bcing//rigs kyi gtso bo ru 'gyur te;rdo rje khu tshur dril bur bcas//steng du khu tshur ral gri rno//'dzub mo dang ni gong
182b7(rgyud): mo dag//mda' ltar mnyam par 'gyur ba'o//khu tshur steng du gsum pa'o//'dzub mo laswogs gsum pa ni//yar yang ni ma 'char ba//dbal gi dri khug khu tshur bcing//dbal gi mthe'u chung zla
182b8(rgyud): phyed pa//'jigs pa byed pa ru ni 'gyur//rna ba'i steng du khu tshur bcing//mda' phyag rgya ru ni 'gyur//mthe bo gong mo'i steng du sbyar//'dzub mo shin du skyog par ni//'gyur ro mi'i bdag po kye//
183a1(rgyud): @#//de bzhin lcags kyu'i khu tshur bcing//rnam par 'dzub mo'i srin lag bkug//dbu ma ltar mda' ltar brgyang ba ni//steng du 'char ba bdud kyi rgya//khu tshur bsags rkyang dbyugs pa'i rgya//
183a2(rgyud): shin du mnyam pa'i lag pa las//mthe bo rkyang pa ste re'o//khu tshur gnyis kyi steng du 'gyur//lha min bdag po blang chen pags//khu tshur steng du 'dzub mo 'char//'che ba'i khu tshur bcing ba ni//lag 
183a3(rgyud): pa gnyis ka'i 'thil la ni//mthe bu chung zla ba phyed par byed//g.yon du lag pa rkyang ba'i//lag pa yi ni mthil 'gyur ba//steng du song ba phub kyi rgya//kha traM khu tshur stong pa yi//'the 'u chung phrag pa rkyang
183a4(rgyud): pa ru//nges pa kho nar 'gyur ba'o//lag pa'i sor mo phyed skyongs pa//pad ma yi ni 'dab lta bu//dpal gi thod par byas pa'o//g.yon pa'i dpung pa rgyas nas su//lag pa'i gan rkyal khu tshur ni/
183a5(rgyud): gzhu'i phyag rgya nyid du 'gyur//'dzub mo bkug pa gong mo la//zhon pa rdo rje zhags pa ru//nges pa ru ni 'gyur ba yin//rin chen sor mo gnyis gnyis kyi//mi bdag gcig ni rkyang
183a6(rgyud): ba'o//sor mo rnams ni rang 'dab ma'o//'dzub mo dang ni mthe bong sbyar//'the bo 'og tu khu tshur te//chu la spyod pa'i rgyu ru 'gyur//ma long gi kha chogs pa'i//shin du mnyam
183a7(rgyud): pa'i lag pa'i 'thil//sor shibs bu ga med pa'o//'the bo la ni 'dzub mo rtsegs//steng du rim pa bzhin bkug pa//yongs bkod lug gu rgyud kyi'o//tshangs pa'i 'gon po'i mthe bong
183a8(rgyud): bswogs//bzhi ni mnyam par phyogs pa dang//mthe 'u chung 'og tu skums pa'o//dung gi phyag rgya'i lag pa dag//'dzub mo gnyis dag sbyar ba ni//gcod pan gi ni phyag rgyar 'gyur//sor
183b1(rgyud): mo lnga ni gcig sbyar zhing//gzhan yang lag pa'i 'thil gi ni//sor mo brgyad glan rna cha'i rgya//lag pa'i rgyab du sor mo rnams//rim pa bzhin du sbrel nas ni//bcings pa 'gul pa yi ni rgya//sor 
183b2(rgyud): mo gsum ni phan phun tshun sbyar//lag pa gnyi ga'i thog mtha' dag//skums pa dag ni ska rags kyi//mthe bo dag dang gong mo dag//zlum po lta bur byas pa yi//khu tshur bcings pa brkyang rdub gi
183b3(rgyud): de bzhin du ni dbang brgyan bzung/gdub bu ru ni 'gyur ba yi//'dzub mo gnyis kyi sbyor ba'o//mthe bo mkha' 'gro ma rnams kyi//rigs mchog gis ni 'gyur ba yin//'dzub mo gsang ba rnams kyi ni//
183b4(rgyud): dri za gdengs can rnams kyi ni//rim par yongs su bstan pa bzhin//dbu ma dang ni ming med do//'byung po rnams ni 'the'u chung//rab 'chog lag pa yi ni 'thil//srin mo rnams kyi y+ni
183b5(rgyud): rigs mchog grub pa rnams kyi ni//khu tshur bcings pa ru ni 'gyur//lha rnams kyi lag pa'i tshigs//lag pa gnye ga'i thal mo'i//sor mo lnga ni steng bkug pa//sder bcas rnams 
183b6(rgyud): kyi rigs kyi rgya//'dzub mo dag ni steng bkug pa//spyi bo ru ni song ba nyid//ra can rnams kyi phyag rgya'o//lag pa dag gi rgyab tu sbyar te//'the chung phan tshun bcings pa yi//gshog pa sbyor 
183b7(rgyud): ba sgong skyes kyi//sor mo lnga ni rtse bkug pa//gdengs can rnams kyi ni//phyag rgya khyad par can du 'gyur//nyi ma so so'i gtor ma la//'dzub mo'i mtha' ma rnam brkyang zhing//gzhan ma dag ni 'og
183b8(rgyud): tu btud//mthe bo sbyar ba 'bar ba'i rgya//mchog gi lag pa'i 'thil las ni//'dzub mo brkyang pa gzhan ma'o//'dzub mo ston par byed pa ni//rnal 'byor ma ni legs 'ongs par//smras par yang ni 
184a1(rgyud): @#//'gyur ba yin//gnyis kyis shin du legs 'ongs pa//mthe bo bcings pas snyan mi mnga'//shin du skal bzangs rab smras yin//sor mo se gol rtogs pa ni//'dir ni sngags la yon 
184a2(rgyud): phul 'byung//zhes nas des par smra ba yin//'the bo ming med dag gis ni//dam tshig can dang rab chags pa//des ni nyong bar ston pa yin//sor mo thams cad brkyang ba ni//rnal 'byor ma ni legs
184a3(rgyud): 'ongs par//bskal bzang ma ni smra bar ston//g.yon gi yan lag rig pa yis//rtag tu rab gsal bar nyid du//bdag gi gnyen na khyod cig bu//skye gnas reg par khyod ston cig//chu dang nu ma 
184a4(rgyud): bzung la yang//sen mo yis ni 'bri ba'o//sor mo phan tshun bcings pa las//gong mo 'the bong rkyang ba ni//dam tshig dag ni ston par smra//slu ba kha dang mchu'i ston//spyi bo phrug par byed pa 
184a5(rgyud): ni//shin du rmongs par smra ba yin//mche ba'i dbus su the chung snun//snying ka kha ru 'dzub mo 'dzud//shin du 'jigs par gsal ba yin//mthe bo khu tshur bcings pa yi//lag pa 
184a6(rgyud): rkang pa'i sor mo rnams//bdebs pa za bar byed pa'o//lce la reg na bza' 'don pa'o//lto ba dang ni so dag la//snan pa nyid ni zos pa'o//lag pa'i rgyab kyis song shig par//nges par
184a7(rgyud): rab tu smra ba yin//'dun stan pa ni 'dun shig pa'o//yan lag thams cad reg bzhin pa'i//lag pas kha ni 'gebs pa'i//bdag dang 'dus pa med par ston//lag pa phan tshun bcings pa yi//bdag gi
184a8(rgyud): khyim na de ring ni//'khor lo 'dus par smra ba'o//mtho bong srin lag rtse sbyar ba//rnam pa sna tshogs lhag ma'o//dam tshig la ni myang bar bya//rkang pa la ni khrag bzhin pa//phyi rol du ni 'dus pa la//de bzhin
184b1(rgyud): du ni 'gro ba'i//'dzub mo phan tshun 'ching ba'i//'jigs pa dag ni med pa ste//de ring khyod ni rdo rje grogs//rang gi so yis skra gcod na//'di ru mi'i phyugs khyod ni//bsad par bya ba smra ba yin//
184b2(rgyud): phan tshun so ni 'dud pa yi//khyod kyi sha ni 'di nyid ni//de ring bdag gis bza' bar bya//lce ni mchu la dril ba nyid//khyod kyi lus kyi khrag rnams ni//btung ba bya zhes smras pa yin//mchu la so ni 'dud
184b3(rgyud): bzhin pa//khyod kyi lto bar chud pa ni//'di ru de ni bza' bar bya//sgyeg mo sbyor bas sgyeg mo ru//'gyur ro mi'i bdag po kye//gzhan pa'i sbyor bas gzhan pa ste//gar gi sbyor ba yis ni gar
184b4(rgyud): rol mo rnam pa mang po yi//sbyor ba yi ni rol mo'o//klu'i sbyor ba yis ni ku//mchog tu yon tan sna tshogs pa'i|dri'i sbyor bas drir 'gyur te//phreng ba'i sbyor bas phreng ba ru//
184b5(rgyud): yon tan gi ni dbang gis 'gyur//sbyos kyi sbyor ba yis ni sbos//mar me'i rnam pas mar me ni//mun pa ma lus 'joms pa'o//snod kyi sbyor ba yis ni snod//de ltar phyag rgya thaMs
184b6(rgyud): cad ni//slar la yang ni de 'og tu//lnga'i dbye bas rnam par dbye//phyag rgya dpag tu med pa bzhan//rang gi rigs kyi lus song ba//rnal 'byor pa yis rig par bya//dngos po gang dang gang rang bzhin//
184b7(rgyud): de'i 'og phyag rgya ru//sa stengs su ni 'gyur ba yin//pho nya mo ni bsegs lta na//khyod ni gang nas 'ongs pa yin//skal bzangs ma ni smra bar ston//lag pa spyi bor btang nas ni//de'i phyogasu lta ba ni//
184b8(rgyud): rnal 'byor ba 'is lan smras pa'o//sa'i steng du lta ba ni//khyod kyi 'dug la ngal sos pa'o//yon por lta ba song shig pa'o//rnal 'byor pa ni chags pas lta//mchog gi dga' ba yis smra ba'o//zhi ba'i
185a1(rgyud): @#//ltas na bdag gi grogs//'khro gnyer 'khro bos lta ba ni//'jigs pa ru ni gsal ba yin//sgra ltar ma rungs pa ni nga//rnal 'byor pa ni rang rang bzhin//skal bzangs ma ni sma bar 'gyur//
185a2(rgyud): 'dzod pu lta bu dam pa dam pa dang//rna ru lta na rnal 'byor ma//yon tan du gsal ba'o//mchu la lta na bskal bzang mar//gsal te nu ma bzung ba ni//lta na phyag rgya bzang mo nga//snying kar lta na sgom pa'i
185a3(rgyud): bdag//du 'gyur lag pa bzung na ni//lta ba na dag ni rab tum mo//'phrag par lta na nus can dang//sen mor bcas pa'i lag 'thil dang//lta na srin mo nga nyid do//sa la lta na lag 'gro ma'o//lte bar
185a4(rgyud): lta na mi dbang po//nga'i dam tshig can nyid do//bsang bar lta na nga dag pa//blar lta shin du chags pa la//bdag ni thul bar dka' ba'o//pus mor lta na bdag grub par//des pa ru ni
185a5(rgyud): ston pa yin//rkang par lta na rdzu 'phrul 'gro//rkang pa'i sor mo ru lta na//gnas gsum du ni bdag nyid ni//dpa'o gcig pa rdo rje'i sku//sor mo kun gi rtse la lta//srid pa
185a6(rgyud): gsum gi gnas na ni//kun 'gro sna tshogs gi ma//de ltar pho nya'i lta stangs rnams//sa'i steng gi gnas dag tu//rnal 'byor pa yis rig par bya//lus kyi nang ni cha drug ni//lag 
185a7(rgyud): pa rkang pa dag dang lus//yan lag mchog gi sbyin pa dag//slob dpon la ni rab phul nas//bde ba'i go 'phang thob par 'gyur//phyag rgya bzang mos rjesu phye ba'o//las kyi dbang po rnams las tshig
185a8(rgyud): dbang po rnams kyi nang nus yid//yon tan byas pas dbul bar bya//khams kyi nang nas cha drug pa//rkang gnyis pa dang phyugs kyi tshogs//de kho na'i cha gzhan yang//srid pa rnam pa gsum song ba//
185b1(rgyud): 'jig rten khams kyi sems can gad//ye shes rdo rje lcags kyu yis//kun nas de rnams bkug nas ni//snying rje chen po sngon 'gro bas//dkyil 'khor du dbang skur te//sangs rgyas rdo rje bdud rtsis so//
185b2(rgyud): dri meda'i bong can gi lus//rdo rje can la las rnyed nas//rang gi gnas su btang bar bya//nyon mongs pa dang bral ba rnams//byang chub spyod pa rjes su bzhon//rdzas med pa'i dbang skuro//rgyal ba'i ni gnas
185b3(rgyud): a ba 'du ti pa la ni pa la sbyin//de bzhin du ba laswogs lam//yon tan mtha' dag gi ni rter//bstan pa'i don du lag pa'i rgya//rang gi sems kyi dbang song ba//nyes pa kun las rnam grol ba//gzhan
185b4(rgyud): las sbyin par mi bya ste//rgyal ba dam pa'i snying po yi//yum gi mchod pa ma rtogs par//dbang gi mthar ru bum rnams kyi//'jam zhing lus la bde ster ba//stod gos dang ni gos ni bzung/
185b5(rgyud): rnal 'byor ma dpal rnams la sbyin//gzhan pa la yang de bzhin du//stod gos dang ni ras ni bzung//sgo gnas rnams la sbyin par bya//tshogs kyi rigs ni ma lus la//rang
185b6(rgyud): gi nus pas de bzhin gzhan//mtharu sbyin sreg byes nas ni//rang gi pad ma'i snying kha ru//ye shes sems dpa' gzhug par bya//'jig rten pa'i rang rang gnas//pad ma'i bar gi rdul tshon
185b7(rgyud): ni//chu'o gtsang mar skyur bar bya//so rtsi dang ni dri dang spos//me tog 'bras bu ldan pa dang/ras yug bu mo rnams la ni//byin nas slob dpon bla mas bcas//tshogs kyi rigs ni ma las pa//
185b8(rgyud): ci 'dod par ni tshim par bya//slob ma la ni gnang ba sbyin//rab 'phyogs tshogs kyi rigs rnams ni//rang rang gnas su btang bar bya//dgra dang seng ge glang chen dang//me dang lto 'phye'i bdag/po dang
186a1(rgyud): @#//rkun mas zhags pas bcings pa dang//rgya mtsho 'khrug pa sha za dang//'chi ba'i 'jigs pa byed pa ya//nad dang bdud kyi 'tshe ba dang//dbul po bud med bral ba dang//rgyal po khro bo 'jigs
186a2(rgyud): pa dang//rdo rje thog thon nyams ba rnams//rnal 'byor ma rnams kyi ni zhags//nyi ma re re gang dran pa//de'i 'jigs pa med par 'gyur//dpal chen po'i sangs rgyas las btus pa'i//dpal chen po dus kyi

186a4(rgyud): rab ye shes kyi ni dbang//srid pa yi ni 'jigs 'joms pa rnal bgrod bya bzhi pa thob//rgyal ba dam pa lhan cig pa'i//sna tshogs rje btsun sgrub pa'i thabs//slar la yang dag par ni 
186a5(rgyud): zhu//zla ba bzang po'i tshig gsan nas//rdo rje can gi sgrub thabs kyang//rgyal ba'i bdag pos nyes par gsungs//zla ba 'i lus la bzung gi zhabs//mi'i 'grin pa chu'i bzhi//dpal
186a6(rgyud): gi zhal ni sna tshogs 'dog//phrag pa drug la nyi ma dbung//rgyal ba yi ni phyag pad ma//stong phrag drug dang me'i tshogs//zhabs dag gi ni bdud phrag po//za ba nyi ma byin pa yi//dkyil 'khor la

186a8(rgyud): dang ni//grub pa'i gnas dang dur khrod dang//mtsho'i gnas ni gab pa yis//gang du yid ni dga' ba yi//de ru sgrub pa bya ba ru//'gyur ro mi'i bdag po kye//sngon gsungs gsung ba byas nas ni//
186b1(rgyud): 'jam pa'i nyid kyis lam cha 'am//stan bde ba la 'jug par bya//dang po snying ga zla dbus su//phyogs bcu ru ni sna tshogs pa'i//de kho na'i 'od zer sgom//kha laswogs pa dag byaste//slar la yang ni
186b2(rgyud): nam mkha' ru//'od zer spros pas rgyal ba dgu//mchod pa sna tshogs byas nas su//srid pa'i sdig pa mang po ni//bsags pa de ni bshags par bya//gsum la skyabs su 'gro ba dang/lus dang
186b3(rgyud): ngag dang sems dag pa//sgrub pa po yis bya ba yin//rdzogs sangs rgyas dang byang chub sems//'phags pa'i dge 'dun gang mdzad pa'i//dge ba'i rnam pa mang po ni//ma lus de la rjes yid rang
186b4(rgyud): nyon mongs dang bral ba'i//byang chub spyod pa rjes su gnas//sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun te//srid pa yi ni 'jigs sel ba//byang chub mthar thug rab skyabs mchi//rdzogs sangs rgyas su
186b5(rgyud): bdag gyur cig//zhes pa'i smon lam 'debs pa ni//'dir ni sems can rnams kyi phyir//'gro ba ma lus 'di dag/gi//dngos po'i ngo bo'i rang bzhin no//dngos dang bral bas stong
186b6(rgyud): pa'i//de'i de phyir sangs rgyas byang chub med//gzhan la phan pa'i snying rje dang//mtshan ma med par dam bcas pa'o//de ltar mtha' dag shes bya ste//mi'i bdag po de yang ni//sku 
186b7(rgyud): gsung thugs kyi rdo rje ni//byang chub sems dpa' sgom par bya//dpag tu med pa'i yon tan ldan//dkyil 'khor la ni dkyil 'khor gtso//dbang po 'dir ni sdom brtson gis//chu yis me ni 'jig pa ni//
186b8(rgyud): lus kyi dbus su bya ba yin//phyi nas sa ni chu ru 'gyur//tsha bzhin chu'i dbus su zhugs//rlung gis chu ni skams nas su//nam mkha' ru ni mi snang bar//myur du 'gro ba de nas ni//sems dang me ni mun
187a1(rgyud): @#//pa'i mthar yul dang rnam par bral ba ni//dbus kyi sa ru gzhag par bya//stong pa rlung dang me dang chu//sa dang rgya mtsho ri pad mar//zla ba nyi ma dang ni me//khang pa rtseg ma kun nas ni//
187a2(rgyud): dri med 'od zer 'phro ba yi//rdo rje las ni skyes pa'i gur//de nas rdo rje sa bzhi la//sna tshogs nor bu'i 'od lta bu//dkyil 'khor la ni rnam 'phro ba//oM gi ye shes las skyes pa//rgyal ba dam pa'i dam

187a4(rgyud): shing rta'o//rgyal po nyi ma'i sgo rnams so//nor bu gser gi rang bzhin gi//rta babs rnams dang dud khrod rnams//nang du rgyad gnyis ka ba ni//rigs gtso yi ni rang bzhin te//rgyal

187a6(rgyud): mdzad pa//dus kyi 'khor lo sgom par bya//rdo rje rgyan dang ldan pa'i lus//rgyal ba'i ni phyag pad ma//nyi ma bung ba'i dus kyi zhal 'grin gsum nyi ma yi ni spyan//zhal gdengs mchi ba phyed rtsig+sa

187a8(rgyud): zhabs//mchog gi zhabs kyi 'og tu ni//bdud ni dmar po g.yas kyi ste//drag po dkar po g.yas kyis so//'grin pa dang ni dbus g.yas dang g.yon//bung ba nyi ma zla ba'i mdog//g.yas pa dang ni
187b1(rgyud): g.yon pa yis;phrag pa sngo dang dmar ba dang//ri bong 'dzin pa ltar dka' ba'o//g.yas dang g.yon du gnyis gnyis kyi//dpung ba rnams kyi dkar min dang//nyi ma'i 'dog dang zla ba'i mdog//
187b2(rgyud): de bzhin du ni rgyad 'bum ni//mtshon cha snams dang lhan cig pa//la ga na pa rnams ni rim pa bzhin//sor mo lnga las tshigs gsum gsum//ri bong can lag pad ma la//kha dog lnga ni 'phro ba'o//
187b3(rgyud): nag po dmar po dkar po'o//rab 'chog lag pa'i 'thil la ni//mtshon cha'i tshogs ni yang dag gnas//rdo rje ral gri rtse gsum dang//srid pa gsum ni 'jigs byed pa//gri gug dang ni me'i
187b4(rgyud): mda'//de nas rdo rje lcags kyu nyid//sgra dang bcas bcang de'u//tho ba dang ni 'khor lo nyid//mdung dang g.yug pa dgra sta ni//rdo rje can gi g.yas pa yi//nyi ma'i phyag ni pad ma

188a2(rgyud): rgyal ba'i zhal ni dbye bas so//dbang ldan bden bral 'tshams dang ni//rtso mo can dang rlung dag tu//dung dang chos kyi mgan d+he dang//de bzhin yid bzhin nor bur 'gyur//dpag bsam shing ni rim pa bzhin

189a3(rgyud): nag mo dmar mo dkar po dang//gser ni gzhun ma lta bu ni//shar gi sa laswogs lha mo//rgyal ba'i bdag po'i pad ma yi//du ba laswogs lha mo brgyad//rgyal ba dor bar gyur na ni//
189a4(rgyud): dpal gi 'khor lo snying po dbus//nyi shu rtsa lnga'i bdag nyid du//ma'i bdag por 'gyur ba yin//nus pa rang sems shes bya ste//bcom ldan 'das ni de kho na nyid//rnal 'byor

189b8(rgyud): mchog gnas pa yi//gtso bo pad ma'i 'dab ma la//pad ma lus med gzhon nu ma//ri dgas bdag po la song ba//rin chen 'phreng ba mig bzangs ma//rigs bzangs ma dang bzang mo'o//

191b5(rgyud): ma//sems can rnams ni slu 'dod ma//ku co mang du smra nyid ma//lnga pa brla ma za ba 'dod//rtsod par 'dod dang bdag slu ma//rigs dang 'thun par 'gyur ba ste//bu pho 'phros par
191b6(rgyud): 'dod pa yi//slur 'dod sum bcu rtsa gsum ni//de'i 'og tu slar la yang//dkyil 'khor phyi'i rim pa'o//nyi mi re re'i dbus su ni//sems can bya ba gang ci rung//'dir ni rnal 'byor ma bya'o//

191b8(rgyud): rdo rje//srid pa rnam pa gsum song ba//mi rtag par ni 'gro ba'o//'byung ba'i tshogs ni rtag min par//mig sbrul dag ni ji bzhin no//rtag dang mi rtag par lta ba//de yang bems po'i blo can gis//seMs
192a1(rgyud): @#//ni dag pa'i don kyi phyir//sgrub pa thams cad nyid ni brjod par bya//las las snying du song ba ni//sgrub par nus pa nyid ma yin//rdo rje can gi sgrub pa'i//sgrub pos sgrub par bya ba gang de//
192a2(rgyud): ma lus bar ni 'khrul pa'o//de phyir rgyal po rang gi sems//nyon mongs pa dang bral ba yin//dkyil 'khor nyid ni sgrub par bya//dpal gi rdo rje thugs rdo rje//'gyur ro mi'i bdag po kye//dkyil 'khor

192b3(rgyud): gtsor bcas pa'i gdengs can gi//zhags pa de bzhin bcing//mtshon cha rnams kyis 'jigs bcas te//ma lus par ni sgrub bya ste//slar la yang ni log nas ni//rang gi 'khor lo skyod bya ste//gzhug

192b5(rgyud): mchog gi pad ma'i shar 'dab la//dpal gi nag mo 'bar ba ste//g.yas su dmar mo 'bar ba'o//g.yon du dpal ni dkar mo 'bar ba'o//nub du ser mo 'bar ba ni//rigs kyi

192b8(rgyud): ba dang ni chu//byang du 'du shes ma ru 'gyur//nub tu gzugs ni gzhan nyid do//me dang rlung dang gzugs su 'gyur//bden bral me dang 'du shes gnyis//dbang ldan chu dang tshor ba nyid//rlung du sa dang 'du byed ldan//

198b8(rgyud): gi tshigs//stong pa drug dang me'i grangs//las kyi 'khor lo lha rnams dag//klu dang gtum po bcu rnams dang//sor mo rnams kyi tshigs kyi 'tshams//bya'i 'khor lo dag par 'gyur//
199a1(rgyud): @#//rdo rje can dpal dus dag par//mi'i bdag por 'gyur ba ni//lo dang zla swogs dbye bas so//sems kyi rnam par snang ba med//nyi ma so so'i dbang gi ni//sna tshogs yum ni rnam par dag//
199a2(rgyud): du ba laswogs rlung dag pa//rang gi snying kha'i pad ma'i//rtsa ba 'khor lor gnas pa'o//drag po chung mar bcas mnan pa//nyon mongs pa'i bdud bcom pa'o//khyab bdag thabs kyi mthil gis ni//
199a3(rgyud): bdud gnas bdud tshogs bcom pa'o//dung dang mgan d+he nor bu dang//shing ljon zhes pa de bzhin du//sku'i rdo rje laswogs kyi//bum brgyad lus kyi bdud rtsi brgyad//rgyal ba rnam par rgyal bum
199a4(rgyud): ba;;byang chub sems dang rdul dag dbab//'du byed don yod grub pa ste//tshor ba rin chen 'byung gnas dang !'du shes 'od dpag med pa'o//gzugs ni rnam par snang 'dzad do//
199a5(rgyud): khu ba rkang dang khrag dang ni//dri chu dri chen rnam dag pa'o//khams khrag rnam dag lha mo drug//yul dang yul can rnam dag pa//byang chub sems dpar phyag rgyar bcas//khro bo lnga ni
199a6(rgyud): stobs lnga nyid//gzhan ni dbang po lnga yin no//rgan byad ma swogs brgyad dang 'khor//pad ma'i 'dab mar song ba na//nyi dus chu tshod rnam dag pa'o//bden bral laswogs zla chu gnyis//pad ma'i 'dab mar
199a7(rgyud): song ba rnams//chu tshod kyi ni dbus grangs tshad//klu dang gtum rnams bsang ba yi//mi'i bdag po gnyis 'gyur gi//rnams kyi ni rnas dag pa'o//de bzhin 'dod ma laswogs pa//rang bzhin yon tan
199a8(rgyud): dbang gis ni//lus kyi bye ba rnam dag pa'o//sra rnams grub pa mi lus pa//ba spu rnams ni 'byung po'i tshogs//sa'i steng na gnas rnams so//de nyid rnams ni mtshon cha ma//rang bzhin yon tan dbang
199b1(rgyud): nam gis ni;khams rnams dag pa phyi phyag rgya//rdo rje rnams ni nang phyag rgya//gtso bo yi ni nang snying du//zla stong yang dag gnas pa'o//rdo rje can dpal sna tshogs bum//srid pa rnam pa gsum song 
199b2(rgyud): ba//dus gsum ye shes sbyor lags so//dpa' dag pa las chos kyi sku//chos las longs spyod rdzogs par 'gyur//longaspyod rdzogs las sprul pa'i skur//'gyur te rgyal ba'i bdag por ni//
199b3(rgyud): sems can kun gi don gi phyir//bzhi pa gnas skabs nyal ba dang//slar la yang ni gzhan ma nyid//lus kyi dbye bas sad pa'o//de ltar lus kyi dbye ba yis//srog chags rnams kyi lus la yid//rnam 
199b4(rgyud): par bzhi na gnas pa yin//sprul pa longaspyod rnams la yid//lus dang dbang po'i rjes byed pa'o//longaspyod rdzogs pa'i sems lta ba//rigs kyi dbang gis 'gyur ba yin//chos kyi sku'i gnyid log 
199b5(rgyud): pa//dag pa'i bde bar 'gyur ba ste//'dir ni nyid dang mtshun dbang gis//thig le gsum ni sgrol ba'i mthar//de phyir gi ni mi 'dzag don//nyin dang mtshan gi dus drug ni//rnal 'byor
199b6(rgyud): pa 'is sgom par bya//g.yon du srog ni rgyu 'gyur zhing!de bzhin g.yas su gtul ba gang//de na g.yon brkyang gnas pa'o//mnyam pa'i stangs thabs gzhan g.yas rkyang//me dang nyi ma
199b7(rgyud): las rgyu ba//yangs pa dang ni zlum pa dang//mchog gi rol pa'i ni stobs//rdo rje pad mo yi ni bdan//nam mkha'i la sogs rgyu ba ni//'thun dang mi mthun par song bar//srog gi rlung gi rnam lnga'o
199b8(rgyud): gtum mo lte ba'i 'khor lo la//dgu la lag 'gro'i sa snun pa//rgan byed ma sogs lhag pa'i lha//snying por ho yig ye shes ni//skad cig me ru snang ba ste//ye shes gzi brjid song ba'o//
200a1(rgyud): @#//lte ba la ni rnam snang sogs//sregs shing spyan ma mig laswogs//thams cad sregs nas spyi bor gang//zla ba bzang las 'dzag pa yi//thig le de ni rdo rje lus//shes rab
200a2(rgyud): chos 'byung la gnas pa//slar yang thig le nyid las ni//ma lus pa ni sprog pa nyid//sna tshogs pa'i rgyan dang ldan//me long nang so lta bu yi//ye shes 'khor lo rang 'byung ba//'dod 
200a3(rgyud): pa gzugs dang gzugs med gang//srid pa rnam pa gsum zhes pa//rim pa yis ni sbyangs nas su//phyi nas ye shes mchod pa ni//srid pa gsum ni ma lus pa//lan cig gis ni dgug
200a4(rgyud): par bya//zla nyid a dang ka lasogs//rnam par gsum du dgra 'ong ba//sku'i rdo rje sogs zlas pa//so sor sdud laswogs pa drug//gser ni gtso ma'i 'dab ma la//
200a5(rgyud): sku dang gsung thugs sbyor ba yi//rdo rje pad mar dga' la sogs//ro ni mnyam par 'gyur ba yi//rdo rje gsum ni sgom par bya//shes rab pad mar sems thig le//lhan cig 
200a6(rgyud): skyes pa bde dang gis//mi shegs pa ni sgrom par 'gyur//snyen pa bdud rtsi lnga laswogs//rigs ni bo bzhi yin//sngags kyi zlas pa lasogs pa//so sor sdud pa laswogs pa//rdo rje pad ma las
200a7(rgyud): skyes pa'i//bdud rtsi nye bar grub pa'o//dga' ba lasogs rdo rje gsum//pad mar ro ni mnyam song ba'i//sgom pa sgrub par 'gyur ba'o//shes rab sbyor la mi 'dzag gang//sgrub pa nyen pa nyid
200a8(rgyud): 'gyur te//phra mo 'i ni sbyar bas so//dang po stong pa'i byang chub nyid//de nas thig le zla drus pa//de las sku ni skyed pa nyid//rab 'chog ro'i rigs rnams kyi//yi ge rnams ni dgod pa nyid//

200b2(rgyud): sdud pa rnam pa bcu//rgyal ba yi ni pad mar 'gyur//de bzhin bsam gtan mi skyod pa//srog dang rtsol ba rab dri can//slar la yang ni rnam pa bcu//gzungs pa rin chen phyag rgya can//g.yung
200b3(rgyud): mo rjes su dran nas ni//pad ma 'dzin pa ru ni 'gyur//dpal gi ting 'dzin 'khor lo can//re re yang ni lnga ru 'gyur//slar la yang ni gang dbye ba//a la sogs dang ka la sogs//so sor sdud pa 
200b4(rgyud): yul dang yul dang ni//yul can rnam pa bcu po rnams//lus mi 'jug pa bcu med pa'o//shes rab rtogs dang spyod pa dang//dga' ba mi'i bde ba dang//bsam gtan sems ni gcig pa'o//
200b5(rgyud): srog dang rtsol ba gnyis kyi lam//mi 'grug pa ru 'gyur ba ni//dbu mar srog ni 'jug pa'o//thig le ru ni 'gyu srog'jug cing//gnyi ka'i 'gro ba 'ong ba dang//gzung ba nyid ni sems
200b6(rgyud): gcig pa//gtum mo yi ni snang ba gang//lus la nges par gsal 'gyur ba//nam mkha' der ni rjes dra 'gyur//shes rab thabs kyi bdag nyid kyi//lhan cig skyes pa'i bde gang gis
200b7(rgyud): ye shes sku ni ting 'dzin te//'di ni chung sogs dbye ba yis//gsum tu ni yang ni 'gyur ba yin//sna tshogs bdag pa'i sgrub thabs so//phyag gsum dang tshad ni gsum//rnam pa gsum ni srid dbang gis
200b8(rgyud): las dang kun rtogs bzang mo'o//so sor sdud pas rnal 'byor pa//yul dang rnam par bral ba dang//sngags kun gis ni byin gis brlobs//bsam gtan sbyor ba dag pa yin//mngon shes lnga ni
201a1(rgyud): @#//thob par 'gyur ;srog dang gartsol ba dag pas na//zla ba nyi ma dag dang bral//byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis mchod kyis//gzungs pa yi ni stobs kyi ni//nyon mongs la sogs
201a2(rgyud): bdud 'joms shing//stobs bcu la ni 'jug par 'gyur//rjes dran kyis ni dag par 'gyur//dri med 'od 'phro dkyil 'khor te//gzhan yang ye shes sku lags so//de phyir dag pa'i ting 'dzin g+si

202a1(rgyud): @#//su ni࿒ 'dod pa dngos grub rab tu ster//'dod dang spyod pa lus tsha dang//mchog gi pad ma'i khang pa la//tshig mi 'byung dang gnyid log dang//'jig rten 'dod pa'i gnas skabs 'gul

204b3(rgyud): rnam pa bcu'i ni//bum pa rnams ni mchod par bya//gzugs laswogs pas mnyes par bya//gsal ba ru ni rnam pa ru//skye ga mo la sogs mnyes par byed//'byung po zhes pas mi 'jigs 
204b4(rgyud): byed//rab mchog rnam pa bcu'i ni//khro skyes rnams ni skyong bar byed//klu ma rnams ni 'od 'byed do//mi 'chi ba'i bud med ni//bcu gnyis kyi ni khyud par byed//sa'i steng ni mtha' dag tu
204b5(rgyud): zhi dang rgyas pa'i don gi phyir//gtum mo rnams ni srung bar byed//de ltar sgrub bya ba thams cad//mchog tu sgrub pa byed pa ni//rnal 'byor pa yi ni sgom par bya//zla ba nyi ma
204b6(rgyud): 'i nang du hri//me tog mda' dang me tog bzhu//de nas 'byung nga ba nyid lta bu//gzhi ni mda' yis rnam pa ru//dkar bo yis ni 'jigs byed pa//g.yon brkyang pa yis rta la zhon//
204b7(rgyud): pad ma ri bong can 'dzin pa//sgrub bya ba gnas su btang bar bya//sgrub bya'i snying ka lte ba dang//'grin pa spyi bo bsang ba ru//mda'is skus par bya//sgrub bya 'jigs shing 'khrugs pa 
204b8(rgyud): yang//gzhu yis 'grin par bcings nas ni//dkyil 'khor du ni 'dugs bzhi pa'o//gtum mo rnams kyis gos med par//nges pa ru ni byed pa yin//klu mo rnams kyis zhags pas 'khri//
205a1(rgyud): @#//lha mo rnams kyis nyams par byed//khro skyes rnams kyis rtun shing dang//dbyug par bcas pas snun pa yin//'byung po rnams kyis 'bral ba dang//sgo ma laso gas rnaMs
205a2(rgyud): kyis ni//sor mo rnon pos nges pas 'dabes//rdo rje ma rnams kyis blo rlog//sa dang chu dang byis za dang//rlung las skyes pa rnams kyis bcing//bdag pos gos med zhabs kyis gnon//nus
205a3(rgyud): pa rnams kyis sgyel nas ni//mtha' dag gis ni dregs pa 'joms//de ltar dbang du byas nas ni//slar la yid mi khyab bdag nyid//der ni sgrub bya ma nyes par bya//de bzhan zhags pa lcags kyu
205a4(rgyud): yis//khams gsum dgug pa 'i ni las//rnam pa med pa ru ni 'gyur//nyi ma zla ba'i dbus sgoms nas//char srin nag po lta bu'o//hU~M rigs las skyes ral gri'o//de las skyes pas mdog nag po
205a5(rgyud): phyag na ral dri pad ma dang//sdigs 'dzub zhags pa phyag na'o//rnga mo'i steng na g.yon brkyang gnas//rab tu 'gros pa'i ni zhal//sgrub bya'i gnas su gtang bar bya//khros pa'i ni zhal gis ni//sgrub
205a6(rgyud): bya zhags pas bcings nas su//dkyil 'khor kyi ni sgo ru dgug//ya la skyes pa'i rnga mo ni//rlung gi dkyil tu song ba//de la rang bzhin gis gnas pa//sgrub bya bdag po'i ni gnas//me dang rlung gis 'khor
205a7(rgyud): yul las//thug gi bar du gtong ba nyid//de ltar lha'i bdag po yang//skrod pa ru ni 'gyur na'o//'phran tshegs mi lta smos ci dgos//dbye la rnga mo dang bral ba//rtsod pa rnam pa mang byed pa//g.yas dang g.yon
205a8(rgyud): pa dag gis khrid//nyi ma zla ba'i dbus sgoms pa//las kyi yengs su 'gyur ba ni//'khor lo bzang po ser po ste//de las 'byung ba zhal gcig pa//gser ni gtso ma lta bu'i//phyag na lug gu rgyud 'khor lo
205b1(rgyud): rus sbal lha min gdan gnas pa//shin du dal bar 'gro ba ni//sgrub pa'i gnas su btang bar bya//sgrub bya'i 'khor lo 'is ni dbye//sa la rab tu ltung bar 'gyur//rkang pa lug gu brgyud kyis bcing 
205b2(rgyud): dkyil 'khor du ni bkug pa nyid//rgyal ba dag pa yi ni kas//sa la ltung ba byas pa la//lhun po de'i steng du lta//gser las btso ma'i rang bzhin gis//sgrub par bya ba'i lus ni mnan//
205b3(rgyud): tshigs drag phur bus gdab don du//yang ni rdo rje'i rang bzhin gis//phur bu rnams kyis phur bdag bya//sbrul rnams kun du bstan bzhin pas//'di ni sa la ltung ba nyid//rmongs
205b4(rgyud): pa ru ni sgom par bya//nyi ma zla ba'i nang sgoms pa//skad cig dri ma med pa 'i//d+h+raM gi rang bzhin dri gug go//de las byung ba rab gtum mo//'jigs pa 'i ni sprin lta bu//dri gug dang ni
205b5(rgyud): thod pa'i phyag//g.yon rkyang gos dang bral ba ni//rta'i dga'i steng gnas pa//sgrub bya'i gnas su gtang nas su//sgrub bya'i dag rnams las myur bar//gzung nas kyang ni de
205b6(rgyud): yis ni dkyil 'khor du ni gos bral ba//dpal gi dur khrod du ni bkug bcing ba//rgyal ba bdag po yis ni kas//bya rog bya dgod lce lcang gis//yan lag kun la khrag 'thung
205b7(rgyud): zhing //sha za ba yang de bzhin no//khams rnams thams cad zos pa dang//de ltar sgrub bya ma lus par//rab mchog sa'i steng du ni//gsad pa ru ni sgom par bya//bsam gtan
205b8(rgyud): 'di yis rgya byin yang//gshin rje 'i ni gnas su 'gron//mngal skyes rnams la smos ci dgos//zhi dang rgyas la dkar ba ru//rigs kyi dbang gis 'gyur ba ste//bsam rtan
206a1(rgyud): @#//yang ni chu sa bon//dbang dang dgug pa la dmar po//lus ni sreg par byed pa na//me'i sa bon bdag nyid do//dbye ba dang ni skrag pa la//'jigs pa yi ni sprin lta bu//rang 
206a2(rgyud): gi sa bon rang bzhin ni//rengs dang pur bad ba la sogs pa//gser ni btso ma lta bu ste//sa'i sa bon bdag nyid do//stong pa'i sa bon las sngor stang//ljang khu'i ni 'gyur ba ni//
206a3(rgyud): gsad pa 'i la'o chu'i steng du 'gro ba na//kun nas sa'i zad pa ste//tshar gcod don du bzad par rlung//me'i bzad par bsam pa ni//sa'i dkyil 'khor zhu don du//chu'i zad
206a4(rgyud): par bsam par bsam par bya//nam mya nga' ru ni 'gro ba la//stong pa'i zad par mi snang ba'o//klu'i ngo bo bdag pos 'jigs 'gyur na//sa'i sa bon gnyis las ni//gdong lnga
206a5(rgyud): pa yi bsam gtan bya//glang po'i dbang po de 'jigs la//chu'i sa'i bon de nyid las//glang po dkar po ru ni 'gyur//gser du seng ge las rkang brgyad//'jigs pI ni sprin lta bu
206a6(rgyud): rlung gi sa bon bdag nyid 'gyur//rta'i drag la gse ru 'byung//sa'i rigs kyi dbang gis ste//lha min 'jigs la khro skyes so//dang po ru ni khyab bdag gi//dpal gi sngags dang 
206a7(rgyud): sku dag ni//rnal 'byor pa yis sgrub par bya//phyi nas las rnams sgrub par bya//dpag tu med pa'i las rnams ni//las kyis dbye bas gnas par bya//sngags dang sku ni ma grub ni//
206a8(rgyud): srid pa'i ni gnas su ni//cung zad kyang ni 'grub pa med//de phyir rgyal po rang gi sems//nyon mongs pa dang bral ba yis//sngags dang sku ni 'grus par bya//dpa'o g.yul gi 
206b1(rgyud): sa ru ni;gsad par 'gyur ba 'am de bzhin du//kun ma dbyangs pa nyid kyang rung//brgyad dam 'byung bo'i mtshan mor ni//mi dbang ro sregs kyi gnasu//bum pa rgyad gis 'gra bar
206b2(rgyud): bya//dri dang sbos dang mar me dang//rnam pa sna tshogs bza' ba dang//me tog dmar pos rab tu mchod//spyi bo dang ni snying khar yang//lte ba 'grin pa gsang ba ru//rdo rje bkod pa
206b3(rgyud): rab tu bya//thab khung gru gsum byas nas su//gang yang rdul mtshon dmar po yis//nang du pad ma mtshan mar bcas//'dab ma'i phyogs dang mtshan ma ru//rgyal ba rnams kyi mtshan
206b4(rgyud): ma dang//lha mo rnams kyi rim bzhin te//phyir ru (bar) mi gsum nyid dang//phyogs bcu yi ni khor yug tu//khro bo'i ma ni de bzhin no//de'i g.yon du ri dgas te//dkyil 'khor g.yas su
206b5(rgyud): rkang pa'o//lag pa'i pad ma ral gri 'dzin//gasngon gsungs ma mo'i nyes pa las//rgyal ba'i bdag po'i rigs rnams kyis//bdag nyid srung bar rab tu bya//sngags pas thab khung dbus
206b6(rgyud): ni//sha dang khrag dang bcas pa yis//sbyin sreg nyid ni rab tu bya//oM hri hri hUM phaT kyi mtha'//brgya ni bcu ru bsgres pa yi//de'i sngags kyis sbyin sreg bya//rdo rje dkyil bkrung bcas
206b7(rgyud): pa na//mi mchi ba'i rang bzhin du//mi g.yo ba'i mtshan phyed na//sbyin sreg rdzogs nas 'bar ba nyid//'khro ni 'gril bar byed pa bzhin//mche ba rnon po dmigs gsum pa//'phral
206b8(rgyud): du sgra ni bsal bar sgrogs//sa la yang ni rkang snun pa//sgrub pa so ni 'jigs byed de//thab khung gis ni rab gnas pa//ha ha zhes ni bzhad pa'i//'jigs pa lus ni gar byed pa//de mthong
207a1(rgyud): @#//sngags pa 'jigs na ni//skad cig gis ni sems nyams pa//gshin rje'i ni gnas su 'gro//sngags pas dngos grub rgyu ru ni//ro sregs pa'i gnas su ni//lha min
207a2(rgyud): dbang la 'jigs ma bya//'jigs pa med pa'i sems kyis lta//sgrub pa'i 'byung po 'khan po ni//slar la 'di ni smras par 'gyur//grub pa bdag ste dpa' bo khyod//ma 
207a3(rgyud): lus pa ni bdag la smros//da lta bdag gi ci bzhes pa//de ni smras nas sgrub pos//rang gi yid la gang 'dod//gzhan gi don du gsol gdab//reg pa ral dri ro dbang po//bdud
207a4(rgyud): rtsi'i 'bras bu ri lu dang//'gi wang dang ni mig sman dang//rkang pa byugs dang 'thil lam rnams//'jig rten pa'i dngos grub brgyad//sgrub pa bdag la stsal du gsol//dbye ba dang ni skrad
207a5(rgyud): pa dang//mingon spyod dang ni gsad pa dang//rengs dang dgug pa dbang dag dang//thams cad zhi byed dngos grub ni//'di dag thams cad bdag gis bgyi//de ni slar la yang
207a6(rgyud): smra bar 'gyur//'byung po'i bdag po sgrubs nas ni//sgrub pa po ni gar dga' ba//der ni mi bdag 'gro bar 'gyur//sa 'og dang ni bang snang dang//lha mchog gi ni gnas su 
207a7(rgyud): 'am//rgya mtsho'i pha rol lhun po'i steng//de ru hu po seng ge zhon//sa'i steng gi gnas su yang//'jig rten bya ba dag ni byed//de phyir sems can don gi phyir//dam pa yi 
207a8(rgyud): ni snying rje yis//lha min dbang po sgrub par bya//ming gi dang po thugs rje yis//'gyur ro mi'i dag po kye//lha dang lha mo rnams kyi ni//ming gi ye ge thams cad ni//gsung gi 
207b1(rgyud): rdo rje ru ni 'gyur;de'i phyir nyin lag gis sngags//rnam pa mang bor 'gyur ba ni//res 'ga' klags pas sgrub pa 'o ni//sgo dang sngags kyi las bya ba//rgyal ba nyid kyis rigs dbang gis
207b2(rgyud): thugs gsung sku'i dbye ba'o//so so'i sngags la skyes pa'i//sems pa ru ni 'gyur ba yi//res 'ga' zlas pa bya ba ru ni grags//de'i bcu cha sbyin sreg go//rang bzhin yon tan dbang
207b3(rgyud): gis ni;;sgrub pa ji srid pa nyid do//phyi nas zhi ba laswogs kyis//'bras bu stor ba ru ni 'gyur//gzhan du dngos grub med pa yin//bsam gtan dang bcas zlas sbyin sreg//rtag 
207b4(rgyud): zhugs nges par dang ldan pas//sngags kyi skye gnas sgrub par bya//grub nas lcags kyu gsum gis gtums pa//'khro'i sha'i ri lu yis//mkha' la spyod pa nyid ster 'gyur//kyi dang rta 
207b5(rgyud): bsogs med med yis//lus kyis ltos dang skom lags//nad rnams sel bu ru ni 'gyur//de rnams mig dang 'gris pa yis//mi'i sa'i phyogs su yang//mig sman gi ni mchog tu 'gyur//
207b6(rgyud): mi snang ba dang dbang dag dang//las mchog ma'i yid ni 'phrog//dpal gi dur khrod ni sgrubs pas//nag po'i rgyad pa'i mtshan ma la//yang ni nyid kyi nyid zhag la//dkyil 'khor bri
207b7(rgyud): ba ru ni bya//kun nas 'brang bar byas na ni//shar dag gi ni ri lus 'am//mig sman de'i dbus su gzhan//shin du dri zha mi me tog gis//yang dag par ni mchod byas nas//sngags kyi 
207b8(rgyud): zlas par rab tu bya//ji srid 'od zer 'phro ba'i bar//nam mkha' la ni nyi ma bzhin//de nas blangs te gzung bar bya//srog bcas de'i zla zad cing//nam mkha'i ni phyogs song na//zin 
208a1(rgyud): @#//ba mchi ba ru ni 'gyur//gang yang pho nya 'bra mi mthun//gang mthun mi mchi ster gsung ste//bde mthong mang po'i 'bras ster ba//pho nya sgrub la swogs pa'i ming//de che smra ba bde che 
208a2(rgyud): rab tu smra 'gyur ni//zin bar mchis ba thob par 'gyur//gal te srog ni 'jug pa na//'di ni dge ba ru ni 'gyur//'byung la mi dge bar 'gyur ro//pho nya rkang yon 'dun sngon cing lang btso 
208a3(rgyud): ma'i g.yas 'dun du//skyes pa bud med g.yas g.yon du 'thun//rang gis lus la lag pa yis//gang la reg pa'i yid skyes te//phas phyogs pho nya 'dir ni zin smra zhing//mig
208a4(rgyud): ni rab tu rgyas pa yis//kha yis 'chis bar smra ba dang rna ba'i dug du nag po'i sgra//pad ma dkar po de dag ni//gal te bcung zad smra 'gyur na//dang po srung ba'i cho ga yis//zin ba yi 
208a5(rgyud): ni bdag nyid ni//bde ba ru ni 'gyur ba yin//sa dang chu dang me dang rlung//nam mkha' yang ni de bzhin te//mthe bo la sogs pa la sbyar//dang po la gnas ha'i mtha'//rim gis rla dang 
208a6(rgyud): lto ba dang//snying ka kha dang dbral dbus su//hU~M ha h+ya h+ya la sogs pa klu//phyogs dang 'tshams su song ba ni//thung dang ring po'i rab dbye bas//g.yon lus sa bon thung ngu ste//rna ba 'grin pa dag
208a7(rgyud): song dang//'chan khyad bla blo'i sa phyogs su//lus kyi g.yas su ring ba nyid//gdengs can gyi ni 'gyur ba nyid//spro dang sdu ba'i sbyor ba yis//rgyal ba gsungs pa srung bya ste//sor mo shin
208a8(rgyud): du mnyam bshims pa//phye ba ma yin rkyang ma min//hU~M dang k+sha mo ldan pa yis//snying gar myur du nges snun na//zin pa'i 'khrug pa ru ni gyur//pral bar sa ni sa bon te//mkha' la skyes pa rkang 
208b1(rgyud): par gnas//myur du nges pa ru na 'gyur//spyi bo ru ni chung dgod do//lte ba ru ni rtse mo can//dug ni med par kho nar 'gyur//dbral ba rlung gi sa bon nyid//snying kha ru ni rtse mo can//dug na 'pho ba

208b3(rgyud): par byed pa yin//dmar por 'khrugs cing char srin ni//nag po lta bus 'jug pa yi//kha dog ser pos rigs par byed//srog rgyu ba la tsha ba gsum//btags te ma de'i gur byas
208b4(rgyud): pa ni//sbyar bas dug ni med par 'gyur//kha ni legs par phye nas su//lce'u chung ba bu la so sor ma de yis//tshe lo nyid ni sbyar bar byed//rdo rje can dang sa bon dang //gzhon nu ma
208b5(rgyud): dang tsha ba gsum//lan tsha klu 'jug lha 'dab can//tshangs pa thal ba mu zi dang //nyin mo byed dang lhan cig pa//mo shams dang ni stobs rgyu ma//dri chen sha dang khu ba khrag//mi mnyam
208b6(rgyud): ba las ru lus ni//mi skyod pa dang btags te bya//'byung po 'am 'byung po'i ni rims//ltos 'gro yi ni dug sten pa//sna ru byin na nges par 'joms//nyi ma la sogs bza' ni bdun/
208b7(rgyud): nyin mo dang ni mtshan dus su//bza' bdun gi ni mthug par//stong pa nyi ma man dbus su//rigs ldan rgyu ba ru ni 'gyur//tshu tshod phyed ni mchi ba'i gzugs//snad pa'i rtse mo mi bya zhing 
208b8(rgyud): dug ni rnam pa gsum dag kyang //de ru bza' bar mi bya'o//de phyir de bzhin zin pa ni//'byung po mi dag ba snyed pas//gshin rje'i ni gnas su 'gro//nyi ma'i phyed dang mtshan mo'i tshad//
209a1(rgyud): //rtag tu gzi ba'i rab dbye bas//stong pa'i nyi ma'i mtshams dbus su /rigs ldan rab tu rgyu ba ni//dus kyi mchi ba'i chu tshod gcig//tho rangs ri rtser nyi ma shar//slar la yang
209a2(rgyud): ni de bzhin du//dus kyi chu tshod la 'chi'o//thun 'tshams 'di ni sgra gcan te//nyin mo dang ni mtshan dus su//yang dag par ni rig par bya//nyi ma la yang mtha' yas spyod//nyin dang mtshan mo'i
209a3(rgyud): nyin dang mtshan mo'i dus dag tu//nyi ma zhag gi dang po'i cha//phyi nas lhag ma klu rnams kyi//bzi' drug gi ni rab dbye bas//tshul ni de lta bur 'gyur ro//mkha' ru mkha' bug mkha' dang mnya

209a6(rgyud): kyi rgyu ni dmangs rigs te//pad ma mthar ni skye bar rigs//srin nag 'od ni sna tshogs mdog//de rnams kyi ni skye ba'i gnas//chu dang me dang sa dang rlung//rlung dang nam mkha'i ni khams//
209a7(rgyud): de bzhin nam mkha' lding gis ni//rkang pa dang ni skyed pa ser//lte ba thug pa kha'i mdog//mgrin pa thug par kha dog dmar//dkral ba smin ma gnyis kyi bar//srin ni nag po lta bu'o//spyi
209a8(rgyud): gtsug thug par sna tshogs mdog//gdengs can rigs dang ldan pa'i//de nyid lnga yin lnga yis rgyas ni gdab//de'i phyag rgya stoms nas ni//gdengs can dug ni 'brog pa dang//'byung ba'i
209b1(rgyud): nad ni legs par sel//bya'i 'gron po'i snying ka yi//pad ma yi ni nyi steng du//k+sha'i yi ge sgom par bya//ku'i rgyan dang rab tu ldan//rigs ni mtha' dag gi dbang gis//kha dog lnga ni 'gro bar byed//
209b2(rgyud): bak+shi mtha' mar sa dang ha//de bzhin dang por 'tshal ba la//bya ba ji 'gron pa'i ni sngags//de ni bye ba khrag gcig zlas//gdengs can rigs dang lnga lnga lhan cig pa//bya'i 'gon po sgrub par bya//bya bdag
209b3(rgyud): grub pa'i gdengs dbang phyug//gdengs can bdag mo bu mo ni//mngag bzhug pa ni nyid du 'gyur//'byung po gnod sbyin gdon rnams dang//rab mchog sa'i steng gi ni//mkha' 'gro ma dang mo mi rnams//sngags
209b4(rgyud): kyi dgug ba ru ni 'gyur//gdon gi tshogs ni ma lus par//dus su zin ces smra bar 'gyur//de phyir sems can don gi phyir//dang po ru ni mi rnams kyis//dang po bya'i dbang po sgrub par bya//rig byed 'dab me rgyad
209b5(rgyud): de bzhin mi bdag lha 'dab ma//rgya mtsho drug dang de phyed la//lte bar sgrub dang rang phyogs su//dang po yi ni a yig bzung//a la swogs pa 'dab rgyad par//e ya la swogs bcu drug la//bcu gsum du ni sgres pa yi//
209b6(rgyud): ka swogs k+sha dang ha gnyis te//phyogs dang 'dab mar sgrub bya'o//tshes la thug dus kyis sgres pa//la'i mthar thug gug skyed bcas//a swogs re re dbyangs la sogs//chu srin dang ni bum pa bzhin//ring
209b7(rgyud): dang thung ba'i rnam ba lnga//phya ru 'dab ma sum bcu gnyis//gzhan yang bdag gi snying po ni//badzra sha sa la swog pa yi yig//phyi ru zhi swogs las rnams ni//gzhan yang ya dang ra dang la//de bzhin de rnams 'khor
209b8(rgyud): los skor//lte bar yi ge yan lag mchog//rtse na can g.yo ba rab rkang ltas//dbang dang dgug pa dag la'o//zhi sogs lte bar rkang stan pa//me dang rlung du g+ho ltas pa//sngags pa yi ni bri bar bya//
210a1(rgyud): //sngon bsungs zhi ba rgyas pa las//srid pa gsum ni 'jig byed dang //skrad pa dang ni dgug dang dbang //rengs pa dang ni rmongs pa dang //sred pa gsum gis gnas su ni//'khor lo 
210a2(rgyud): 'di nyid kyis ni byed//dang po 'dir ni phul byung ba'i//badzra ti ka laswogs sngags bzlas dang //sbyin sreg gis ni gi sgrub par bya//re'u mig brgyad dang bcu gnyis kyi//phyogs dang 'tshaMs
210a3(rgyud): song ba dang //re'u mig bcu drug par bgyi//'tshams su sgrub bya'i ming //dbus su yang ni dbang du dgod//ma ra dza sa de mi ru ni'o//de nas dbus kyis rtsibs 'bram nas//de ni re yang k+she tsa'o
210a4(rgyud): de nas mtha' mar ni dgod do//de nas phyi ru dgod par bya//oM hri Shi Ta pa'i kri na tA na hU~M//hU~M ni rnam par gnyis dang phaT//re'u mig dang ni re'u mig par//sgrub bya'i mig dang sngags 'di ni//
210a5(rgyud): bri bar 'gyur ro mi bdag po//dbye dang gsad dang dbang la ni//ya ma ra tsa k+shed mod dang yo//rengs dgug dang ni rmongs pa dang //gzhan yang stobs ni byed pa dang //zhi ba dang ni
210a6(rgyud): skrad pa'o//lte ba nas ni reng ni yang //yon tan gi ni dbang gis 'gyur//phyi ru sngags bzhin thams cad dang //yi ge rnams kyi lus mchog la//'dun du dgod pa ru ni 'gyur//gang zhig

216b7(rgyud): kyang ni rgyal ba'i//slar yang sde tshan re re ni//rgyal ba mchogi ma lus pa//rnam pa lnga yis dbye bar bya//yi ge so so'i dbye bas te//sa dang chu dang byin za dang //rlung dang nam mkha'i rang bzhin gis
216b8(rgyud): dang por stong pa'i rab dbye dbes//nga nya na ma na yang ngo//de bzhin tha na dang po dbyangs//rlung gi dbye ba yis ni/i//g+ha dz+ha d+ha b+ha d+ha sa'o//me'i dbye bas rig swogs te//chu'i dbye bas yis ni u//
217a1(rgyud): @#//kha tsha tha tha pha pa ma ba//sa'i dbye bas ka liswo gas//de ltar mnan pa lnga dbye bas//rgyal ba dam pa'i rigs kyis ni//kaswo gas ste ni rnams par//gtsug tor stong pa yig ge lnga//

217a6(rgyud): dang bcu dang dgu phyogs rnams//sum bcu rtsa drug bdag nyid dang nya ya laswogs pa sgo skyed rnams//mtha' dag gi ni rigs kyi tshogs//lhag ma gug bskyed rnams de bzhin//khro bo drug ni 
217a7(rgyud): ha swogs 'gyur//rang gi rigs kyi phyogs song ba//rlung gis gug skyed rtsogs kyis phye//yi ge so so dbye ba yi//ro la ro yis sgres ba ni//rnal 'byor ma dang rnal 'byor rgyud//de ltar yi ge re re ni
217a8(rgyud): ro la ro yis sgres pa ni//dkyil 'khor pa dang dkyil 'khor gtso//phung po 'byung ba dbang po rtsogs//mtha' dag gi ni rig kyi tshogs//sngags kyi dbye bas sgrub par bya//stong pa mi shigs laswogs pa

217b2(rgyud): rab dbye//rab ni 'gyur rgyal ba'i bdag//ma mo'i dbye ba'i phyag rnams te//de nyid kyi ni phyag rgyas dbye//gzhan ma yang ni de bzhin du//sku'i rdo rje laswogs pas//rnal 'byor rgyud ni sum bcu drug
217b3(rgyud): dpag tu med pa'i yon tan gis//yi ge du ma rab tu dbye//yi ge so so'i ni mid//'thun dang mi mthun pa'i rig//lha dang lha ma yin rnams kyi'o//sa bon gcig ni dpa'o gcig//rtul dang ro ni mnyam

217b5(rgyud): ku nas bya ba 'di nyid ni//nyi shu rtsa lnga'i bdag nyid do//de ba bzhin so ni skyes pa nyid//slar la yang ni so ni skyes nas//dpag med dbang phyug 'khor lo can//dag par rab dag
217b6(rgyud): chu las ni;ri bong 'dzin pa ltar bkar ba//pad ma'i mtshan ma 'od dpag med//rlom pa'i dbang phyug nyid//rta'i shing rtas 'gro ma dang //ma ma kyi 'i gzugs rdo rje ma//drag mo 'bar ba'i
217b7(rgyud): tshogs dbang phyug//gnyis skyes gdengs can lhan cig dang//stag gis gdong dang 'ugu par bcas//'di rnams dpal gi g.yon zhal nas//dam pa yi ni snying rje yis//rdo rje can gis
217b8(rgyud): spros pa yis//chos la rab dag rlung las ni//don yod grub dang sgrol ma ni//namakha' snying reg bya rdo rje ma//stobs po che dang lhan cig pa//rgan byad ma dang lug rgyu ma//bden bral dang ni rlung lha dang//
218a1(rgyud): @#//rmang rigs klu dang de bzhin du//khyi gdong ma dang bya rog bcas//srid pa gsum gi bdag pa'i//byams pa'i sems kyis de rnams nyid//shar gi zhal nas sbos pa yis//longs spyod du ni 
218a2(rgyud): dag med las//nyi ma 'char ba lta bu'o//rin chen 'dzin dang gos kar mo//sa'i snying po rkugs byed dang//mchog gi ro'i rdo rje ma//phag ma gdong drug me'i lha//shar gi zhal nas gsal ba ru ni khro 
218a3(rgyud): gnyer ma//rgyal rigs gdengs can dbang po dag//phag gi gdong ma bya rgod bcas//'dis rnams dpal gi g.yas zhal nas//mchog gi dga' ba nyid kyis ni//sna tshogs bdag pos smras pa yin/
218a4(rgyud): lte ba rab tu dgas lag ni//gser ni gtso ma lta ma bu ni//thub pa 'khor lo phyag snabs pa//spyan ma dang ni sgrib sel dang//rab mchog ma'i bdag po kye//rengs byed dri'i rdo rje dang//'od zer

218a6(rgyud): pa yis//gtsug gtor dag pa'i stong pa las//rdo rje phyag na snams pa yis//mi skyod dbyings kyi dbang phyug gnyis//gtsug tor dang ni chos kyi dbyings//nam mkha'i yon tan dbang gis
218a7(rgyud): ni//phyag na rdo rje la sogs gzhan//gsang ba ma lus dag pa las//mnyam pa'i bde ba yi ni gnas//sgra'i rdo rje la sogs pa//'og dang steng du spros pa nyid//yon tan mang po yi ni gnas
218a8(rgyud): srog chags rnams kyi thar don du//phyogs kyi 'dab mar sbyin ma sogs//mtshams kyi 'dab mar song ba ni//gang ba yi ni thong pa rnams//nyi ma'i steng na g.yon brkyang ba//'jig pa yi ni sprin
218a9(rgyud): lta bu//u ma dbang phyug gnod pa yi//thod pa yi ni phreng ba bcings//khrag mthung zhal phyag bcu gnyis phyag rgya dang//ni stag lpags dang//mi bzang glang po'i bdag po ni//dkral ba'i pags
218b1(rgyud): rlom 'dzin pa'o//lha mo rnams kyis gnag dang dmar//dri med ri bong can lta bu'i lus//'khor lo gsum gi phyi rol du//rdo rje drug dang khro skyes rnams//phyag gnyis ri bong can gi zhal//de bzhin
218b2(rgyud): 'byung ba las skyes rnams//sgo ru rtu mo'i ro gnas ma//mtshams su yang ni nges par ru//dri gug thod pa phyag na'o//dpa' bo rnams ni rig byed phyag//rdo rje dang ni cang de'u rang//rdo rje kha dam dril ba'i
218b3(rgyud): phag gis lhug po'i phyogs brgyad du//sa dang chu'i sa skor bar//thams cad gdag nas nye ba'i gnas//zhing dang 'dun pa 'dus pa dang//me dang rlung gi dbus su ni//ro ni sregs pa dag gi gnas
218b4(rgyud): de bzhin yul dang grong khyer du//phyogs dang mtshams su song ba yis//lha mo rnams ni rig par bya//rnal 'byor ma'i rgyud zhes bya//srid pa gsum gis bla ma yis//shar gi zhes zhal
218b5(rgyud): nas spros pa yin//snying po'i 'khor lo cha ni brgyad//zla ba nyi ma pa dam la//dkyil 'khor las ni phyed phyed//rdo rje rkang ba 'phreng ba yis//phyes ni smras pa dag tu ni//phung po khas
218b6(rgyud): rnams lhag pa'i ha lha//phyi'i ra ba rtsig pa ru//yul dang yul can dga' ba rnams//zla ba nya ma'i ba dan la gnas//sgo skyong rnams ni nyi mar gnas//gzhan ma yi ni tshogs kyi rigs//kha khyer

218b8(rgyud): nas lag na 'bras thogs pa//dge spogs pa lag bkang ba dang//b+ha ga nyi ma zla ba dang//dus kyi rtsa ba dang bzhin no//shes rab thabs ni ma lus pa//zhal gsum pa dang phyag drug pa//zhal ni g.yas

219b1(rgyud): lte bar nyid la'o//'khor lo sngon po 'phreng ba yang//sa bzhi ljang ku ru ni bya//'khor lo gser las bzhi gnas dang//'khor lo dgar la sa bzhi dmar//sa bzhi yang ni dkar po la//'khor lo phreng
219b2(rgyud): ba dmar po'o//bser dang stong pa'i sa bzhi la//dkar po min par 'gyur ba dang//kha dog ljang gu' 'khor lo'o//cha drug gis ni rdul mtshon sa//bskyed mdzad pa'i zhal dbang gis//sgo'i 'khor lo

220a3(rgyud): 'go bo'i 'phreng ba rgyan du brjod//zla phyed rdo rje gcod pa na can//rang gi rkang pa la 'ong ba'i//gung pags blang po'i pags pa 'i//zla ba nyi ma byi za'i//dkyil 'khor rnams kyi steng gi ni//'dod

220b6(rgyud): dus kun ni rdo rje can//de ru zla ba zhu ba gang//bla ma'i ni kha ru 'jug//de nyid dus kyis 'khor lo ste//bdud rnams kyis ni dus su gsungs//'chi ba'i 'jig gas pa 'joms byed pa//'di na 'gro ma 'khor lo'i
220b7(rgyud): shes rabs thabs kyi yi ge dag//gnyi ga'i rigs kyi dbang gis ni//mkha' 'gro ma dang mkha' 'gro'i mdi//zlas byas rdo rje gsum dang ni//gzhan gi tshig dang lhan cig pas//thog ma dang ni mthar phye ba//
220b8(rgyud): srog gis byin gis brlabs pa gang//gnyi ga'i 'gro ba'i dbang gis ni//rang rang las dang rjes 'thun pas//myur du dngos grub du ni 'gyur//ri'i 'gro ba rnams bral ba//rdo rje mkha' 'gro ma ni 'khroms//
221a1(rgyud): @#//dbu ma'i ni dbus gang dag//cha dang bcas pa'i ro dang 'da'//skye ba'i dus su 'gyur ba ya 'gyur//de'is 'khor lo gzhan brgyad 'gyur//'og dang nam mkha'i dbang gis ni//sgo'i dbus
221a2(rgyud): na lha mo rnams//cha cig las ni skyes pa ni//srid pa gsum gi skyed byed ma//phyag na dri khug thod pa snams//nyi zla'i steng na g.yas brkyang ba//bdud rtsi'i ni bde ba'i chas//dus kyi 'khor lo lo ni 'khyud//
221a3(rgyud): sgo'i dbus lnga dang gzhan gzhi zla nyi ma//yul gi yon tan dbang gis ni//bdan ni bzhan bzhi rgyud pa yis//bza' bdun dang ni nya nyi ma//nyin mo byed dang ri bong can//gzhan ma'i gdan rnams so//nag po dkar po
221a4(rgyud): rdzogs nyi ma//nyin mo phyed dang ri bong can//gzhon ma'i ni gnas rnams so//bza' bdun nyi ma dang ni nyi//'thun dang mi mthun pa gnas su//nyi mi zla ba'i gnas rnams so//nyi ma zla ba rgyu bas rtsibs//
221a5(rgyud): pad ma 'i ni bdan rnams bzhi//sgo dang 'tshams kyi bdan rnams so//slar kha yang ni rtsibs gnyis 'gyur//nyin mo phyed dang ri brong can//lha rnams kyi mi zla ba'i ni bdan rnams so//nya nas nyi ma'i rgyu ba
221a6(rgyud): rnams//'thun dang mi mthun par gnas la//rgyu ba'i ni bdan rnams zhi//nyi ma 'am zla ba rdzogs pa ni//rigs kyi dbang 'gyur ba yin//gtso bo'i yang ni kha sbyor ba//rlung dang ri bya ci nyid de//yon n+ta gzhan 
221a7(rgyud): dbang gis ni//phyag rgya'i zhal ni gnyis pa'o//gzugs dang reg bya'i zhal ni gnyis//rtse mo can gi phyag rgya'i zhal//gsum pa ru ni 'gyur ba yin//reg bya'i gzugs dang ro'i zhal//de ni bzhin yang
221a8(rgyud): zhal ni gsum du 'gyur//phyag rgya'i zhal ni zhi ba'o//dri sogs reg bya'i las skyes mthar//sa'i yon tan gi ni zhal//lnga pa phyag rgya'i ni zhal//stong pa nyid ni rnam pa lnga//phyag rgya'i zhal ni drug pa'o/
221a9(rgyud): de bzhin du ni ye shes khams//yon tan gi ni dbang gis 'gyur//stong pa 'i ni zhal drug pa//gcig gi bzhi dang sres pa 'i//mnyam pa'i bde ba'i 'bras bu'o//lnga dang gnyis gsum sres pa ni//shes rab thabs
221b1(rgyud): kyis zhal ni bcu//yon tan rnam pa gsum gang gis//rnam pa bzhi 'i zhal bcu gnyis//chu tshod dbye bas rnam pa bzhi//zhal ni rnam pa gsum po yang //dus kyi dbye bas kha dog gsum//dus phyed dbye bas
221b2(rgyud): phyag tu 'gyur//'thun dang mi mthun pa'i na//'khyud pa'i lus dang sems la'o//thun tshod zhal ni shing nub tu//zhal gsum dang ni ro'i phyag//u ma'i ni lus 'khyud do//shes rab lus dang snyoms 
221b3(rgyud): 'jug cing //rgod pa la ni swogs pa yang //mtshan mo 'i ni thun mtshams la//shes rab la ni dmar ser la//dus kyi 'khor lo'i dngos po ni//nges pa ru ni 'jug 'gyur cing //rdo rje can lus g.yon

221b8(rgyud): po min pa'i rigs kyis ni//sngon po rnams kyi sna tshogs mdog//slar la ljang khu rnams kyi ni//sngon mo ru ni de bzhin gsungs//de bzhin lha mo rnams kyi ni//rang gi rigs kyi phyogs song ba'i//
221b9(rgyud): lha rnams rig par bya ba ni//zhal nas shes rab rgyud pa 'i//slar la nub kyi zhal nas ni//rnal 'byor rjes su 'jug pa'i rgyud//g.yas zhal nas ni rnal 'byor rgyud//rgyal ba'i ni bdag pos gsungs
222a1(rgyud): @#//g.yon zhal nas ni bya ba'i rgyud//shar nas rnal 'byor spyod pa nyid//yang ni nub kyi zhal nas ni//dbus ma'i chos ni mtha' nyid dang//mdo sde g.yas kyi zhal nas ni gsungs//zhal 
222a2(rgyud): ni kar po nas ni//bye brag smras pa dag ni 'o//des brjod rig byed nub zhal nas//rgyal ba yi ni bdag pos gsungs//mchod sbyin g.yon kyi zhal yin te//dam pa 'i ni me'i rigs//g.yas kyi
222a3(rgyud): zhal nas snyam tshig byed//srid srung shar kyi zhal naso//shar gi shal nas des de nyid//slar yang nub kyi zhal nas ni//mkha' lding dang ni 'byung po'i rgyud//grub pa'i mtha' ni g.yon 
222a4(rgyud): zhal nas//nyi ma shar ba lta bu nas//zhal g.yas nas ni khyab 'jug chos//rgyab nas mdo la skye dang ldog//mchog tu kar ba'i zhal nas ni//g.yon pa'i lha dang rab gnas gang//
222a5(rgyud): g.yas nas kun 'jigs rig pa'o//slar la yang ni rtsa zhal nas//tshangs pa dang ni bzhi ba yi//stong ba'i zhal nas stong pa brtso//srid pa gsum gi bdag po yod//'jig rten pa'i
222a6(rgyud): don du gsungs//rdzogs ldan gnyis ldan gsum ldan dang//gzhan yang brtsod pa yi ni dus//rgyab kyi zhal sogs dbye ba las de bzhin sa bon laswogs b.rim//nyi ma 'gro ba'i dbang kyis ni//
222a7(rgyud): de ltar thun tshod rnam pa bzhi//zas ni g.yas kyi zhal nas te//mchog tu yang ni 'jigs pa ni//rgyab gi zhal nas spros pa yin//shar gi zhal nas mkhrig pa nyid//mchog tu dga' ba'i zhal nas ni//rgyal po'i rme
222a8(rgyud): lam dang ni gnyid//rgyab nas rje rigs bram ze ste//de bzhin rgyal rigs nyi ma gnas//rmang rigs rnams ni rim pa bzhin//shar nas thams cad yod smra ba//rgyal ba yi ni bdag po gsungs//g.yas nas mang pos bkur ba

222b4(rgyud): gten pa'i࿒ chos dang chu ni de bzhin tu//mnyam pa 'i ni nor bu 'gyur//sa bon khams la rten pa yis//sems can rnams kyi sems can dpal//sngon gi las kyi mtha' las ni//rnam pa mang po ru ni 'gyur//

223a6(rgyud): zad par spros pa yin//dga' dang bcas pa'i yul rnams kyi sa//dngos po kun gi rang bzhin no//gcig gi bdag nyid kun nas ni//nam mkha' dang ni mtshungs pa ni//stong pa'i sa ni bstan par bya//de ltar

226b2(rgyud): don du//de yang dam tshig rgyal po ste//rmongs pa rnams ni bde don du//rin chen sdug bsngal can don du//de yang pad ma 'dzin pa ste//chags can rnams kyi chogs don du//rgyegs rnams zhom

226b7(rgyud): nyan thos rnams ni kyi ched du ste//e dang bam la gnas pa gang//mchog gi nges pa can rnams ni//gang ma rung ni gzhan don du//sems can rnams ni smon don du//rnam pa gsum du
226b8(rgyud): gnas pa ni//sku gdus nga thugs su dbye ba ste//phyi dang nang dang gzhan ma ru//nges pa ru ni 'gyur ba ni//rdo rje can ni dus thams cad//phyi'i sna tshogs gasna su ni//'gyur te mchog gi lus su

227a1(rgyud): @#//bar 'gyur dang gzhan ma der dang 'dra ba nyid//e dang bam la gnas pa gang//mchog tu dga' ba'i go 'phang ni//sku dang gsung dang thugs ni 'di//rdo rje gcig gsum du dbye ba
227a2(rgyud): yul dang yul can rnams dang ni//khams rnams kyis ni bskor ba dang//dus dang gnas ni gsum gnas pa//srid pa gsum du yang song ba//sems can du ma'i don phyir du//brgyud du
227a3(rgyud): 'di skad bdag gis gang//thos pa zhes pa'i na tshig//de dag gtogs pa 'di skad do//spyi bo 'grin pa snying ka'i//pad ma lte ba gsang ba ru//zla ba bzhu ba rdo rje ru//rdo rje
227a4(rgyud): bud med ba ga ru//de ni mchog gi pad mas so//thig le gsum ni grol ba yin//sangs rgyas zhing du gzhugsa pa ni//deng 'dir bcom ldan 'das ni de//rnal 'byor pa y+si

227b5(rgyud): dag smras byed pa//ma lus sar song ba//sems can rnams kyis sna tshogs gzugs//rdo rje can kyi thugs rdo rje//dngos po rnams ni 'dzin pa gang//dri med nor bu ni lta bu ni

227b7(rgyud): pa bcu po 'di rnams so//nyon mongs bdud ni zad pa gang//'dod pa zhes pa 'phrog pa ni//rdo rje sogs dang ljon shing sogs//phyag rgya drug ni stong pa drung//nor kyi spyod pa bskyed byed pa//ye shes

227b9(rgyud): med pa//mi shes gcod pa'i dri gug ste//mchog gi rigs ni drug ge'o//gzhan gis 'dzin pa nyid dag kyang//de yang de ni rig par bya//nam mkha' bzhin du ro mnyam pa//phung po khams dang
228a1(rgyud): @#//dbang po bswogs//gang du gzugs kyi rigs nyid do//don ni med par 'gyur ba nyid//de ni gzugs chen po ru gsungs//'khor ba gang gi sdug bsngal ni//'jigs

248a4(rgyud): pa laswogs bdag//nang mi sangs rgyas nyid du 'gyur//dbang bdun po 'di yis ti//dkyil 'khor gzhengs nas sbyin par bya//bum pa dang gsang ba dang//shes rab ye shes mngon brjod pa//slar yang shes 
248a5(rgyud): rab chen po ni//de'i ye shes mngon par brjod//'gyur dang gyur zhing de ni mthun//de nas gzhan ni rgyu 'thun pa//mngon par dbang skur rim gsum gis//lus ngag yid gsum dag byed pa//zhi pa yang ni skye rgu'i
248a6(rgyud): bdag//shes rab yang ni nus ma yin//rig pa'i byang chub sems dpa' 'pho//gang phyin byis pas bde thob pa//bkar 'dzin kyi de ba dang bskur//gsang mnyes las yun rings bskyes//byang chub sems 
248a7(rgyud): 'pho bde ba ste//gang phyir gsang bas bde thob po//dang ma bde bsang dbang bskur ro//yun rings gsang ba mnyes la bskur//rdo rje rtse mor mthun las skyes//gang phyir rgan pa mthun gyur pa//shes rab ye shes de
248a8(rgyud): dbang bskur//phyag rgya che la rjes chags pa//rgyu mthun lus bskyes bde ba gang//gang phyir rgyu 'thun nyid du gyur//phyag rgya chen pos de dbang bskur//skyob pa thams cad bskyed mdzad pa//skye dgu'i bdag por de shes bya
248b1(rgyud): rdo rje sems dpa' sems dpa' chen//mi 'gyur gnyis med byang chub sems//dam tshig sems dpa' 'di yin te//rdo rje rnal 'byor rnam bzhi pa//dus kyi 'khor lo 'di brjod te//rnal 'byor rnams la bde bster ba'o
248b2(rgyud): 'di nyid sgrub bar bya ba ni//ma byams pa'i snang ba dang//du ba laswogs mtshan ma 'o//shes rab gzugs ni mkha' mnyam dang//yod dang med la rnam 'das pa'i//sems kyi skyon ni don rnams dang//brdul phran las skyes
248b3(rgyud): tshogs pa rnams//kun tu yongs su spangs pa'o//du ba mig skyes me khyer dang//mar me 'bar dang nyi zla dang//mun mtshan thig les che//sna tshogs gzugs ni 'od gsal ba'o//mig gnyis ma che ma btsums pa//
248b4(rgyud): gang zhig stong bar ma rtags pas//mi lam bzhan du gzugs mthong 'gyur//gzugs de rtag tu sgom par bya//med pa'i gzugs la sgom par ni//rnal 'byor rnams kyis de sgoms min//stong par ma rtags par
248b5(rgyud): mthong ba'i phyir//sems la dngos dang dngos min med//ji ltar ma long skra las las ni//dngos med las bu mos mthong//mkhas la de nyid rnal 'byol pas//'das dang ma 'ongs chas de bzhin//de med pa yis dngos
248b6(rgyud): yod min//dngos med pa'i don yod phyir//dngos po med pa'i don yod pa//rmi lam sgyu ma mig 'phrul 'tshungs//chos can med pa las ni mtshungs//skye ba 'di nyid mthong ba la//yid bzhin nor ltar mtha' yas pa'i
248b7(rgyud): re ba thams cad rdzogs par mdzod//gzhon nu ma'i pral las ni//'on te med pa'i gzugs mthong nas//ri pod ra ni ji ltar min//mig gzhan gis ni mi mthong bzhin//bdag gis mig gis kyang mi mthong//ma skyes pa ni mthong
248b8(rgyud): ba min//bzhon nu ma'i bu bzhin no//gzugs mthong de'i 'og tu ni//steng dang 'og dang rtsa gsum las//las dag sems ni dga' ba'i phyir//rgyun mi mchad par srog brtsol bya//nyi zla sgra gcan dri can dang/dri chus
249a1(rgyud): @#//bon 'bab pa'i lam//chu me stong pa sa bcu rlung dang//ye shes khams kyis rigs pa yin//gang rnams nyi zla mun can ni//las dag sems kyi rtsa rnams te//srog dang thur sel rim pa yis//dri chen
249a2(rgyud): dri chu sa bon 'bab//thabs kyi sku ni zla ba ste//shes pa rab gsung ni nyid yin ni//shes rab sku ni dri chen rtsa//dri chu 'bab pa'i rtse mo'i gsung//steng dang 'og gi cha gnyis ni//sa bon 'bab dang mun can nam/
249a3(rgyud): thabs kyis thugs ni mun pa can//sa bon 'bab ma shes rab kyi sku gsung thugs kyi rnal 'byor kyis//steng 'og rigs drug gang yin no//thabs dang shes rab 'og gi cha gnyis so//sa bon 'bab dang mu na
249a4(rgyud): can nam//thabs kyis thugs ni mun nga can//sa bon shes rab sku dngos kyis//lus can kun la yang dag gnas//lus dag rtsa las mchi ltas min//mun can sa bon 'bab ma las//rkang pa dang ni
249a5(rgyud): bcwo lnga bcu drug pa//dbang phyug dbyugs so mi dbang kyi//bdun bcu rtsa lngas lhag pa'i//drug brgyad nyid dang mtshan mo'o//gang yas drug rgyas lhag pa yi//stong phrag nyi shu rtsa gcig ste//g.yas dang g.yon
249a6(rgyud): pa'i rtsa las ni//'di yis man pa da rnams 'bab//phyogs bcu dang bcas lo rnams ni//lo brgya yi ni dbu mar 'gyur//steng du g.yas dang g.yon par ni//nyi zla sug dang ser skye gzhan//chu dang me'i rang bzhin te
249a7(rgyud): pad ma rin chen 'dzin par brjod//'og tu dri chen dri chu 'bab//sa dang rlung gi rang bzhin te//dbus ma dang ni g.yon pa'i rtsa//'khor lo rab bri 'dzin par 'dod//stod du dbus ma 'og ma'i g.yas//sgra gcan dang ni
249a8(rgyud): sa bon 'bab//stong pa ye shes rang bzhin te//der 'dzin rdo rje 'dzin pa'i gsung//zla ba'i lam las dri chen rtsa//nyid las dri chu 'bab brtso//skye sems mnyam dang 'dod chags la//sgra gcan sa bon 'bab
249b1(rgyud): ma'o//gang zhig lte steng kun 'das ma//mun 'bab shin du yid bzangs te//gang 'og nam mkha' gdeng de ni//sa bon 'bab pa dus can ma//lte ba'i pad ma snying mgrin dang //phral bar gtsug tor 'dab 
249b2(rgyud): skyes la//sa chu me rlung stong ba ni//sdud pa yid ni 'bab pa'o//gshegs pa dang ni byon pa ni//skyed pa yis ni sa la 'jug//lte ba las ni lte ba'i dbus//kun dang ma ni gshegs dang byin//lte gsang nor bu'i pad
249b3(rgyud): ma las//lus ngag sems ni 'bab ma ste//gshegs pa dang ni byonapa de//sdud dang skyed pa gzugs can ma//skyes pa'i kun la dung can ma//thur se la rlung ni 'bab par dbye//'di ni bud med med pa yis//

249b6(rgyud): dbus su drug pa 'bab pa'o//lte ba'i pad ma 'das pa la//dkyil 'khor drug bcu'i rim pa yis//dus sbyor g.yas dang g.yon pa la//lug sogs khyung mchog la sogs swo//rtsa ba g.yas dang de nas
249b7(rgyud): g.yon//stong dang dbus dang rim pas rlung //dkyil 'khor re re dbyug gu ni//sa sogs snang phug gnyis pa'o//drug bcur bcas pa'i sum sum rgyas dbyugs//rtsa ni rim pa'i 'bab pa'o//stong dag rgya'i
249b8(rgyud): dbugs rnams ni//rtsa lnga po yis de rnams 'bab//rtsa ci drug bcu gnyis mtshan du//lus kyi dkyil 'khor zhes bya'o//'dab ma'i dbus dang de nas shar//g.yas dang g.yon tu rim par rlung //
250a1(rgyud): //g.yon tu rtag par mkha' rtsogs te//g.yas la swogs pa gzhan ma'o//g.yon du srid pa rnam shes rtsogs//g.yas su sa swogs sdud pa'o//nub dang shar dang dus dang rlung//phan tshun thabs dang shes
250a2(rgyud): ste//'the bong dang ni min med kyi//gang gi kha la dbang skur ro//g.yas dang g.yon tu me dang chu//phan tshun thabs dang shas rab ste//'dzub mo dang ni gong mo gnyis//gang phyir ral dri 'gyur las so//phyag rgya zla
250a3(rgyud): ba phyed pa'i tshul//mche dri chu dri gri gug ste//stong pa mi 'gyur steng 'og tu//phan tshun thabs dang shis rab po//sor mo rnams ni phan tshun du//dkyil 'khor bcu ni yang dag 'byor//spyi bor thal mo sbyar ba yis//
250a4(rgyud): gri gug dang bcas rtse gcig po//skye ba dang ni sdud byed pa//mnyam pa de yang dbus ma'i rtsar//phung po khams dang rdo rje gsum//dbus mar song na gcig tu 'gyur//zla ba nyi ma'i rtsa 'gas nas//dri chen dri chu'i
250a5(rgyud): rtsa 'gag go//sgra gcan rtsa ni 'gags pa na//steng 'og sa bon mi 'bab po//krol na 'di thams cad grol bar 'gyur//skyed pa 'am ni sdud byed pa//srog dang thur sel lam gsum gis//rtsa rnams kun spyod
250a6(rgyud): 'di yin no//g.yon pa 'am g.yon pa la//nyin dang mtshan bzhi deng bzhin lnga//rlung ni lhag par 'bab pa na//de nas lo gsum gis 'chi 'gyur//lnga phyogs tshes dang nyi shu dang//de nyid rim pa gcig gis lhag
250a7(rgyud): nyi ma sum bcu sum gnas pa//g.yas pa'i nyi mar mi 'dod pa'o//gsum gnyis gcig mnyam zla ba ni//gru gsum zung la nyi ma ni//phyogs lnga dang ni yon tan lnga//gcig gis lus can tshe 'gror 'gyur//rtsa 
250a8(rgyud): gcig gis ni lhags//g.yon nas zla ba dbyung bar 'gyur//lo brgya yongs su rdzogs pa yis//dbus la ni 'chi bar 'byung//mnyam par dang mi mnyam nyin mtshan gis//de'i dkyil 'khor skye bar 'gyur
250b1(rgyud): sa phyogs gnyis rnam par nyams pa'i phyir//g.yas dang g.yon pa'i kun sbyor ni//dkyil 'khor lnga ni 'bab par 'gyur//gzhan du yi ni 'chi ba min//mchi ltas mtshan nyid shes bya la//srog ni thig ler zhug
250b2(rgyud): par bya//kun spangs go 'phang rtan bca' la//mchog tu mi 'gyur sgom par bya//nyi zla 'gro ba zhom pa'i phyir//rtag par rdo rje slang bar bya//bzhin du na ni kun spangs rtsar//srog gi rlung ni 'jug par mda'࿒
250b3(rgyud): 'og tu dud can thur sel ni//mchog tu mi de ma bzhugs na mchi bar yin//mchi ba slu ba de nyid ni//rnal 'byor rnams la dga' bas 'gyu//gtsug tor steng gi tsha pad ma las//shu kra byung na dga' ba yin//
250b4(rgyud): 'grin pa snying khar mchog dga' ba//de nas dga' bral dga' bral 'gyur//sna tshogs dga' ba lte ba las//gal te lte bar pad mar song//gsang gnas rdo rje nor bu'i bar//lhan cig skyes dga' mi
250b5(rgyud): 'pho ba'o//rab tu mi ma gnas mya ngan 'das//chags chen mi 'gyur minga' bdag po//dga' ldan las ni bde 'pho gang//mya ngan 'das la bde ba gnas//sa bon zla ba gtsug tor shar//gsang ba'i chu 
250b6(rgyud): skyes dga' la rdzogs//cha ni bcu drug ma gang de//rdo rje nur ba pad rtsor song//de 'og 'pho ba dus yin te//nag po'i phyogs gcig 'ongs nas song//stong la dga' ba bral ba yis//nyi ma 'dzod pur
250b7(rgyud): 'gro bar 'gyur//de'i cha na chu drug ma//gtsug tor pad mar song ba na//'di ni zla ba zad par brjod//'dod chags bde ba bral phyir ro//lus can thams cad 'chi ba na//zla ba bdud rtsi thur du 'gro//steng du
250b8(rgyud): nye rdul sgra gcan dang//rnam shes srid pa'i mtshan nyid do//de bas zla bas bdud rtsi ste//'gro bar bya'o rgyal po kyi//'og tu nye bdul sgra gcan dang//rnam she mi 'byung bde ba'o//mdzod spu chu skyes sangs 
251a1(rgyud): @#//rgyas kun o zla ba bdud rtsi nyid yin no//nyi rdul gnam stong bsang ba la//gtsug tor nor bu de gnyis cha//ston pa rdo rje'i tshig 'di ni//lus can sa la phyin cing log//mi gnas mya ngan 'das pa 
251a2(rgyud): yis//khams rnams phyin ci log tu 'gyur//nor bu thugs dang bcas par gsung//lte bar sku ni bde chen phyir//dag pa'i sku las spra ba ni//chos dang long spyod spral pa 'di//'og tu thabs gyur rgyal 
251a3(rgyud): po kye//steng du shes rab dag tu 'gyur//dpral bar dkar ba'i rdo rje yis//'grin snying lte ba'i dam skyes las//gsung thugs ye shes rdo rje ni//sku laswogs 'di rnams spral bar 'dzad//phung po khams spral bar 'dzad//
251a4(rgyud): sgrib pa med pa yis ni bde//las kyi phyag rgya snyom 'jug dang//ye shes phyag rgya swogs dmigs pa//phyag rgya chen pos cig sbyor bas//'phel bar 'gyur ba ma 'gyur ba'o//'phel ba la ni 'phel ba med//grol 
251a5(rgyud): ba la ni grol ba med/zad pa la ni zad pa med//nub pa la ni nub pa med//shar ba la ni shar ba med//gsal ba la ni gsal ba med//btsums pa la ni btsums pa med//skyes pa la ni skyes pa med//mi gnas pa 
251a6(rgyud): la gnas pa med//dngos po la ni dngos med min//'gyur ba la ni 'gyur ba med//mi 'gyur la ni mi 'gyur med//chos rnams rang bzhin med pa ni//rgyu 'byung dang ni zang bzhin no//'byung bar bzad par 'gyur ro//
251a7(rgyud): rang bzhin gis skye ba med//dngos dang dngos med mtshan nyid mtshan nyid gcig/sna tshogs 'di ni mtshan nyid bral//rab kyi shes rab las 'byung sems//pad ma rdo rje nor bur song//de la zla ba mthun 'gyur ba//mi 'gyur
251a8(rgyud): sgom pa (rgyu)mthun pa'o//lus ngag sems kyis rtsa rnams la//lus ngag sems ni sgom par bya//rdo rje gsum ni sdug pa 'i//dung can ma ni ye shes sgom//mgo bo las ni rim pa 'i//ye shes rab chags thig le zhu//rtsogs 
251b1(rgyud): pa'i go 'phang bzhugs rnams la//don dam ba ru 'ching bar bya//rim par 'di ltar zla ba shar//cha rnams kyis ni rdzogs par 'gyur//nyin mo bzung ba rdzogs pa ste//zla ba tsha gsu tshang ma rdzogs pa bzhin
251b2(rgyud): de bzhin rig pas ye shes 'char//sa rnams kyis ni rdzogs par gyur//nyon mongs laswogs bcom rdzogs pa//ye shes chad pa rdzogs pa min//dbus su ri bong gis mtshan pa//nya la ri bong can mi 
251b3(rgyud): gnas//'khor ba'i bag chags kyis mtshan pa//mi 'gyur bde ba de bzhin sems//skye ba dang ni 'jigs pa'i phyogs//yar ngo mar ngo rab tu gnas//de'i dbus su rdzogs pa gang //rdzogs pa de ni
251b4(rgyud): rab mi gnas//skad cig gis ni rdzogs sangs rgyas//rdzogs pa 'i ni g.yo mi 'gyur//gang zhig rdo rje nor bu sems//skad cig thams cad de dag byed//dkar po'i phyogs la 'di mi gnas//dkar 
251b5(rgyud): dmar pa la ye mi gro//gnyis med rdzogs pa la gnas mig//phyogs gnyis pa'i dbus su gnas//gtsug tor las ni byung ba ste//rdo rje nor bur rdzogs par 'gyur//dga' bral bcas pas cha bcom
251b6(rgyud): par rdo rje nas ni rdzogs par bcom//slar yang gtsug tor las byung ba//slar yang rdo rje nor bu rtsa rdzogs//dga' blar bcas pas cha bcom pa//lus can ye shes bcom pa min//bde ba'i chen po'i rIn+na

251b8(rgyud): dang //dgrod pa gnyis la nya bzhin no//rdzogs la mi g.yo ba 'dir ni//mi 'gyur skad cig gis ni zos pa'o//dgu stong nyi khri drug rgya yis//sa rnams kyis ni yongs su rdzogs//phyogs gnyis yang dag nin
251b9(rgyud): bas ni//sa phyogs kyis ni yongs su rdzogs //bde don rnam pa bcu gnyis bstan//rnam pa bcu drug mi 'gyur pa'o//rnam pa bcu gnyis sa rnams kyis//rdzogs nas gnyis su med pa'i mchog//cha rnams kyi ni
252a1(rgyud): //rdzogs pa'i sems//rnam pa bcu drug mi 'gyur ba//don gcig gnyis su med pa'i sems//don dam par ni mi nyams pa//rdzogs pa'i gnas skabs sems gyur nas//kun
252a2(rgyud): nas rab tu rdzogs pa min//chags bral dang po chags pa che//rdo rje yan lag mi 'gyur che//phyogs gnyis pa'i bag chags kyis//yongs su gang ba ma 'dres pa//ji ltar rgya mtsho'i chu bo chu

252a9(rgyud): ma med//de bzhin phug pa'i sems la ni//gang du'ang bag chags yod ma min//gser nyid du ni lcags gyur na//me yis dri ma med par 'gyur//de bzhin sems ni yang du yang //
